
Can you explain all the topics of Microservices production checklist with hands on and explanation step wise step?

1. Service Design
Single Responsibility per Service
Concept: Each service should have one clear purpose and reason to change.

Hands-on Example:

csharp
// BAD: Combined service
[ApiController]
public class UserOrderController : ControllerBase
{
    // Handles both user management and orders
}

// GOOD: Separate services
// UserService
[ApiController]
[Route("api/users")]
public class UserController : ControllerBase
{
    private readonly IUserService _userService;
}

// OrderService
[ApiController]
[Route("api/orders")]
public class OrderController : ControllerBase
{
    private readonly IOrderService _orderService;
}
API-First Contract Design (OpenAPI 3.0)
Step-by-step:

Define your API contract before coding

Use OpenAPI specification

yaml
# openapi.yaml
openapi: 3.0.0
info:
  title: Order Service API
  version: 1.0.0
paths:
  /api/v1/orders:
    post:
      summary: Create order
      requestBody:
        required: true
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              $ref: '#/components/schemas/CreateOrderRequest'
      responses:
        '201':
          description: Order created
          headers:
            Idempotency-Key:
              schema:
                type: string
components:
  schemas:
    CreateOrderRequest:
      type: object
      required:
        - customerId
        - items
      properties:
        customerId:
          type: string
          format: uuid
        items:
          type: array
Idempotent Operations
Implementation:

csharp
[HttpPost]
public async Task<IActionResult> CreateOrder([FromBody] CreateOrderRequest request)
{
    // Check for idempotency key in header
    var idempotencyKey = Request.Headers["Idempotency-Key"].ToString();
    
    if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(idempotencyKey))
        return BadRequest("Idempotency-Key header required");
    
    // Check if already processed
    var existingOrder = await _idempotencyService.GetOrder(idempotencyKey);
    if (existingOrder != null)
        return Ok(existingOrder); // Return existing result
    
    // Process order
    var order = await _orderService.CreateOrder(request);
    
    // Store result with idempotency key
    await _idempotencyService.StoreResult(idempotencyKey, order);
    
    return CreatedAtAction(nameof(GetOrder), new { id = order.Id }, order);
}
2. Resilience & Fault Tolerance
Circuit Breaker Pattern (Polly)
Step-by-step implementation:

csharp
// 1. Install Polly
// dotnet add package Polly

// 2. Configure circuit breaker
public static class ResiliencePolicies
{
    public static IAsyncPolicy<HttpResponseMessage> GetCircuitBreakerPolicy()
    {
        return Policy<HttpResponseMessage>
            .Handle<HttpRequestException>()
            .OrResult(r => !r.IsSuccessStatusCode)
            .CircuitBreakerAsync(
                exceptionsAllowedBeforeBreaking: 3,
                durationOfBreak: TimeSpan.FromSeconds(30),
                onBreak: (exception, duration) =>
                {
                    // Log circuit opened
                    Console.WriteLine($"Circuit broken for {duration}");
                },
                onReset: () =>
                {
                    // Log circuit reset
                    Console.WriteLine("Circuit reset");
                }
            );
    }
}

// 3. Use in HTTP client
services.AddHttpClient<PaymentServiceClient>()
    .AddPolicyHandler(ResiliencePolicies.GetCircuitBreakerPolicy())
    .AddPolicyHandler(GetRetryPolicy());
Retry with Exponential Backoff
csharp
private static IAsyncPolicy<HttpResponseMessage> GetRetryPolicy()
{
    return Policy<HttpResponseMessage>
        .Handle<HttpRequestException>()
        .OrResult(r => r.StatusCode == HttpStatusCode.TooManyRequests)
        .WaitAndRetryAsync(
            retryCount: 3,
            sleepDurationProvider: retryAttempt => 
                TimeSpan.FromSeconds(Math.Pow(2, retryAttempt)),
            onRetry: (outcome, timespan, retryCount, context) =>
            {
                // Log retry attempt
                Console.WriteLine($"Retry {retryCount} after {timespan}");
            });
}
Bulkhead Isolation
csharp
// Configure bulkhead for different consumer types
public class OrderProcessingService
{
    // Dedicated thread pool for critical orders
    private static readonly BulkheadPolicy _criticalBulkhead = 
        Policy.BulkheadAsync(
            maxParallelization: 10,  // Max 10 concurrent
            maxQueuingActions: 20);   // Queue up to 20
    
    // Separate pool for bulk orders
    private static readonly BulkheadPolicy _bulkBulkhead = 
        Policy.BulkheadAsync(
            maxParallelization: 50,
            maxQueuingActions: 100);
    
    public async Task ProcessCriticalOrder(Order order)
    {
        await _criticalBulkhead.ExecuteAsync(async () =>
        {
            await _orderProcessor.ProcessAsync(order);
        });
    }
}
3. Data Management
Database per Service
Implementation pattern:

csharp
// Each service has its own database context
// OrderService
public class OrderDbContext : DbContext
{
    public DbSet<Order> Orders { get; set; }
    public DbSet<OrderItem> OrderItems { get; set; }
    
    protected override void OnConfiguring(DbContextOptionsBuilder options)
        => options.UseSqlServer("Server=orders-db;Database=OrdersDb;");
}

// InventoryService
public class InventoryDbContext : DbContext
{
    public DbSet<Product> Products { get; set; }
    public DbSet<InventoryTransaction> Transactions { get; set; }
    
    protected override void OnConfiguring(DbContextOptionsBuilder options)
        => options.UseSqlServer("Server=inventory-db;Database=InventoryDb;");
}
Outbox Pattern for Reliability
csharp
// 1. Outbox table
public class OutboxMessage
{
    public Guid Id { get; set; }
    public string Type { get; set; }
    public string Content { get; set; }
    public DateTime OccurredOn { get; set; }
    public DateTime? ProcessedOn { get; set; }
}

// 2. Save in same transaction
public async Task CreateOrder(CreateOrderRequest request)
{
    using var transaction = await _context.Database.BeginTransactionAsync();
    
    try
    {
        // Save order
        var order = new Order { /* ... */ };
        _context.Orders.Add(order);
        
        // Save outbox message
        var outboxMessage = new OutboxMessage
        {
            Id = Guid.NewGuid(),
            Type = "OrderCreated",
            Content = JsonSerializer.Serialize(order),
            OccurredOn = DateTime.UtcNow
        };
        _context.OutboxMessages.Add(outboxMessage);
        
        await _context.SaveChangesAsync();
        await transaction.CommitAsync();
    }
    catch
    {
        await transaction.RollbackAsync();
        throw;
    }
}

// 3. Background processor
public class OutboxProcessor : BackgroundService
{
    protected override async Task ExecuteAsync(CancellationToken stoppingToken)
    {
        while (!stoppingToken.IsCancellationRequested)
        {
            await ProcessOutboxMessages();
            await Task.Delay(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(5), stoppingToken);
        }
    }
    
    private async Task ProcessOutboxMessages()
    {
        var messages = await _context.OutboxMessages
            .Where(m => !m.ProcessedOn.HasValue)
            .Take(100)
            .ToListAsync();
        
        foreach (var message in messages)
        {
            try
            {
                // Publish to message broker
                await _messageBus.PublishAsync(message.Type, message.Content);
                
                message.ProcessedOn = DateTime.UtcNow;
                await _context.SaveChangesAsync();
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                // Log error and continue
                _logger.LogError(ex, "Failed to process outbox message {MessageId}", message.Id);
            }
        }
    }
}
4. Security
Zero Trust & mTLS
Kubernetes implementation with Istio:

yaml
# Enable mTLS in namespace
apiVersion: security.istio.io/v1beta1
kind: PeerAuthentication
metadata:
  name: default
  namespace: microservices
spec:
  mtls:
    mode: STRICT
---
# Authorization policy
apiVersion: security.istio.io/v1beta1
kind: AuthorizationPolicy
metadata:
  name: order-service-policy
spec:
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app: order-service
  rules:
  - from:
    - source:
        principals: ["cluster.local/ns/microservices/sa/payment-service"]
    to:
    - operation:
        paths: ["/api/orders/*"]
        methods: ["POST"]
JWT Validation
csharp
// Configure JWT authentication
services.AddAuthentication(JwtBearerDefaults.AuthenticationScheme)
    .AddJwtBearer(options =>
    {
        options.Authority = "https://identity-service";
        options.Audience = "order-service";
        options.TokenValidationParameters = new TokenValidationParameters
        {
            ValidateIssuer = true,
            ValidateAudience = true,
            ValidateLifetime = true,
            ValidateIssuerSigningKey = true,
            ClockSkew = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(30)
        };
        
        // Enable HTTPS metadata address
        options.RequireHttpsMetadata = true;
    });

{
    // Check tenant isolation
    var tenantId = User.FindFirst("tenant_id")?.Value;
    if (tenantId != request.TenantId)
        return Forbid();
    
    // Continue processing...
}
5. Testing
Contract Tests with Pact
csharp
// Consumer test (OrderService calling InventoryService)
public class InventoryServiceConsumerTests
{
    [Fact]
    public async Task CheckInventory_ShouldReturnAvailable()
    {
        // Arrange
        var mockProvider = new MockProviderService(8080);
        mockProvider.Given("Product in stock")
            .UponReceiving("A request to check inventory")
            .With(new ProviderServiceRequest
            {
                Method = HttpMethod.Get,
                Path = "/api/inventory/123",
                Headers = new Dictionary<string, string>
                {
                    { "Accept", "application/json" }
                }
            })
            .WillRespondWith(new ProviderServiceResponse
            {
                Status = 200,
                Headers = new Dictionary<string, string>
                {
                    { "Content-Type", "application/json" }
                },
                Body = new { productId = "123", quantity = 10 }
            });
        
        // Act
        var client = new HttpClient { BaseAddress = new Uri("http://localhost:8080") };
        var response = await client.GetAsync("/api/inventory/123");
        
        // Assert
        response.EnsureSuccessStatusCode();
        
        // Write pact
        mockProvider.WritePactToFile("inventory-service-consumer.json", 
            "InventoryService", 
            "OrderService");
    }
}

// Provider test (InventoryService)
[TestClass]
public class InventoryServiceProviderTests
{
    [TestMethod]
    public void EnsureProviderHonoursPactWithConsumer()
    {
        // Arrange
        var config = new PactVerifierConfig
        {
            Outputters = new List<IOutput>
            {
                new ConsoleOutput()
            },
            Verbose = true
        };
        
        // Act/Assert
        new PactVerifier(config)
            .ProviderState($"{TestContext.DeploymentDirectory}/provider-states")
            .ServiceProvider("InventoryService", new Uri("http://localhost:5000"))
            .HonoursPactWith("OrderService")
            .PactUri("../../../pacts/inventory-service-consumer.json")
            .Verify();
    }
}
Chaos Engineering Tests
csharp
// Using Chaos Toolkit or custom implementation
[Fact]
public async Task OrderProcessing_WithServiceFailure_ShouldDegradeGracefully()
{
    // Arrange
    _chaosSimulator.EnableFaultInjection();
    _chaosSimulator.SetFault(new HttpFailureFault
    {
        Service = "payment-service",
        FailureRate = 0.8, // 80% failure rate
        ResponseTime = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(30)
    });
    
    // Act
    var task = _orderService.CreateOrderAsync(testOrder);
    
    // Assert
    var ex = await Assert.ThrowsAsync<CircuitBreakerException>(() => task);
    
    // Verify fallback behavior
    var orderStatus = await _orderService.GetOrderStatusAsync(testOrder.Id);
    Assert.Equal(OrderStatus.Pending, orderStatus);
    Assert.True(_notificationService.SentAlerts);
}

          requests:
            memory: "256Mi"
            cpu: "250m"

            memory: "512Mi"
            cpu: "500m"

            path: /health/live
            port: 8080

          periodSeconds: 10

            path: /health/ready
            port: 8080
          initialDelaySeconds: 5

7. Observability
Structured Logging with Correlation IDs
csharp
// 1. Add correlation ID middleware
public class CorrelationIdMiddleware
{
    private readonly RequestDelegate _next;
    
    public async Task InvokeAsync(HttpContext context)
    {
        var correlationId = context.Request.Headers["X-Correlation-ID"].FirstOrDefault()
            ?? Guid.NewGuid().ToString();
        
        context.Items["CorrelationId"] = correlationId;
        context.Response.Headers["X-Correlation-ID"] = correlationId;
        
        using (LogContext.PushProperty("CorrelationId", correlationId))
        {
            await _next(context);
        }
    }
}

// 2. Configure Serilog
Log.Logger = new LoggerConfiguration()
    .Enrich.WithProperty("Application", "OrderService")
    .Enrich.WithProperty("Environment", Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("ASPNETCORE_ENVIRONMENT"))
    .Enrich.WithMachineName()
    .WriteTo.Console(new JsonFormatter())
    .WriteTo.OpenTelemetry()
    .CreateLogger();

// 3. Use structured logging
_logger.LogInformation(
    "Order created {@Order} by {@User}",
    new { order.Id, order.TotalAmount },
    new { UserId = userId, TenantId = tenantId });
OpenTelemetry Tracing
csharp
// Program.cs
services.AddOpenTelemetry()
    .WithTracing(tracerProviderBuilder =>
    {
        tracerProviderBuilder
            .AddSource("OrderService")
            .SetResourceBuilder(ResourceBuilder.CreateDefault()
                .AddService("OrderService"))
            .AddHttpClientInstrumentation()
            .AddAspNetCoreInstrumentation(options =>
            {
                options.RecordException = true;
                options.EnrichWithHttpRequest = (activity, request) =>
                {
                    activity.SetTag("tenant.id", request.Headers["X-Tenant-ID"]);
                };
            })
            .AddSqlClientInstrumentation()
            .AddOtlpExporter(options =>
            {
                options.Endpoint = new Uri("http://jaeger:4317");
            });
    });

// Custom spans
public async Task<Order> ProcessOrderAsync(Order order)
{
    using var activity = _activitySource.StartActivity("ProcessOrder");
    
    try
    {
        activity?.SetTag("order.id", order.Id);
        activity?.SetTag("order.total", order.TotalAmount);
        
        // Call inventory service
        using var inventorySpan = _activitySource.StartActivity("CheckInventory");
        await _inventoryClient.CheckStockAsync(order.Items);
        inventorySpan?.Stop();
        
        // Process payment
        await _paymentClient.ProcessPaymentAsync(order);
        
        activity?.SetStatus(ActivityStatusCode.Ok);
        return order;
    }
    catch (Exception ex)
    {
        activity?.SetStatus(ActivityStatusCode.Error, ex.Message);
        activity?.RecordException(ex);
        throw;
    }
}
Health Checks
csharp
// Configure health checks
services.AddHealthChecks()
    .AddSqlServer(connectionString, name: "database")
    .AddUrlGroup(new Uri("https://identity-service/health"), name: "identity-service")
    .AddRedis(redisConnectionString, name: "cache")
    .AddCheck<DependencyHealthCheck>("external-apis");

// Custom health check
public class DependencyHealthCheck : IHealthCheck
{
    public async Task<HealthCheckResult> CheckHealthAsync(
        HealthCheckContext context,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        var isCircuitClosed = _circuitBreaker.State == CircuitState.Closed;
        
        if (!isCircuitClosed)
        {
            return HealthCheckResult.Degraded("Circuit breaker is open");
        }
        
        return HealthCheckResult.Healthy();
    }
}

// Endpoint mapping
app.MapHealthChecks("/health/ready", new HealthCheckOptions
{
    Predicate = _ => true,
    ResponseWriter = UIResponseWriter.WriteHealthCheckUIResponse
});

app.MapHealthChecks("/health/live", new HealthCheckOptions
{
    Predicate = _ => false // Only basic checks
});
8. Messaging & Events
Dead Letter Queue Handling
csharp
public class OrderCreatedConsumer : IConsumer<OrderCreatedEvent>
{
    public async Task Consume(ConsumeContext<OrderCreatedEvent> context)
    {
        try
        {
            // Process message
            await ProcessOrder(context.Message);
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            // Move to DLQ after retries
            await context.ForwardToDeadLetterQueue(context.Message);
            
            _logger.LogError(ex, 
                "Failed to process order {OrderId}, moved to DLQ", 
                context.Message.OrderId);
            
            throw; // Reject message
        }
    }
}

// Retry policy with MassTransit
services.AddMassTransit(x =>
{
    x.AddConsumer<OrderCreatedConsumer>()
        .Endpoint(e => e.Name = "order-created")
        .Message<OrderCreatedEvent>(m => m.UseMessageRetry(r => 
            r.Immediate(3).Exponential(5, TimeSpan.FromSeconds(1), TimeSpan.FromMinutes(5))));
    
    x.UsingRabbitMq((context, cfg) =>
    {
        cfg.ReceiveEndpoint("order-created", e =>
        {
            e.ConfigureConsumer<OrderCreatedConsumer>(context);
            
            // Configure DLQ
            e.DeadLetterExchange = "order-created.dlx";
            e.DeadLetterQueue = "order-created.dlq";
        });
    });
});
Message Replay Capability
csharp
public class MessageReplayService
{
    public async Task ReplayMessages(DateTime fromDate, DateTime toDate, string eventType)
    {
        // Fetch messages from archive
        var messages = await _eventStore.GetEventsAsync(fromDate, toDate, eventType);
        
        foreach (var message in messages)
        {
            // Publish to replay topic
            await _messageBus.PublishAsync(message, new PublishOptions
            {
                Headers = new Dictionary<string, object>
                {
                    { "is-replay", true },
                    { "original-timestamp", message.Timestamp }
                }
            });
        }
    }
}

        "Transforms": [
          { "PathPattern": "/api/orders/{**catch-all}" }
        ]

      "product-route": {
        "ClusterId": "product-cluster",

          "Path": "/products/{**catch-all}"
        },

      }
    },
    "Clusters": {
      "order-cluster": {
        "LoadBalancingPolicy": "RoundRobin",
        "Destinations": {
          "order1": {
            "Address": "http://order-service:8080/"
          },
          "order2": {
            "Address": "http://order-service:8081/"
          }
        },
        "HealthCheck": {
          "Active": {
            "Enabled": true,
            "Interval": "00:00:10",
            "Path": "/health/ready"
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }
}
gRPC for Internal Communication
protobuf
// order.proto
syntax = "proto3";

service OrderService {
  rpc CreateOrder (CreateOrderRequest) returns (OrderResponse);
  rpc GetOrder (OrderRequest) returns (OrderResponse);
  rpc GetOrdersByCustomer (CustomerRequest) returns (OrderListResponse);
}

message CreateOrderRequest {
  string customer_id = 1;
  repeated OrderItem items = 2;
}

message OrderResponse {
  string id = 1;
  string status = 2;
  double total_amount = 3;
}

// C# implementation
public class OrderGrpcService : OrderService.OrderServiceBase
{
    private readonly IOrderService _orderService;
    
    public override async Task<OrderResponse> CreateOrder(
        CreateOrderRequest request, 
        ServerCallContext context)
    {
        var order = await _orderService.CreateOrderAsync(new CreateOrderCommand
        {
            CustomerId = request.CustomerId,
            Items = request.Items.Select(i => new OrderItemDto
            {
                ProductId = i.ProductId,
                Quantity = i.Quantity
            })
        });
        
        return new OrderResponse
        {
            Id = order.Id.ToString(),
            Status = order.Status,
            TotalAmount = order.TotalAmount
        };
    }
}
10. CI/CD & Release
GitHub Actions Pipeline
yaml
# .github/workflows/deploy.yml
name: Build and Deploy

on:
  push:
    branches: [main]
  pull_request:
    branches: [main]

env:
  DOTNET_VERSION: '8.0.x'

jobs:
  build-and-test:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
    - uses: actions/checkout@v3
    
    - name: Setup .NET
      uses: actions/setup-dotnet@v3
      with:
        dotnet-version: ${{ env.DOTNET_VERSION }}
    
    - name: Restore dependencies
      run: dotnet restore
    
    - name: Build
      run: dotnet build --configuration Release --no-restore
    
    - name: Run Unit Tests
      run: dotnet test --configuration Release --filter "Category=Unit"
    
    - name: Run Integration Tests
      run: dotnet test --configuration Release --filter "Category=Integration"
    
    - name: Run Security Scan
      run: dotnet tool run security-scan --project ./src/OrderService
    
    - name: SonarQube Analysis
      run: dotnet sonarscanner begin /k:"OrderService" /d:sonar.host.url="${{ secrets.SONAR_HOST_URL }}"
    
    - name: Build with SonarQube
      run: dotnet build
    
    - name: End SonarQube Analysis
      run: dotnet sonarscanner end
    
    - name: Build Docker Image
      run: docker build -t order-service:${{ github.sha }} .
    
    - name: Push to Container Registry
      run: |
        docker tag order-service:${{ github.sha }} myregistry.azurecr.io/order-service:${{ github.sha }}
        docker push myregistry.azurecr.io/order-service:${{ github.sha }}
    
    - name: Deploy to Staging
      run: |
        kubectl set image deployment/order-service order-service=myregistry.azurecr.io/order-service:${{ github.sha }} -n staging
        kubectl rollout status deployment/order-service -n staging --timeout=5m
    
    - name: Run Smoke Tests
      run: dotnet test --filter "Category=Smoke"
    
    - name: Deploy to Production (Canary)
      if: github.ref == 'refs/heads/main'
      run: |
        kubectl set image deployment/order-service order-service=myregistry.azurecr.io/order-service:${{ github.sha }} -n production --record
        kubectl rollout status deployment/order-service -n production --timeout=5m
    
    - name: Rollback on Failure
      if: failure()
      run: |
        kubectl rollout undo deployment/order-service -n production
Feature Flags with Azure App Configuration
csharp
// Configure feature flags
services.AddAzureAppConfiguration()
    .AddFeatureFlags(options =>
    {
        options.Select("OrderService:*");
        options.CacheExpirationInterval = TimeSpan.FromMinutes(5);
    });

// Use feature flags
public class OrderService
{
    private readonly IFeatureManager _featureManager;
    
    public async Task<Order> CreateOrderAsync(Order order)
    {
        // Check if new checkout flow is enabled
        if (await _featureManager.IsEnabledAsync("NewCheckoutFlow"))
        {
            return await _newCheckoutService.ProcessAsync(order);
        }
        else
        {
            return await _legacyCheckoutService.ProcessAsync(order);
        }
    }
}

// Configuration in Azure App Configuration
{
  "FeatureManagement": {
    "NewCheckoutFlow": {
      "EnabledFor": [
        {
          "Name": "Percentage",
          "Parameters": {
            "Value": 10  // 10% of users
          }
        }
      ]
    }
  }
}
This comprehensive guide covers each aspect of the production readiness checklist with practical, hands-on examples. The key is to implement these patterns gradually, starting with the most critical ones for your specific use case, and continuously improve based on production feedback and incidents.

Can you also provide explanation with examples for all the above step wise step?
I'll provide a comprehensive step-by-step explanation with detailed examples for each topic in the Microservices Production Readiness Checklist.

1. Service Design
Single Responsibility per Service
Step-by-Step Explanation:

Step 1: Identify Bounded Contexts
Start by analyzing your domain and identifying natural boundaries.

csharp
// BAD EXAMPLE - Monolithic approach with multiple responsibilities
[ApiController]
[Route("api/ecommerce")]
public class EcommerceController : ControllerBase
{
    // This service handles everything - users, orders, products, payments
    
    [HttpPost("users/register")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> RegisterUser(UserRegistrationDto user) { }
    
    [HttpPost("orders/create")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> CreateOrder(OrderDto order) { }
    
    [HttpPost("products/add")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> AddProduct(ProductDto product) { }
    
    [HttpPost("payments/process")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> ProcessPayment(PaymentDto payment) { }
}

// GOOD EXAMPLE - Separate services with single responsibility

// 1. User Service - ONLY handles user management
[ApiController]
[Route("api/users")]
public class UserController : ControllerBase
{
    private readonly IUserRepository _userRepository;
    private readonly IUserValidator _userValidator;
    
    public UserController(IUserRepository userRepository, IUserValidator userValidator)
    {
        _userRepository = userRepository;
        _userValidator = userValidator;
    }
    
    [HttpPost("register")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> RegisterUser(UserRegistrationDto user)
    {
        // Validate user data
        var validationResult = await _userValidator.ValidateAsync(user);
        if (!validationResult.IsValid)
            return BadRequest(validationResult.Errors);
        
        // Create user
        var newUser = await _userRepository.CreateAsync(user);
        
        // Publish event for other services (not directly calling them)
        await _eventBus.Publish(new UserCreatedEvent { UserId = newUser.Id });
        
        return Ok(newUser);
    }
}

// 2. Order Service - ONLY handles order management
[ApiController]
[Route("api/orders")]
public class OrderController : ControllerBase
{
    private readonly IOrderRepository _orderRepository;
    private readonly IOrderValidator _orderValidator;
    
    [HttpPost]
    public async Task<IActionResult> CreateOrder(CreateOrderDto order)
    {
        // Validate order
        var validationResult = await _orderValidator.ValidateAsync(order);
        if (!validationResult.IsValid)
            return BadRequest(validationResult.Errors);
        
        // Create order
        var newOrder = await _orderRepository.CreateAsync(order);
        
        // Publish event - other services will react
        await _eventBus.Publish(new OrderCreatedEvent { OrderId = newOrder.Id });
        
        return Ok(newOrder);
    }
}
API-First Contract Design (OpenAPI 3.0)
Step-by-Step Explanation:

Step 1: Define OpenAPI Specification First
Create your API contract before writing any code.

yaml
# openapi/order-service.yaml
openapi: 3.0.0
info:
  title: Order Service API
  version: 2.0.0
  description: |
    Manages customer orders with full lifecycle support.
    ## Features
    * Create orders with idempotency support
    * Track order status
    * Cancel orders within 30 minutes
servers:
  - url: https://api.example.com/v2
    description: Production server
  - url: https://staging-api.example.com/v2
    description: Staging server

paths:
  /orders:
    post:
      summary: Create a new order
      operationId: createOrder
      parameters:
        - name: Idempotency-Key
          in: header
          required: true
          schema:
            type: string
            format: uuid
          description: |
            Unique key to ensure idempotency. 
            Must be UUID v4 format. Key is valid for 24 hours.
      requestBody:
        required: true
        content:
          application/json:
            schema:
              $ref: '#/components/schemas/CreateOrderRequest'
      responses:
        '201':
          description: Order created successfully
          headers:
            Location:
              schema:
                type: string
                format: uri
              description: URL of the created order
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: '#/components/schemas/OrderResponse'
        '409':
          description: Duplicate order detected (idempotency key already used)
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: '#/components/schemas/ErrorResponse'
        '422':
          description: Validation error
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: '#/components/schemas/ValidationErrorResponse'

  /orders/{orderId}:
    get:
      summary: Get order by ID
      parameters:
        - name: orderId
          in: path
          required: true
          schema:
            type: string
            format: uuid
      responses:
        '200':
          description: Order found
          content:
            application/json:
              schema:
                $ref: '#/components/schemas/OrderResponse'
        '404':
          description: Order not found

components:
  schemas:
    CreateOrderRequest:
      type: object
      required:
        - customerId
        - items
        - shippingAddress
      properties:
        customerId:
          type: string
          format: uuid
          description: ID of the customer placing the order
        items:
          type: array
          minItems: 1
          items:
            $ref: '#/components/schemas/OrderItem'
        shippingAddress:
          $ref: '#/components/schemas/Address'
        notes:
          type: string
          maxLength: 500
          description: Optional notes for the order
    
    OrderItem:
      type: object
      required:
        - productId
        - quantity
      properties:
        productId:
          type: string
          format: uuid
        quantity:
          type: integer
          minimum: 1
          maximum: 999
        unitPrice:
          type: number
          format: decimal
          readOnly: true
          description: Price at time of order (populated by server)
    
    OrderResponse:
      type: object
      properties:
        id:
          type: string
          format: uuid
        status:
          type: string
          enum:
            - pending
            - confirmed
            - processing
            - shipped
            - delivered
            - cancelled
        createdAt:
          type: string
          format: date-time
        totalAmount:
          type: number
          format: decimal
        items:
          type: array
          items:
            $ref: '#/components/schemas/OrderItem'
    
    ErrorResponse:
      type: object
      properties:
        error:
          type: string
        code:
          type: string
        requestId:
          type: string
          format: uuid
Step 2: Generate Code from OpenAPI

Step 3: Implement the Contract

csharp
// Implement the contract exactly as defined
[ApiController]
[Route("api/v2/orders")]  // Version in URL
[Produces("application/json")]
public class OrdersController : ControllerBase
{
    private readonly IOrderService _orderService;
    private readonly ILogger<OrdersController> _logger;
    
    [HttpPost]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(OrderResponse), StatusCodes.Status201Created)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ErrorResponse), StatusCodes.Status409Conflict)]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(ValidationErrorResponse), StatusCodes.Status422UnprocessableEntity)]
    public async Task<ActionResult<OrderResponse>> CreateOrder(
        [FromBody] CreateOrderRequest request,
        [FromHeader(Name = "Idempotency-Key")] Guid idempotencyKey)
    {
        // Validate input according to spec
        if (!ModelState.IsValid)
            return UnprocessableEntity(new ValidationErrorResponse
            {
                Errors = ModelState.Values
                    .SelectMany(v => v.Errors)
                    .Select(e => e.ErrorMessage)
            });
        
        try
        {
            var order = await _orderService.CreateOrderAsync(
                request, 
                idempotencyKey);
            
            return CreatedAtAction(
                nameof(GetOrder), 
                new { orderId = order.Id }, 
                order);
        }
        catch (IdempotencyConflictException ex)
        {
            return Conflict(new ErrorResponse
            {
                Error = "Duplicate order detected",
                Code = "IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_USED",
                RequestId = HttpContext.TraceIdentifier
            });
        }
    }
    
    [HttpGet("{orderId:guid}")]
    [ProducesResponseType(typeof(OrderResponse), StatusCodes.Status200OK)]
    [ProducesResponseType(StatusCodes.Status404NotFound)]
    public async Task<ActionResult<OrderResponse>> GetOrder(Guid orderId)
    {
        var order = await _orderService.GetOrderAsync(orderId);
        
        if (order == null)
            return NotFound();
            
        return Ok(order);
    }
}

Idempotent Operations
Step-by-Step Implementation:

csharp
// Step 1: Create idempotency service
public interface IIdempotencyService
{
    Task<bool> TryProcessAsync<T>(string key, Func<Task<T>> operation, TimeSpan? ttl = null);
    Task<T> GetResultAsync<T>(string key);
}

public class IdempotencyService : IIdempotencyService
{
    private readonly IDistributedCache _cache;
    private readonly ILogger<IdempotencyService> _logger;
    
    public IdempotencyService(IDistributedCache cache, ILogger<IdempotencyService> logger)
    {
        _cache = cache;
        _logger = logger;
    }
    
    public async Task<bool> TryProcessAsync<T>(
        string key, 
        Func<Task<T>> operation, 
        TimeSpan? ttl = null)
    {
        // Check if already processed
        var cachedResult = await _cache.GetStringAsync(key);
        if (cachedResult != null)
        {
            _logger.LogInformation("Idempotent request {Key} already processed", key);
            return false;
        }
        
        // Try to lock the key
        var lockAcquired = await AcquireLockAsync(key);
        if (!lockAcquired)
        {
            _logger.LogWarning("Could not acquire lock for {Key}", key);
            return false;
        }
        
        try
        {
            // Execute operation
            var result = await operation();
            
            // Cache the result
            var options = new DistributedCacheEntryOptions
            {
                AbsoluteExpirationRelativeToNow = ttl ?? TimeSpan.FromHours(24)
            };
            await _cache.SetStringAsync(key, JsonSerializer.Serialize(result), options);
            
            return true;
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            _logger.LogError(ex, "Operation failed for idempotent request {Key}", key);
            throw;
        }
        finally
        {
            await ReleaseLockAsync(key);
        }
    }
    
    private async Task<bool> AcquireLockAsync(string key)
    {
        // Use Redis distributed lock
        var database = _redis.GetDatabase();
        return await database.LockTakeAsync($"lock:{key}", Environment.MachineName, TimeSpan.FromSeconds(30));
    }
}

// Step 2: Use in controller
[HttpPost]
public async Task<IActionResult> CreateOrder(
    [FromBody] CreateOrderRequest request,
    [FromHeader(Name = "Idempotency-Key")] string idempotencyKey)
{
    if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(idempotencyKey))
        return BadRequest("Idempotency-Key header is required");
    
    Order result = null;
    var processed = await _idempotencyService.TryProcessAsync(
        idempotencyKey,
        async () =>
        {
            // This will only execute once
            var order = await _orderService.CreateOrderAsync(request);
            await _eventBus.Publish(new OrderCreatedEvent { OrderId = order.Id });
            return order;
        });
    
    if (!processed)
    {
        // Retrieve previous result
        result = await _idempotencyService.GetResultAsync<Order>(idempotencyKey);
        if (result == null)
        {
            // Handle race condition - retry or return accepted
            return Accepted(new { Message = "Request is being processed", IdempotencyKey = idempotencyKey });
        }
    }
    else
    {
        result = await _orderService.GetOrderByKeyAsync(idempotencyKey);
    }
    
    return Ok(result);
}
Stateless Service Design
Step-by-Step Implementation:

csharp
// BAD - Stateful design
public class OrderService
{
    private static Dictionary<Guid, Order> _inMemoryOrders = new(); // BAD!
    private DateTime _lastProcessedTime; // BAD!
    
    public async Task ProcessOrders()
    {
        // Uses in-memory state that won't survive restart
        _lastProcessedTime = DateTime.UtcNow;
    }
}

// GOOD - Stateless design
public class OrderService
{
    private readonly IOrderRepository _repository; // External state storage
    private readonly IRedisCache _cache; // External cache
    
    public async Task<Order> GetOrderAsync(Guid orderId)
    {
        // State is stored externally
        return await _repository.GetByIdAsync(orderId);
    }
    
    public async Task<Order> ProcessOrderAsync(Guid orderId)
    {
        // All state is passed in or retrieved from external stores
        var order = await _repository.GetByIdAsync(orderId);
        
        // Process order
        order.Status = OrderStatus.Processing;
        
        // Save state externally
        await _repository.UpdateAsync(order);
        
        return order;
    }
}

2. Resilience & Fault Tolerance
Circuit Breaker Pattern (Polly)
Step-by-Step Implementation:

Step 1: Install Polly

bash
dotnet add package Polly
dotnet add package Microsoft.Extensions.Http.Polly
Step 2: Configure Circuit Breaker

csharp
// Program.cs
public static IHostBuilder CreateHostBuilder(string[] args) =>
    Host.CreateDefaultBuilder(args)
        .ConfigureServices((context, services) =>
        {
            // Configure HTTP client with circuit breaker
            services.AddHttpClient<IPaymentServiceClient, PaymentServiceClient>(client =>
            {
                client.BaseAddress = new Uri("https://payment-service");
                client.Timeout = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(30);
            })
            .AddPolicyHandler(GetCircuitBreakerPolicy())
            .AddPolicyHandler(GetRetryPolicy())
            .AddPolicyHandler(GetTimeoutPolicy());
        });

private static IAsyncPolicy<HttpResponseMessage> GetCircuitBreakerPolicy()
{
    return HttpPolicyExtensions
        .HandleTransientHttpError() // Handles 5xx and 408 responses
        .Or<TimeoutException>() // Handle timeouts
        .CircuitBreakerAsync(
            handledEventsAllowedBeforeBreaking: 3, // Fail 3 times
            durationOfBreak: TimeSpan.FromSeconds(30), // Break for 30 seconds
            onBreak: (outcome, breakDelay) =>
            {
                // Log when circuit opens
                Console.WriteLine($"Circuit broken for {breakDelay.TotalSeconds}s. Error: {outcome.Exception?.Message}");
                
                // Send alert to monitoring
                _telemetryClient.TrackEvent("CircuitBreakerOpened", new Dictionary<string, string>
                {
                    ["service"] = "payment-service",
                    ["reason"] = outcome.Exception?.Message
                });
            },
            onReset: () =>
            {
                // Log when circuit closes
                Console.WriteLine("Circuit reset");
                _telemetryClient.TrackEvent("CircuitBreakerReset");
            },
            onHalfOpen: () =>
            {
                // Log when testing the waters
                Console.WriteLine("Circuit half-open - testing");
            });
}

private static IAsyncPolicy<HttpResponseMessage> GetRetryPolicy()
{
    return HttpPolicyExtensions
        .HandleTransientHttpError()
        .Or<TimeoutException>()
        .WaitAndRetryAsync(
            retryCount: 3,
            sleepDurationProvider: (retryAttempt) => TimeSpan.FromSeconds(Math.Pow(2, retryAttempt)), // Exponential: 2,4,8 seconds
            onRetry: (outcome, timespan, retryCount, context) =>
            {
                Console.WriteLine($"Retry {retryCount} after {timespan.TotalSeconds}s");
                
                // Add retry count to logs
                using (LogContext.PushProperty("RetryCount", retryCount))
                {
                    _logger.LogWarning("Retry {RetryCount} for request", retryCount);
                }
            });
}

private static IAsyncPolicy<HttpResponseMessage> GetTimeoutPolicy()
{
    return Policy.TimeoutAsync<HttpResponseMessage>(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(10));
}
Step 3: Advanced Circuit Breaker with Monitoring

csharp
public class AdvancedCircuitBreaker
{
    private readonly ICircuitBreakerPolicy _policy;
    private readonly ILogger _logger;
    private readonly IMetrics _metrics;
    
    public async Task<T> ExecuteWithCircuitBreakerAsync<T>(
        Func<Task<T>> operation,
        string operationName,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        try
        {
            // Record attempt
            _metrics.IncrementCounter($"circuit_breaker.{operationName}.attempts");
            
            var result = await _policy.ExecuteAsync(async ct =>
            {
                return await operation();
            }, cancellationToken);
            
            // Record success
            _metrics.IncrementCounter($"circuit_breaker.{operationName}.success");
            
            return result;
        }
        catch (BrokenCircuitException ex)
        {
            // Circuit is open
            _metrics.IncrementCounter($"circuit_breaker.{operationName}.rejected");
            _logger.LogWarning(ex, "Circuit breaker is open for {OperationName}", operationName);
            
            // Use fallback
            return await GetFallbackResult<T>(operationName);
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            // Record failure
            _metrics.IncrementCounter($"circuit_breaker.{operationName}.failure");
            _logger.LogError(ex, "Operation {OperationName} failed", operationName);
            throw;
        }
    }
    
    private async Task<T> GetFallbackResult<T>(string operationName)
    {
        // Implement fallback logic
        if (typeof(T) == typeof(PaymentResponse))
        {
            return (T)(object)new PaymentResponse
            {
                Status = PaymentStatus.Pending,
                Message = "Payment processing is temporarily unavailable. Order will be processed later."
            };
        }
        
        throw new ServiceUnavailableException($"Service {operationName} is currently unavailable");
    }
}
Retry with Exponential Backoff
Step-by-Step Implementation:

csharp
public class RetryHandler : DelegatingHandler
{
    private readonly ILogger<RetryHandler> _logger;
    private readonly IAsyncPolicy<HttpResponseMessage> _retryPolicy;
    
    public RetryHandler(ILogger<RetryHandler> logger)
    {
        _logger = logger;
        
        _retryPolicy = Policy<HttpResponseMessage>
            .Handle<HttpRequestException>()
            .OrResult(r => IsTransientFailure(r.StatusCode))
            .WaitAndRetryAsync(
                retryCount: 5,
                sleepDurationProvider: CalculateBackoffDelay,
                onRetry: OnRetryAsync);
    }
    
    protected override async Task<HttpResponseMessage> SendAsync(
        HttpRequestMessage request,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var context = new Context
        {
            ["RequestId"] = Guid.NewGuid().ToString(),
            ["StartTime"] = DateTime.UtcNow
        };
        
        return await _retryPolicy.ExecuteAsync(
            async (ctx, ct) => await base.SendAsync(request, ct),
            context,
            cancellationToken);
    }
    
    private TimeSpan CalculateBackoffDelay(int retryAttempt)
    {
        // Exponential backoff with jitter
        var exponential = Math.Pow(2, retryAttempt); // 2^1=2, 2^2=4, 2^3=8, etc.
        var randomJitter = Random.Shared.NextDouble() * 0.5 + 0.5; // 0.5 to 1.5 multiplier
        var delayInSeconds = exponential * randomJitter;
        
        // Cap at 60 seconds
        delayInSeconds = Math.Min(delayInSeconds, 60);
        
        return TimeSpan.FromSeconds(delayInSeconds);
    }
    
    private Task OnRetryAsync(DelegateResult<HttpResponseMessage> outcome, TimeSpan timespan, int retryCount, Context context)
    {
        var requestId = context["RequestId"];
        var startTime = (DateTime)context["StartTime"];
        var elapsedTime = DateTime.UtcNow - startTime;
        
        _logger.LogWarning(
            outcome.Exception,
            "Retry {RetryCount} after {Delay}s for request {RequestId}. Total elapsed: {Elapsed}s. Status: {StatusCode}",
            retryCount,
            timespan.TotalSeconds,
            requestId,
            elapsedTime.TotalSeconds,
            outcome.Result?.StatusCode);
        
        // Add metrics
        _metrics.IncrementCounter("http_client.retries");
        _metrics.RecordHistogram("http_client.retry_delay", timespan.TotalMilliseconds);
        
        return Task.CompletedTask;
    }
    
    private bool IsTransientFailure(HttpStatusCode statusCode)
    {
        return statusCode == HttpStatusCode.RequestTimeout ||
               statusCode == HttpStatusCode.TooManyRequests ||
               statusCode == HttpStatusCode.ServiceUnavailable ||
               statusCode == HttpStatusCode.GatewayTimeout ||
               (int)statusCode >= 500;
    }
}
Bulkhead Isolation
Step-by-Step Implementation:

csharp
public class BulkheadIsolationService
{
    // Separate bulkheads for different user tiers
    private static readonly BulkheadPolicy _premiumBulkhead = Policy.BulkheadAsync(
        maxParallelization: 50,  // Max 50 concurrent operations
        maxQueuingActions: 100);  // Queue up to 100
    
    private static readonly BulkheadPolicy _standardBulkhead = Policy.BulkheadAsync(
        maxParallelization: 20,
        maxQueuingActions: 50);
    
    private static readonly BulkheadPolicy _freeBulkhead = Policy.BulkheadAsync(
        maxParallelization: 5,
        maxQueuingActions: 10);
    
    public async Task<Order> ProcessOrderAsync(Order order, UserTier userTier)
    {
        var policy = userTier switch
        {
            UserTier.Premium => _premiumBulkhead,
            UserTier.Standard => _standardBulkhead,
            _ => _freeBulkhead
        };
        
        try
        {
            return await policy.ExecuteAsync(async () =>
            {
                // This code is isolated to its own pool
                return await ProcessOrderInternal(order);
            });
        }
        catch (BulkheadRejectedException ex)
        {
            _logger.LogWarning(ex, "Bulkhead rejected request for {UserTier} user", userTier);
            throw new ServiceUnavailableException("System is busy. Please try again later.");
        }
    }
}

// Alternative: Partitioned bulkhead by tenant
public class TenantBulkheadManager
{
    private readonly ConcurrentDictionary<string, BulkheadPolicy> _tenantPolicies = new();
    private readonly SemaphoreSlim _semaphore = new(1, 1);
    
    public async Task<T> ExecuteForTenantAsync<T>(
        string tenantId,
        Func<Task<T>> operation,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var policy = await GetOrCreateTenantPolicy(tenantId);
        
        try
        {
            return await policy.ExecuteAsync(async ct => await operation(), cancellationToken);
        }
        catch (BulkheadRejectedException)
        {
            throw new TenantQuotaExceededException($"Tenant {tenantId} has exceeded its concurrency limit");
        }
    }
    
    private async Task<BulkheadPolicy> GetOrCreateTenantPolicy(string tenantId)
    {
        if (_tenantPolicies.TryGetValue(tenantId, out var policy))
            return policy;
        
        await _semaphore.WaitAsync();
        try
        {
            // Double-check after lock
            if (_tenantPolicies.TryGetValue(tenantId, out policy))
                return policy;
            
            // Get tenant configuration
            var config = await _tenantConfigService.GetTenantConfigAsync(tenantId);
            
            policy = Policy.BulkheadAsync(
                maxParallelization: config.MaxConcurrentRequests,
                maxQueuingActions: config.MaxQueueSize);
            
            _tenantPolicies[tenantId] = policy;
            return policy;
        }
        finally
        {
            _semaphore.Release();
        }
    }
}
3. Data Management
Database per Service
Step-by-Step Implementation:

Step 1: Create Separate Database Contexts

csharp
// OrderService - SQL Server Database
public class OrderDbContext : DbContext
{
    public OrderDbContext(DbContextOptions<OrderDbContext> options) : base(options) { }
    
    public DbSet<Order> Orders { get; set; }
    public DbSet<OrderItem> OrderItems { get; set; }
    public DbSet<OutboxMessage> OutboxMessages { get; set; }
    
    protected override void OnModelCreating(ModelBuilder modelBuilder)
    {
        modelBuilder.Entity<Order>(entity =>
        {
            entity.ToTable("Orders");
            entity.HasKey(e => e.Id);
            entity.Property(e => e.Id).ValueGeneratedNever();
            entity.Property(e => e.CustomerId).IsRequired();
            entity.Property(e => e.TotalAmount).HasPrecision(18, 2);
            entity.Property(e => e.Status).HasConversion<string>();
            entity.HasIndex(e => e.CustomerId);
            entity.HasIndex(e => e.Status);
        });
        
        modelBuilder.Entity<OrderItem>(entity =>
        {
            entity.ToTable("OrderItems");
            entity.HasKey(e => e.Id);
            entity.Property(e => e.UnitPrice).HasPrecision(18, 2);
            entity.HasOne<Order>()
                .WithMany(o => o.Items)
                .HasForeignKey(e => e.OrderId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);
        });
    }
}

// InventoryService - PostgreSQL Database
public class InventoryDbContext : DbContext
{
    public InventoryDbContext(DbContextOptions<InventoryDbContext> options) : base(options) { }
    
    public DbSet<Product> Products { get; set; }
    public DbSet<InventoryTransaction> Transactions { get; set; }
    public DbSet<Reservation> Reservations { get; set; }
    
    protected override void OnModelCreating(ModelBuilder modelBuilder)
    {
        modelBuilder.Entity<Product>(entity =>
        {
            entity.ToTable("products");
            entity.HasKey(e => e.Id);
            entity.Property(e => e.SKU).IsRequired().HasMaxLength(50);
            entity.HasIndex(e => e.SKU).IsUnique();
            entity.Property(e => e.Quantity).HasDefaultValue(0);
        });
        
        modelBuilder.Entity<InventoryTransaction>(entity =>
        {
            entity.ToTable("inventory_transactions");
            entity.Property(e => e.Type).HasConversion<string>();
            entity.HasIndex(e => e.ProductId);
            entity.HasIndex(e => e.CreatedAt);
        });
    }
}
Step 2: Database Migration Strategy

csharp
// Program.cs - Apply migrations at startup
public static async Task Main(string[] args)
{
    var host = CreateHostBuilder(args).Build();
    
    // Apply migrations automatically
    using (var scope = host.Services.CreateScope())
    {
        var dbContext = scope.ServiceProvider.GetRequiredService<OrderDbContext>();
        await dbContext.Database.MigrateAsync();
    }
    
    await host.RunAsync();
}

CQRS for Read/Write Separation
Step-by-Step Implementation:

csharp
// Step 1: Define Commands (Write side)
public record CreateOrderCommand : IRequest<Order>
{
    public Guid CustomerId { get; init; }
    public List<OrderItemDto> Items { get; init; }
    public Address ShippingAddress { get; init; }
    public string? Notes { get; init; }
}

public record UpdateOrderStatusCommand : IRequest
{
    public Guid OrderId { get; init; }
    public OrderStatus NewStatus { get; init; }
}

// Step 2: Define Queries (Read side)
public record GetOrderQuery : IRequest<OrderReadModel>
{
    public Guid OrderId { get; init; }
}

public record GetCustomerOrdersQuery : IRequest<PagedResult<OrderSummaryReadModel>>
{
    public Guid CustomerId { get; init; }
    public int PageNumber { get; init; } = 1;
    public int PageSize { get; init; } = 20;
    public OrderStatus? Status { get; init; }
}

// Step 3: Command Handler (Write side)
public class CreateOrderCommandHandler : IRequestHandler<CreateOrderCommand, Order>
{
    private readonly OrderDbContext _writeDbContext;
    private readonly IEventBus _eventBus;
    private readonly ILogger<CreateOrderCommandHandler> _logger;
    
    public async Task<Order> Handle(CreateOrderCommand command, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        // Validate
        var order = new Order
        {
            Id = Guid.NewGuid(),
            CustomerId = command.CustomerId,
            Status = OrderStatus.Pending,
            CreatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow,
            Items = command.Items.Select(i => new OrderItem
            {
                Id = Guid.NewGuid(),
                ProductId = i.ProductId,
                Quantity = i.Quantity,
                UnitPrice = i.UnitPrice
            }).ToList(),
            TotalAmount = command.Items.Sum(i => i.Quantity * i.UnitPrice),
            ShippingAddress = command.ShippingAddress,
            Notes = command.Notes
        };
        
        // Save to write database
        using var transaction = await _writeDbContext.Database.BeginTransactionAsync(cancellationToken);
        
        try
        {
            _writeDbContext.Orders.Add(order);
            await _writeDbContext.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);
            
            // Publish event for read model updates
            await _eventBus.Publish(new OrderCreatedEvent
            {
                OrderId = order.Id,
                CustomerId = order.CustomerId,
                TotalAmount = order.TotalAmount,
                CreatedAt = order.CreatedAt
            });
            
            await transaction.CommitAsync(cancellationToken);
            
            _logger.LogInformation("Order {OrderId} created", order.Id);
            return order;
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            await transaction.RollbackAsync(cancellationToken);
            _logger.LogError(ex, "Failed to create order");
            throw;
        }
    }
}

// Step 4: Query Handler (Read side)
public class GetCustomerOrdersQueryHandler : IRequestHandler<GetCustomerOrdersQuery, PagedResult<OrderSummaryReadModel>>
{
    private readonly OrderReadDbContext _readDbContext; // Optimized for reads
    
    public async Task<PagedResult<OrderSummaryReadModel>> Handle(
        GetCustomerOrdersQuery query, 
        CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        var queryable = _readDbContext.OrderSummaries
            .Where(o => o.CustomerId == query.CustomerId);
        
        if (query.Status.HasValue)
            queryable = queryable.Where(o => o.Status == query.Status);
        
        var totalCount = await queryable.CountAsync(cancellationToken);
        
        var items = await queryable
            .OrderByDescending(o => o.CreatedAt)
            .Skip((query.PageNumber - 1) * query.PageSize)
            .Take(query.PageSize)
            .Select(o => new OrderSummaryReadModel
            {
                OrderId = o.OrderId,
                Status = o.Status,
                TotalAmount = o.TotalAmount,
                CreatedAt = o.CreatedAt,
                ItemCount = o.ItemCount
            })
            .ToListAsync(cancellationToken);
        
        return new PagedResult<OrderSummaryReadModel>
        {
            Items = items,
            TotalCount = totalCount,
            PageNumber = query.PageNumber,
            PageSize = query.PageSize
        };
    }
}

// Step 5: Read Model (Denormalized for query performance)
public class OrderReadModel
{
    public Guid OrderId { get; set; }
    public Guid CustomerId { get; set; }
    public string CustomerName { get; set; }
    public string CustomerEmail { get; set; }
    public OrderStatus Status { get; set; }
    public decimal TotalAmount { get; set; }
    public DateTime CreatedAt { get; set; }
    public DateTime? ShippedAt { get; set; }
    public DateTime? DeliveredAt { get; set; }
    public List<OrderItemReadModel> Items { get; set; }
    public Address ShippingAddress { get; set; }
}

// Step 6: Event handler to update read model
public class OrderCreatedEventHandler : INotificationHandler<OrderCreatedEvent>
{
    private readonly OrderReadDbContext _readDbContext;
    
    public async Task Handle(OrderCreatedEvent notification, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
    {
        // Update denormalized read model
        var readModel = new OrderReadModel
        {
            OrderId = notification.OrderId,
            CustomerId = notification.CustomerId,
            Status = OrderStatus.Pending,
            TotalAmount = notification.TotalAmount,
            CreatedAt = notification.CreatedAt
        };
        
        await _readDbContext.OrderReadModels.AddAsync(readModel, cancellationToken);
        await _readDbContext.SaveChangesAsync(cancellationToken);
    }
}

1. Service Design
Single Responsibility per Service
Step-by-Step Explanation:

Step 1: Identify Bounded Contexts

java
// BAD EXAMPLE - Monolithic approach with multiple responsibilities
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/ecommerce")
public class EcommerceController {
    // This service handles everything - users, orders, products, payments
    
    @PostMapping("/users/register")
    public ResponseEntity<UserDto> registerUser(@RequestBody UserRegistrationDto user) { }
    
    @PostMapping("/orders/create")
    public ResponseEntity<OrderDto> createOrder(@RequestBody OrderDto order) { }
    
    @PostMapping("/products/add")
    public ResponseEntity<ProductDto> addProduct(@RequestBody ProductDto product) { }
    
    @PostMapping("/payments/process")
    public ResponseEntity<PaymentDto> processPayment(@RequestBody PaymentDto payment) { }
}

// GOOD EXAMPLE - Separate services with single responsibility

// 1. User Service - ONLY handles user management
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/users")
public class UserController {
    
    private final UserRepository userRepository;
    private final UserValidator userValidator;
    private final EventPublisher eventPublisher;
    
    public UserController(UserRepository userRepository, 
                         UserValidator userValidator,
                         EventPublisher eventPublisher) {
        this.userRepository = userRepository;
        this.userValidator = userValidator;
        this.eventPublisher = eventPublisher;
    }
    
    @PostMapping("/register")
    public ResponseEntity<UserDto> registerUser(@Valid @RequestBody UserRegistrationDto user) {
        // Validate user data
        ValidationResult validationResult = userValidator.validate(user);
        if (!validationResult.isValid()) {
            return ResponseEntity.badRequest().body(validationResult.getErrors());
        }
        
        // Create user
        User newUser = userRepository.save(user.toEntity());
        
        // Publish event for other services (not directly calling them)
        eventPublisher.publish(new UserCreatedEvent(newUser.getId()));
        
        return ResponseEntity.ok(UserDto.fromEntity(newUser));
    }
}

// 2. Order Service - ONLY handles order management
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/orders")
public class OrderController {
    
    private final OrderRepository orderRepository;
    private final OrderValidator orderValidator;
    private final EventPublisher eventPublisher;
    
    @PostMapping
    public ResponseEntity<OrderDto> createOrder(@Valid @RequestBody CreateOrderDto order) {
        // Validate order
        ValidationResult validationResult = orderValidator.validate(order);
        if (!validationResult.isValid()) {
            return ResponseEntity.badRequest().body(validationResult.getErrors());
        }
        
        // Create order
        Order newOrder = orderRepository.save(order.toEntity());
        
        // Publish event - other services will react
        eventPublisher.publish(new OrderCreatedEvent(newOrder.getId()));
        
        return ResponseEntity.ok(OrderDto.fromEntity(newOrder));
    }
}

Step 3: Implement Contract with Annotations

java
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/v2/orders")
@OpenAPIDefinition(
    info = @Info(
        title = "Order Service API",
        version = "2.0.0",
        description = "Manages customer orders with full lifecycle support"
    ),
    servers = @Server(url = "https://api.example.com/v2", description = "Production server")
)
public class OrderController {
    
    private final OrderService orderService;
    
    @Operation(summary = "Create a new order")
    @ApiResponses(value = {
        @ApiResponse(responseCode = "201", description = "Order created successfully",
                     content = @Content(schema = @Schema(implementation = OrderResponse.class))),
        @ApiResponse(responseCode = "409", description = "Duplicate order detected",
                     content = @Content(schema = @Schema(implementation = ErrorResponse.class))),
        @ApiResponse(responseCode = "422", description = "Validation error")
    })
    @PostMapping
    public ResponseEntity<OrderResponse> createOrder(
            @Valid @RequestBody CreateOrderRequest request,
            @RequestHeader("Idempotency-Key") UUID idempotencyKey) {
        
        OrderResponse order = orderService.createOrder(request, idempotencyKey);
        return ResponseEntity.status(HttpStatus.CREATED).body(order);
    }
    
    @Operation(summary = "Get order by ID")
    @GetMapping("/{orderId}")
    public ResponseEntity<OrderResponse> getOrder(@PathVariable UUID orderId) {
        OrderResponse order = orderService.getOrder(orderId);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(order);
    }
}

                        .scheme("bearer")

    }
