Retry with Exponential Backoff
Definition: A retry strategy that increases the delay between retry attempts exponentially (e.g., 1s, 2s, 4s, 8s). This prevents overwhelming a struggling service and allows time for recovery. Jitter is often added to prevent retry storms.

Why it matters: Simple immediate retries can overwhelm a failing service. Exponential backoff with jitter distributes retry attempts, reduces load on struggling services, and increases the chance of successful recovery.

Step-by-Step Implementation:

java
// Step 1: Custom retry with exponential backoff
@Service
@Slf4j
public class RetryWithExponentialBackoffService {
    
    private final RestTemplate restTemplate;
    private final RetryTemplate retryTemplate;
    
    public RetryWithExponentialBackoffService(RestTemplate restTemplate) {
        this.restTemplate = restTemplate;
        this.retryTemplate = createRetryTemplate();
    }
    
    private RetryTemplate createRetryTemplate() {
        RetryTemplate retryTemplate = new RetryTemplate();
        
        // Exponential backoff policy
        ExponentialBackOffPolicy backOffPolicy = new ExponentialBackOffPolicy();
        backOffPolicy.setInitialInterval(1000);  // Start with 1 second
        backOffPolicy.setMultiplier(2.0);        // Double each time
        backOffPolicy.setMaxInterval(60000);     // Max 60 seconds
        
        retryTemplate.setBackOffPolicy(backOffPolicy);
        
        // Retry on specific exceptions
        SimpleRetryPolicy retryPolicy = new SimpleRetryPolicy();
        retryPolicy.setMaxAttempts(5);  // Try up to 5 times
        
        Map<Class<? extends Throwable>, Boolean> retryableExceptions = new HashMap<>();
        retryableExceptions.put(HttpServerErrorException.class, true);
        retryableExceptions.put(TimeoutException.class, true);
        retryableExceptions.put(ResourceAccessException.class, true);
        retryableExceptions.put(ConnectException.class, true);
        
        retryPolicy.setRetryableExceptions(retryableExceptions);
        retryTemplate.setRetryPolicy(retryPolicy);
        
        // Add retry listeners for monitoring
        retryTemplate.registerListener(new RetryListener() {
            @Override
            public <T, E extends Throwable> boolean open(RetryContext context, 
                                                         RetryCallback<T, E> callback) {
                log.info("Starting retry for method with max attempts: {}", 
                    context.getRetryCount() + 1);
                return true;
            }
            
            @Override
            public <T, E extends Throwable> void onError(RetryContext context,
                                                         RetryCallback<T, E> callback,
                                                         Throwable throwable) {
                int attempt = context.getRetryCount();
                log.warn("Retry attempt {}/{} failed. Will retry after {}ms", 
                    attempt, 
                    context.getAttribute("maxAttempts"),
                    getBackoffDelay(attempt),
                    throwable);
            }
            
            @Override
            public <T, E extends Throwable> void close(RetryContext context,
                                                       RetryCallback<T, E> callback,
                                                       Throwable throwable) {
                if (throwable != null) {
                    log.error("All retry attempts exhausted after {} attempts", 
                        context.getRetryCount(), throwable);
                } else {
                    log.info("Operation succeeded after {} attempts", context.getRetryCount());
                }
            }
        });
        
        return retryTemplate;
    }
    
    private long getBackoffDelay(int attempt) {
        long delay = (long) (1000 * Math.pow(2, attempt));  // Exponential
        long jitter = (long) (delay * 0.1 * Math.random());  // Add 10% jitter
        return Math.min(delay + jitter, 60000);  // Cap at 60 seconds
    }
    
    public OrderResponse createOrderWithRetry(CreateOrderRequest request) {
        return retryTemplate.execute(context -> {
            log.info("Attempting to create order (attempt: {})", context.getRetryCount() + 1);
            
            ResponseEntity<OrderResponse> response = restTemplate.postForEntity(
                "http://order-service/api/orders",
                request,
                OrderResponse.class
            );
            
            return response.getBody();
        });
    }
}

// Step 2: Using Resilience4j annotations
@Service
@Slf4j
public class OrderRetryService {
    
    @Retry(name = "order-creation", fallbackMethod = "createOrderFallback")
    @CircuitBreaker(name = "order-service")
    @TimeLimiter(name = "order-creation", fallbackMethod = "timeoutFallback")
    public CompletableFuture<OrderResponse> createOrder(CreateOrderRequest request) {
        return CompletableFuture.supplyAsync(() -> {
            log.info("Creating order with retry support");
            
            // Simulate potential failures
            if (shouldSimulateFailure()) {
                throw new HttpServerErrorException(
                    HttpStatus.SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE, 
                    "Order service temporarily unavailable"
                );
            }
            
            return restTemplate.postForObject(
                "http://order-service/api/orders",
                request,
                OrderResponse.class
            );
        });
    }
    
    // Fallback method with same signature + exception parameter
    public CompletableFuture<OrderResponse> createOrderFallback(
            CreateOrderRequest request, Exception ex) {
        
        log.error("All retries exhausted for order creation", ex);
        
        // Create a pending order that will be processed later
        OrderResponse pendingOrder = OrderResponse.builder()
            .id(UUID.randomUUID())
            .status(OrderStatus.PENDING_RETRY)
            .message("Order creation is pending. We'll notify you once processed.")
            .build();
        
        // Store in database for later processing
        savePendingOrder(pendingOrder, request);
        
        return CompletableFuture.completedFuture(pendingOrder);
    }
    
    public CompletableFuture<OrderResponse> timeoutFallback(
            CreateOrderRequest request, Exception ex) {
        
        log.warn("Order creation timed out, using fallback", ex);
        
        return CompletableFuture.completedFuture(
            OrderResponse.builder()
                .id(UUID.randomUUID())
                .status(OrderStatus.PENDING)
                .message("Request is taking longer than expected. We'll notify you.")
                .build()
        );
    }
    
    private boolean shouldSimulateFailure() {
        // Simulate 30% failure rate for testing
        return Math.random() < 0.3;
    }
}

// Step 3: Advanced retry with jitter and backoff
@Configuration
public class AdvancedRetryConfig {
    
    @Bean
    public RetryTemplate advancedRetryTemplate() {
        RetryTemplate retryTemplate = new RetryTemplate();
        
        // Exponential backoff with jitter
        ExponentialRandomBackOffPolicy backOffPolicy = new ExponentialRandomBackOffPolicy();
        backOffPolicy.setInitialInterval(1000);
        backOffPolicy.setMultiplier(2);
        backOffPolicy.setMaxInterval(60000);
        
        retryTemplate.setBackOffPolicy(backOffPolicy);
        
        // Retry on any exception except specific ones
        ExceptionClassifierRetryPolicy retryPolicy = new ExceptionClassifierRetryPolicy();
        
        // Don't retry on business exceptions
        retryPolicy.setExceptionClassifier(
            classifiable -> {
                if (classifiable instanceof BusinessException) {
                    return new NeverRetryPolicy();
                }
                if (classifiable instanceof AuthenticationException) {
                    return new NeverRetryPolicy();
                }
                return new SimpleRetryPolicy(5);
            }
        );
        
        retryTemplate.setRetryPolicy(retryPolicy);
        
        return retryTemplate;
    }
}

Timeout Policies
Definition: Setting maximum time limits for operations, especially external calls. If an operation doesn't complete within the timeout, it's automatically cancelled, preventing resource exhaustion and ensuring system responsiveness.

Why it matters: Without timeouts, a slow or stuck downstream service can cause thread pool exhaustion, increased latency, and cascading failures. Timeouts ensure that services fail fast and resources are freed promptly.

Step-by-Step Implementation:

java
// Step 1: Configure timeouts at different levels
@Configuration
@Slf4j
public class TimeoutConfiguration {
    
    @Bean
    public RestTemplate restTemplate() {
        RestTemplate restTemplate = new RestTemplate();
        
        // Connection timeout
        HttpComponentsClientHttpRequestFactory factory = new HttpComponentsClientHttpRequestFactory();
        factory.setConnectTimeout(3000);      // 3 seconds to establish connection
        factory.setConnectionRequestTimeout(2000);  // 2 seconds to get connection from pool
        factory.setReadTimeout(10000);        // 10 seconds to read data
        
        restTemplate.setRequestFactory(factory);
        
        // Add interceptors for timeout tracking
        restTemplate.setInterceptors(List.of(new TimeoutTrackingInterceptor()));
        
        return restTemplate;
    }
    
    @Bean
    public WebClient webClient() {
        return WebClient.builder()
            .clientConnector(new ReactorClientHttpConnector(
                HttpClient.create()
                    .option(ChannelOption.CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MILLIS, 3000)
                    .responseTimeout(Duration.ofSeconds(10))
                    .doOnConnected(conn -> 
                        conn.addHandlerLast(new ReadTimeoutHandler(10))
                            .addHandlerLast(new WriteTimeoutHandler(10))
                    )
            ))
            .build();
    }
    
    // Timeout tracking interceptor
    class TimeoutTrackingInterceptor implements ClientHttpRequestInterceptor {
        @Override
        public ClientHttpResponse intercept(HttpRequest request, byte[] body, 
                                           ClientHttpRequestExecution execution) 
                throws IOException {
            long startTime = System.currentTimeMillis();
            
            try {
                ClientHttpResponse response = execution.execute(request, body);
                long duration = System.currentTimeMillis() - startTime;
                
                if (duration > 5000) {
                    log.warn("Slow API call to {} took {}ms", 
                        request.getURI(), duration);
                }
                
                return response;
            } catch (SocketTimeoutException e) {
                log.error("Timeout calling {}", request.getURI(), e);
                throw new TimeoutException("Request timed out");
            }
        }
    }
}

// Step 2: Service with timeout handling
@Service
@Slf4j
public class OrderProcessingService {
    
    private final RestTemplate restTemplate;
    private final AsyncRestTemplate asyncRestTemplate;
    
    // Timeout using CompletableFuture
    public OrderResponse createOrderWithTimeout(CreateOrderRequest request) {
        CompletableFuture<OrderResponse> future = CompletableFuture.supplyAsync(() -> {
            return restTemplate.postForObject(
                "http://order-service/api/orders",
                request,
                OrderResponse.class
            );
        });
        
        try {
            // Wait for result with timeout
            return future.get(10, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
        } catch (TimeoutException e) {
            log.error("Order creation timed out after 10 seconds", e);
            future.cancel(true);  // Cancel the operation
            
            // Return fallback response
            return OrderResponse.builder()
                .status(OrderStatus.PENDING)
                .message("Order is being processed. We'll notify you when complete.")
                .build();
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("Order creation failed", e);
            throw new RuntimeException("Order creation failed", e);
        }
    }
    
    // Timeout using @Async and Future
    @Async
    public CompletableFuture<InventoryResponse> checkInventoryWithTimeout(List<OrderItem> items) {
        return CompletableFuture.supplyAsync(() -> {
            try {
                return restTemplate.postForObject(
                    "http://inventory-service/api/check",
                    items,
                    InventoryResponse.class
                );
            } catch (Exception e) {
                log.error("Inventory check failed", e);
                throw new RuntimeException("Inventory check failed", e);
            }
        })
        .orTimeout(5, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
        .exceptionally(throwable -> {
            if (throwable instanceof TimeoutException) {
                log.warn("Inventory check timed out");
                return InventoryResponse.builder()
                    .available(true)
                    .message("Inventory check timed out, assuming items available")
                    .build();
            }
            return null;
        });
    }
}

// Step 3: Timeout with Resilience4j
@Service
@Slf4j
public class TimeoutService {
    
    @TimeLimiter(name = "payment-processing", fallbackMethod = "processPaymentFallback")
    public CompletableFuture<PaymentResponse> processPayment(PaymentRequest request) {
        return CompletableFuture.supplyAsync(() -> {
            // Simulate slow operation
            try {
                Thread.sleep(15000);  // 15 seconds
            } catch (InterruptedException e) {
                Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
            }
            
            return restTemplate.postForObject(
                "http://payment-service/api/payments",
                request,
                PaymentResponse.class
            );
        });
    }
    
    public CompletableFuture<PaymentResponse> processPaymentFallback(
            PaymentRequest request, TimeoutException ex) {
        
        log.warn("Payment processing timed out for order: {}", request.getOrderId(), ex);
        
        return CompletableFuture.completedFuture(
            PaymentResponse.builder()
                .status(PaymentStatus.PENDING)
                .message("Payment processing is taking longer than expected. " +
                        "We'll notify you once completed.")
                .orderId(request.getOrderId())
                .build()
        );
    }
    
    @Bulkhead(name = "inventory-bulkhead")
    @TimeLimiter(name = "inventory-check", fallbackMethod = "inventoryCheckFallback")
    public CompletableFuture<InventoryResponse> checkInventory(List<OrderItem> items) {
        return CompletableFuture.supplyAsync(() -> {
            // Set timeout on HTTP call
            RequestConfig config = RequestConfig.custom()
                .setConnectTimeout(3000)
                .setConnectionRequestTimeout(2000)
                .setSocketTimeout(5000)
                .build();
            
            CloseableHttpClient client = HttpClientBuilder.create()
                .setDefaultRequestConfig(config)
                .build();
            
            HttpComponentsClientHttpRequestFactory factory = 
                new HttpComponentsClientHttpRequestFactory(client);
            
            RestTemplate timeoutRestTemplate = new RestTemplate(factory);
            
            return timeoutRestTemplate.postForObject(
                "http://inventory-service/api/check",
                items,
                InventoryResponse.class
            );
        });
    }
}

Bulkhead Isolation
Definition: A pattern that isolates different components or tenants into separate "pools" so that failure in one doesn't affect others. Like compartments in a ship, if one compartment floods, others remain operational. Bulkheads can be thread pool-based or semaphore-based.

Why it matters: Prevents cascading failures by ensuring that a failure in one service or for one tenant doesn't consume all resources and cause system-wide outage. Critical operations are protected from noisy neighbors.

Step-by-Step Implementation:

java
// Step 1: Configure thread pool bulkheads
@Configuration
@Slf4j
public class BulkheadConfiguration {
    
    // Dedicated executor for premium customers
    @Bean(name = "premiumExecutor")
    public ExecutorService premiumExecutor() {
        return new ThreadPoolExecutor(
            20,   // core pool size
            50,   // max pool size
            60L,  // keep alive time
            TimeUnit.SECONDS,
            new LinkedBlockingQueue<>(100),
            new ThreadPoolExecutor.CallerRunsPolicy()  // Backpressure
        );
    }
    
    // Dedicated executor for standard customers
    @Bean(name = "standardExecutor")
    public ExecutorService standardExecutor() {
        return new ThreadPoolExecutor(
            10,   // core pool size
            20,   // max pool size
            60L,  // keep alive time
            TimeUnit.SECONDS,
            new LinkedBlockingQueue<>(50),
            new ThreadPoolExecutor.CallerRunsPolicy()
        );
    }
    
    // Dedicated executor for free tier
    @Bean(name = "freeExecutor")
    public ExecutorService freeExecutor() {
        return new ThreadPoolExecutor(
            2,    // core pool size
            5,    // max pool size
            60L,  // keep alive time
            TimeUnit.SECONDS,
            new LinkedBlockingQueue<>(10),
            new ThreadPoolExecutor.AbortPolicy()  // Reject if full
        );
    }
}

// Step 2: Service with bulkhead isolation
@Service
@Slf4j
public class OrderProcessingService {
    
    private final ExecutorService premiumExecutor;
    private final ExecutorService standardExecutor;
    private final ExecutorService freeExecutor;
    
    public OrderProcessingService(
            @Qualifier("premiumExecutor") ExecutorService premiumExecutor,
            @Qualifier("standardExecutor") ExecutorService standardExecutor,
            @Qualifier("freeExecutor") ExecutorService freeExecutor) {
        this.premiumExecutor = premiumExecutor;
        this.standardExecutor = standardExecutor;
        this.freeExecutor = freeExecutor;
    }
    
    public CompletableFuture<OrderResponse> processOrder(OrderRequest request, 
                                                          CustomerTier tier) {
        // Select appropriate executor based on customer tier
        ExecutorService executor = switch (tier) {
            case PREMIUM -> premiumExecutor;
            case STANDARD -> standardExecutor;
            case FREE -> freeExecutor;
        };
        
        return CompletableFuture.supplyAsync(() -> {
            log.info("Processing order for {} tier on thread pool: {}", 
                tier, executor.getClass().getSimpleName());
            
            // Simulate work
            try {
                Thread.sleep(2000);
            } catch (InterruptedException e) {
                Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
            }
            
            return OrderResponse.builder()
                .id(UUID.randomUUID())
                .status(OrderStatus.PROCESSING)
                .tier(tier)
                .build();
                
        }, executor).exceptionally(throwable -> {
            log.error("Order processing failed for {} tier", tier, throwable);
            
            if (throwable.getCause() instanceof RejectedExecutionException) {
                // Pool is full - provide appropriate response
                return OrderResponse.builder()
                    .status(OrderStatus.QUEUED)
                    .message(getQueueFullMessage(tier))
                    .build();
            }
            
            return OrderResponse.builder()
                .status(OrderStatus.FAILED)
                .message("Processing failed")
                .build();
        });
    }
    
    private String getQueueFullMessage(CustomerTier tier) {
        return switch (tier) {
            case PREMIUM -> "System is busy. Priority processing in progress.";
            case STANDARD -> "High load. Expected processing time: 2-3 minutes.";
            case FREE -> "Free tier capacity reached. Please try again later.";
        };
    }
}

// Step 3: Resilience4j bulkhead with annotations
@Service
@Slf4j
public class InventoryService {
    
    // Semaphore-based bulkhead (limits concurrent calls)
    @Bulkhead(name = "inventory-check", type = Bulkhead.Type.SEMAPHORE)
    public InventoryResponse checkInventory(String productId) {
        log.info("Checking inventory for product: {}", productId);
        
        // This limits concurrent calls to this method
        // If maxConcurrentCalls is reached, calls are rejected immediately
        return restTemplate.getForObject(
            "http://inventory-service/api/products/" + productId,
            InventoryResponse.class
        );
    }
    
    // Thread pool-based bulkhead
    @Bulkhead(name = "inventory-bulk-operations", type = Bulkhead.Type.THREADPOOL)
    @TimeLimiter(name = "inventory-bulk")
    public CompletableFuture<List<InventoryResponse>> bulkInventoryCheck(
            List<String> productIds) {
        
        return CompletableFuture.supplyAsync(() -> {
            // This uses a dedicated thread pool
            return productIds.stream()
                .map(this::checkInventory)
                .collect(Collectors.toList());
        });
    }
    
    // Fallback when bulkhead is full
    public CompletableFuture<List<InventoryResponse>> bulkInventoryCheckFallback(
            List<String> productIds, Exception ex) {
        
        log.warn("Bulk inventory check rejected, using cached data");
        
        // Return cached responses
        return CompletableFuture.completedFuture(
            productIds.stream()
                .map(id -> getCachedInventory(id))
                .collect(Collectors.toList())
        );
    }
}

// Step 4: Tenant-based bulkhead isolation
@Component
@Slf4j
public class TenantBulkheadManager {
    
    private final Map<String, Bulkhead> tenantBulkheads = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<String, ThreadPoolBulkhead> tenantThreadPools = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final TenantConfigService tenantConfigService;
    
    public TenantBulkheadManager(TenantConfigService tenantConfigService) {
        this.tenantConfigService = tenantConfigService;
    }
    
    public <T> T executeForTenant(String tenantId, Supplier<T> operation) {
        Bulkhead bulkhead = getOrCreateSemaphoreBulkhead(tenantId);
        
        try {
            return bulkhead.executeSupplier(operation);
        } catch (BulkheadFullException e) {
            log.warn("Bulkhead full for tenant: {}, current capacity: {}", 
                tenantId, bulkhead.getMetrics().getAvailableConcurrentCalls());
            
            // Record metrics
            recordBulkheadRejection(tenantId);
            
            throw new TenantQuotaExceededException(
                "Tenant " + tenantId + " has exceeded its request limit");
        }
    }
    
    public <T> CompletableFuture<T> executeAsyncForTenant(
            String tenantId, Supplier<T> operation) {
        
        ThreadPoolBulkhead bulkhead = getOrCreateThreadPoolBulkhead(tenantId);
        
        return bulkhead.executeSupplier(operation)
            .exceptionally(throwable -> {
                if (throwable.getCause() instanceof BulkheadFullException) {
                    throw new TenantQuotaExceededException(
                        "Tenant " + tenantId + " queue is full");
                }
                throw new RuntimeException(throwable);
            });
    }
    
    private Bulkhead getOrCreateSemaphoreBulkhead(String tenantId) {
        return tenantBulkheads.computeIfAbsent(tenantId, id -> {
            TenantConfig config = tenantConfigService.getTenantConfig(id);
            
            BulkheadConfig bulkheadConfig = BulkheadConfig.custom()
                .maxConcurrentCalls(config.getMaxConcurrentRequests())
                .maxWaitDuration(Duration.ofMillis(config.getMaxWaitTime()))
                .build();
            
            log.info("Created bulkhead for tenant {} with max calls: {}", 
                id, config.getMaxConcurrentRequests());
            
            return Bulkhead.of("tenant-" + id, bulkheadConfig);
        });
    }
    
    private ThreadPoolBulkhead getOrCreateThreadPoolBulkhead(String tenantId) {
        return tenantThreadPools.computeIfAbsent(tenantId, id -> {
            TenantConfig config = tenantConfigService.getTenantConfig(id);
            
            ThreadPoolBulkheadConfig threadPoolConfig = 
                ThreadPoolBulkheadConfig.custom()
                    .maxThreadPoolSize(config.getMaxThreads())
                    .coreThreadPoolSize(config.getCoreThreads())
                    .queueCapacity(config.getQueueSize())
                    .keepAliveDuration(Duration.ofMinutes(1))
                    .build();
            
            log.info("Created thread pool bulkhead for tenant {} with threads: {}/{}", 
                id, config.getCoreThreads(), config.getMaxThreads());
            
            return ThreadPoolBulkhead.of("tenant-pool-" + id, threadPoolConfig);
        });
    }
    
    @EventListener
    public void onBulkheadEvent(BulkheadOnCallRejectedEvent event) {
        log.warn("Bulkhead call rejected: {}", event.getBulkheadName());
        // Send alert if threshold exceeded
    }
}

Event-Driven Architecture Overview
Definition: Event-Driven Architecture (EDA) is a software architecture pattern where services communicate asynchronously by producing and consuming events. An event represents a state change or significant occurrence (e.g., "OrderCreated", "PaymentProcessed"). Services are decoupled, communicating through a message broker (like Kafka) rather than direct synchronous calls.

High-Level Design:

text
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                     Event-Driven Microservices                    │
├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│                                                                   │
│  ┌──────────┐    ┌──────────┐    ┌──────────┐    ┌──────────┐  │
│  │   User   │    │  Order   │    │ Payment  │    │Inventory │  │
│  │ Service  │    │ Service  │    │ Service  │    │ Service  │  │
│  └────┬─────┘    └────┬─────┘    └────┬─────┘    └────┬─────┘  │
│       │               │               │               │          │
│       ▼               ▼               ▼               ▼          │
│  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐    │
│  │              Apache Kafka (Message Broker)              │    │
│  │  ┌────────────┐  ┌────────────┐  ┌────────────┐       │    │
│  │  │ user.events│  │order.events│  │payment.events│      │    │
│  │  │  Topic     │  │   Topic    │  │   Topic     │      │    │
│  │  └────────────┘  └────────────┘  └────────────┘       │    │
│  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘    │
│                                                                   │
│  ┌──────────┐    ┌──────────┐    ┌──────────┐                 │
│  │  Email   │    │Analytics │    │   Saga   │                 │
│  │ Service  │    │ Service  │    │Orchestrator│               │
│  └──────────┘    └──────────┘    └──────────┘                 │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
1. Event Design
Event as a First-Class Citizen
Definition: Events are treated as core domain concepts, not just data carriers. They have their own design, versioning, lifecycle, and governance. Events are explicitly modeled and documented, with clear semantics and contracts.

Step-by-Step Implementation:

java
// Base Event Class - All events extend this
@AllArgsConstructor
@NoArgsConstructor
@Data
public abstract class BaseEvent {
    private String eventId;
    private String eventType;
    private String eventVersion;
    private String correlationId;
    private String causationId;
    private String sourceService;
    private String sourceInstance;
    private Instant timestamp;
    private String schemaVersion;
    private String userId;
    private String tenantId;
    
    // Builder pattern for consistent event creation
    public static abstract class Builder<T extends BaseEvent, B extends Builder<T, B>> {
        protected String eventId = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        protected String correlationId;
        protected String causationId;
        protected String sourceService;
        protected String sourceInstance = System.getenv("HOSTNAME");
        protected Instant timestamp = Instant.now();
        protected String userId;
        protected String tenantId;
        
        @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
        public B correlationId(String correlationId) {
            this.correlationId = correlationId;
            return (B) this;
        }
        
        @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
        public B causationId(String causationId) {
            this.causationId = causationId;
            return (B) this;
        }
        
        @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
        public B sourceService(String sourceService) {
            this.sourceService = sourceService;
            return (B) this;
        }
        
        @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
        public B userId(String userId) {
            this.userId = userId;
            return (B) this;
        }
        
        @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
        public B tenantId(String tenantId) {
            this.tenantId = tenantId;
            return (B) this;
        }
        
        public abstract T build();
    }
}

// Domain Event - Represents something that happened in the domain
@Data
@EqualsAndHashCode(callSuper = true)
public class OrderCreatedEvent extends BaseEvent {
    private static final String EVENT_TYPE = "order.created";
    private static final String EVENT_VERSION = "2.0.0";
    
    private UUID orderId;
    private UUID customerId;
    private List<OrderItem> items;
    private BigDecimal totalAmount;
    private Address shippingAddress;
    private PaymentMethod paymentMethod;
    
    private OrderCreatedEvent() {}
    
    private OrderCreatedEvent(Builder builder) {
        this.eventId = builder.eventId;
        this.eventType = EVENT_TYPE;
        this.eventVersion = EVENT_VERSION;
        this.correlationId = builder.correlationId;
        this.causationId = builder.causationId;
        this.sourceService = builder.sourceService;
        this.sourceInstance = builder.sourceInstance;
        this.timestamp = builder.timestamp;
        this.userId = builder.userId;
        this.tenantId = builder.tenantId;
        
        this.orderId = builder.orderId;
        this.customerId = builder.customerId;
        this.items = builder.items;
        this.totalAmount = builder.totalAmount;
        this.shippingAddress = builder.shippingAddress;
        this.paymentMethod = builder.paymentMethod;
    }
    
    public static Builder builder() {
        return new Builder();
    }
    
    public static class Builder extends BaseEvent.Builder<OrderCreatedEvent, Builder> {
        private UUID orderId;
        private UUID customerId;
        private List<OrderItem> items;
        private BigDecimal totalAmount;
        private Address shippingAddress;
        private PaymentMethod paymentMethod;
        
        public Builder orderId(UUID orderId) {
            this.orderId = orderId;
            return this;
        }
        
        public Builder customerId(UUID customerId) {
            this.customerId = customerId;
            return this;
        }
        
        public Builder items(List<OrderItem> items) {
            this.items = items;
            return this;
        }
        
        public Builder totalAmount(BigDecimal totalAmount) {
            this.totalAmount = totalAmount;
            return this;
        }
        
        public Builder shippingAddress(Address shippingAddress) {
            this.shippingAddress = shippingAddress;
            return this;
        }
        
        public Builder paymentMethod(PaymentMethod paymentMethod) {
            this.paymentMethod = paymentMethod;
            return this;
        }
        
        @Override
        public OrderCreatedEvent build() {
            return new OrderCreatedEvent(this);
        }
        
        @Override
        protected Builder self() {
            return this;
        }
    }
}

// Integration Event - For cross-service communication
@Data
@EqualsAndHashCode(callSuper = true)
public class OrderPaymentProcessedIntegrationEvent extends BaseEvent {
    private static final String EVENT_TYPE = "integration.order.payment.processed";
    private static final String EVENT_VERSION = "1.0.0";
    
    private UUID orderId;
    private UUID paymentId;
    private PaymentStatus paymentStatus;
    private BigDecimal amount;
    private String transactionReference;
    
    public static OrderPaymentProcessedIntegrationEvent from(OrderCreatedEvent orderEvent, 
                                                              PaymentResult payment) {
        return OrderPaymentProcessedIntegrationEvent.builder()
            .correlationId(orderEvent.getCorrelationId())
            .causationId(orderEvent.getEventId())
            .sourceService("payment-service")
            .orderId(orderEvent.getOrderId())
            .paymentId(payment.getPaymentId())
            .paymentStatus(payment.getStatus())
            .amount(orderEvent.getTotalAmount())
            .transactionReference(payment.getTransactionRef())
            .build();
    }
    
    public static Builder builder() {
        return new Builder();
    }
    
    public static class Builder extends BaseEvent.Builder<OrderPaymentProcessedIntegrationEvent, Builder> {
        private UUID orderId;
        private UUID paymentId;
        private PaymentStatus paymentStatus;
        private BigDecimal amount;
        private String transactionReference;
        
        public Builder orderId(UUID orderId) {
            this.orderId = orderId;
            return this;
        }
        
        public Builder paymentId(UUID paymentId) {
            this.paymentId = paymentId;
            return this;
        }
        
        public Builder paymentStatus(PaymentStatus paymentStatus) {
            this.paymentStatus = paymentStatus;
            return this;
        }
        
        public Builder amount(BigDecimal amount) {
            this.amount = amount;
            return this;
        }
        
        public Builder transactionReference(String transactionReference) {
            this.transactionReference = transactionReference;
            return this;
        }
        
        @Override
        public OrderPaymentProcessedIntegrationEvent build() {
            OrderPaymentProcessedIntegrationEvent event = new OrderPaymentProcessedIntegrationEvent();
            event.eventId = this.eventId;
            event.eventType = EVENT_TYPE;
            event.eventVersion = EVENT_VERSION;
            event.correlationId = this.correlationId;
            event.causationId = this.causationId;
            event.sourceService = this.sourceService;
            event.sourceInstance = this.sourceInstance;
            event.timestamp = this.timestamp;
            event.userId = this.userId;
            event.tenantId = this.tenantId;
            event.orderId = this.orderId;
            event.paymentId = this.paymentId;
            event.paymentStatus = this.paymentStatus;
            event.amount = this.amount;
            event.transactionReference = this.transactionReference;
            return event;
        }
        
        @Override
        protected Builder self() {
            return this;
        }
    }
}
Immutable Event Payloads
Definition: Once created, events cannot be modified. If a correction is needed, a new compensating event is created. Immutability ensures event sourcing, replayability, and audit trails are reliable.

java
// Immutable Event Design
@Value  // Lombok makes class immutable (all fields final)
@Builder(toBuilder = true)  // toBuilder allows creating modified copies
@JsonDeserialize(builder = InventoryReservedEvent.InventoryReservedEventBuilder.class)
public class InventoryReservedEvent {
    
    @NonNull
    private final UUID eventId;
    
    @NonNull
    private final String eventType;
    
    @NonNull
    private final String eventVersion;
    
    @NonNull
    private final String correlationId;
    
    @NonNull
    private final UUID orderId;
    
    @NonNull
    private final List<ReservedItem> reservedItems;
    
    @NonNull
    private final Instant reservedAt;
    
    private final String reservationId;
    
    // Immutable nested objects
    @Value
    @Builder
    public static class ReservedItem {
        @NonNull UUID productId;
        @NonNull Integer quantity;
        @NonNull UUID warehouseId;
        LocalDateTime expiryTime;  // Reservation expiry
    }
    
    // Factory method for creating corrected events
    public InventoryReservedEvent withCorrection(String correctionReason) {
        return this.toBuilder()
            .eventId(UUID.randomUUID())
            .eventType("inventory.reserved.corrected")
            .eventVersion(this.eventVersion + "-correction")
            .causationId(this.eventId)
            .build();
    }
}
Domain vs Integration Events
Definition: Domain events represent changes within a service boundary and are used internally. Integration events are published to other services via the message broker, representing cross-boundary communication.

java
// Domain Event - Internal to Order Service
@Value
@Builder
public class OrderStatusChangedDomainEvent {
    UUID orderId;
    OrderStatus oldStatus;
    OrderStatus newStatus;
    Instant changedAt;
    String changedBy;
    String reason;
    
    // This is only used within the Order Service
    // Not published to Kafka
}

// Integration Event - Published to other services
@Value
@Builder
public class OrderShippedIntegrationEvent {
    UUID eventId;
    UUID orderId;
    UUID customerId;
    Address shippingAddress;
    String trackingNumber;
    String carrier;
    Instant shippedAt;
    
    // This is published to Kafka for other services to consume
}

// Service using both
@Service
@Slf4j
public class OrderShippingService {
    
    private final DomainEventPublisher domainEventPublisher;
    private final KafkaTemplate<String, Object> kafkaTemplate;
    private final ApplicationEventPublisher applicationEventPublisher;
    
    @Transactional
    public void shipOrder(UUID orderId, ShippingDetails shippingDetails) {
        Order order = orderRepository.findById(orderId)
            .orElseThrow(() -> new OrderNotFoundException(orderId));
        
        // Update order
        OrderStatus oldStatus = order.getStatus();
        order.setStatus(OrderStatus.SHIPPED);
        order.setTrackingNumber(shippingDetails.getTrackingNumber());
        order.setShippedAt(Instant.now());
        orderRepository.save(order);
        
        // 1. Publish Domain Event (internal, within same service)
        OrderStatusChangedDomainEvent domainEvent = OrderStatusChangedDomainEvent.builder()
            .orderId(orderId)
            .oldStatus(oldStatus)
            .newStatus(OrderStatus.SHIPPED)
            .changedAt(Instant.now())
            .changedBy("shipping-system")
            .reason("Order shipped via " + shippingDetails.getCarrier())
            .build();
        
        // Domain event handled internally
        applicationEventPublisher.publishEvent(domainEvent);
        
        // 2. Publish Integration Event (external, to other services)
        OrderShippedIntegrationEvent integrationEvent = OrderShippedIntegrationEvent.builder()
            .eventId(UUID.randomUUID())
            .orderId(orderId)
            .customerId(order.getCustomerId())
            .shippingAddress(order.getShippingAddress())
            .trackingNumber(shippingDetails.getTrackingNumber())
            .carrier(shippingDetails.getCarrier())
            .shippedAt(Instant.now())
            .build();
        
        // Integration event goes to Kafka
        kafkaTemplate.send("order-events", integrationEvent);
        
        log.info("Order {} shipped. Domain event published, Integration event sent to Kafka", 
                 orderId);
    }
}

// Domain Event Handler (internal)
@Component
@EventListener
@Slf4j
public class OrderDomainEventHandler {
    
    private final OrderAnalyticsService analyticsService;
    
    @Async
    @TransactionalEventListener(phase = TransactionPhase.AFTER_COMMIT)
    public void handleOrderShipped(OrderStatusChangedDomainEvent event) {
        if (event.getNewStatus() == OrderStatus.SHIPPED) {
            // Update internal analytics
            analyticsService.recordOrderShipped(event.getOrderId());
            log.debug("Recorded shipping analytics for order: {}", event.getOrderId());
        }
    }
}
Event Naming: Past-Tense Verbs
Definition: Events should be named using past-tense verbs (e.g., "OrderCreated", "PaymentProcessed") indicating something that has already happened. This makes events immutable facts about the system's history.

java
// Correct - Past tense, clear, descriptive
public class OrderCreatedEvent { }
public class PaymentProcessedEvent { }
public class InventoryReservedEvent { }
public class UserRegisteredEvent { }
public class OrderShippedEvent { }
public class OrderDeliveredEvent { }
public class PaymentRefundedEvent { }

// Incorrect - Present or future tense
public class CreateOrderEvent { }      // Wrong - sounds like a command
public class OrderProcessingEvent { }   // Wrong - ongoing action
public class WillShipOrderEvent { }     // Wrong - future event

// Event naming conventions in code
public enum EventType {
    // Domain Events
    ORDER_CREATED("order.created"),
    ORDER_UPDATED("order.updated"),
    ORDER_CANCELLED("order.cancelled"),
    ORDER_SHIPPED("order.shipped"),
    ORDER_DELIVERED("order.delivered"),
    
    // Payment Events
    PAYMENT_AUTHORIZED("payment.authorized"),
    PAYMENT_CAPTURED("payment.captured"),
    PAYMENT_REFUNDED("payment.refunded"),
    PAYMENT_FAILED("payment.failed"),
    
    // Inventory Events
    INVENTORY_RESERVED("inventory.reserved"),
    INVENTORY_RELEASED("inventory.released"),
    INVENTORY_UPDATED("inventory.updated"),
    STOCK_OUT("stock.out");
    
    private final String value;
    
    EventType(String value) {
        this.value = value;
    }
    
    public String getValue() {
        return value;
    }
}
Event Size < 256 KB Enforced
Definition: Events should be kept small (typically under 256 KB) to ensure efficient processing, prevent network bottlenecks, and avoid Kafka's message size limits.

java
@Component
@Slf4j
public class EventSizeValidator {
    
    private static final int MAX_EVENT_SIZE_BYTES = 256 * 1024;  // 256 KB
    private static final int WARNING_THRESHOLD_BYTES = 100 * 1024;  // 100 KB
    
    private final ObjectMapper objectMapper;
    private final MeterRegistry meterRegistry;
    
    public EventSizeValidator(ObjectMapper objectMapper, MeterRegistry meterRegistry) {
        this.objectMapper = objectMapper;
        this.meterRegistry = meterRegistry;
    }
    
    public <T> void validateEventSize(T event, String eventType) {
        try {
            byte[] serialized = objectMapper.writeValueAsBytes(event);
            int size = serialized.length;
            
            // Record metrics
            meterRegistry.summary("event.size.bytes", 
                "event.type", eventType).record(size);
            
            if (size > MAX_EVENT_SIZE_BYTES) {
                String errorMsg = String.format(
                    "Event %s size %d bytes exceeds maximum allowed size %d bytes",
                    eventType, size, MAX_EVENT_SIZE_BYTES);
                log.error(errorMsg);
                throw new EventTooLargeException(errorMsg);
            }
            
            if (size > WARNING_THRESHOLD_BYTES) {
                log.warn("Event {} is large: {} bytes (threshold: {} bytes)", 
                    eventType, size, WARNING_THRESHOLD_BYTES);
            } else {
                log.debug("Event {} size: {} bytes", eventType, size);
            }
        } catch (JsonProcessingException e) {
            log.error("Failed to serialize event for size check", e);
        }
    }
    
    // Large event handling - store in separate storage
    public <T> void handleLargeEvent(T event, String eventType, byte[] payload) {
        if (payload.length > MAX_EVENT_SIZE_BYTES) {
            // Store large payload in external storage (S3, Redis, etc.)
            String storageKey = "event:" + eventType + ":" + UUID.randomUUID();
            
            // Store in Redis with expiration
            redisTemplate.opsForValue().set(storageKey, payload, Duration.ofDays(7));
            
            // Replace event with reference
            EventReference eventRef = EventReference.builder()
                .eventType(eventType)
                .reference(storageKey)
                .originalSize(payload.length)
                .build();
            
            // Publish reference instead
            kafkaTemplate.send("event-references", eventRef);
        }
    }
}

// Strategy for large event handling
@Component
public class LargeEventStrategy {
    
    // Use event sourcing pattern for large events
    @Service
    public static class OrderWithManyItemsStrategy {
        
        public OrderCreatedEvent createOrderWithManyItems(CreateOrderRequest request) {
            if (request.getItems().size() > 100) {
                // Store items separately
                String itemsStorageKey = storeItemsSeparately(request.getItems());
                
                // Create event with reference
                return OrderCreatedEvent.builder()
                    .orderId(UUID.randomUUID())
                    .customerId(request.getCustomerId())
                    .itemsReference(itemsStorageKey)  // Reference instead of items
                    .totalAmount(calculateTotal(request.getItems()))
                    .itemCount(request.getItems().size())
                    .largeEventFlag(true)
                    .build();
            }
            
            return OrderCreatedEvent.builder()
                .orderId(UUID.randomUUID())
                .customerId(request.getCustomerId())
                .items(request.getItems())  // Items included directly
                .totalAmount(calculateTotal(request.getItems()))
                .build();
        }
        
        private String storeItemsSeparately(List<OrderItem> items) {
            String key = "order-items:" + UUID.randomUUID();
            redisTemplate.opsForValue().set(key, items, Duration.ofDays(7));
            return key;
        }
    }
}

Event Ordering Strategy

Definition: Defines how events are ordered within a stream. Usually based on partition key (e.g., entity ID) to ensure events for a specific entity are processed in order. Critical for maintaining consistency.

java
@Configuration
@Slf4j
public class EventOrderingConfiguration {
    
    // Kafka producer configuration with ordering guarantees
    @Bean
    public ProducerFactory<String, Object> producerFactory() {
        Map<String, Object> config = new HashMap<>();
        config.put(ProducerConfig.BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS_CONFIG, "localhost:9092");
        config.put(ProducerConfig.KEY_SERIALIZER_CLASS_CONFIG, StringSerializer.class);
        config.put(ProducerConfig.VALUE_SERIALIZER_CLASS_CONFIG, JsonSerializer.class);
        
        // Ensure ordering within a partition
        config.put(ProducerConfig.MAX_IN_FLIGHT_REQUESTS_PER_CONNECTION, 1);
        config.put(ProducerConfig.RETRIES_CONFIG, Integer.MAX_VALUE);
        config.put(ProducerConfig.ACKS_CONFIG, "all");
        config.put(ProducerConfig.ENABLE_IDEMPOTENCE_CONFIG, true);
        
        return new DefaultKafkaProducerFactory<>(config);
    }
    
    // Kafka consumer configuration with ordering
    @Bean
    public ConcurrentKafkaListenerContainerFactory<String, Object> kafkaListenerContainerFactory() {
        ConcurrentKafkaListenerContainerFactory<String, Object> factory = 
            new ConcurrentKafkaListenerContainerFactory<>();
        
        Map<String, Object> config = new HashMap<>();
        config.put(ConsumerConfig.BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS_CONFIG, "localhost:9092");
        config.put(ConsumerConfig.GROUP_ID_CONFIG, "order-service-group");
        config.put(ConsumerConfig.KEY_DESERIALIZER_CLASS_CONFIG, StringDeserializer.class);
        config.put(ConsumerConfig.VALUE_DESERIALIZER_CLASS_CONFIG, JsonDeserializer.class);
        
        // Ensure single-threaded consumption per partition for ordering
        config.put(ConsumerConfig.MAX_POLL_RECORDS_CONFIG, 1);
        config.put(ConsumerConfig.FETCH_MAX_BYTES_CONFIG, 1024 * 1024);
        
        factory.setConsumerFactory(new DefaultKafkaConsumerFactory<>(config));
        factory.setConcurrency(1);  // Single thread per partition
        
        return factory;
    }
}

// Producer with ordering key
@Service
@Slf4j
public class OrderedEventProducer {
    
    private final KafkaTemplate<String, Object> kafkaTemplate;
    
    public void publishOrderEvent(OrderEvent event) {
        // Use order ID as partition key to ensure all events for an order
        // go to the same partition and maintain order
        String partitionKey = event.getOrderId().toString();
        
        ListenableFuture<SendResult<String, Object>> future = 
            kafkaTemplate.send("order-events", partitionKey, event);
        
        future.addCallback(
            result -> log.info("Event published to partition {} with offset {}",
                result.getRecordMetadata().partition(),
                result.getRecordMetadata().offset()),
            ex -> log.error("Failed to publish event", ex)
        );
    }
}

// Consumer maintaining order
@Component
@Slf4j
public class OrderedEventConsumer {
    
    private final ConcurrentHashMap<UUID, OrderState> orderStates = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    
    @KafkaListener(topics = "order-events", groupId = "order-processor")
    public void consumeOrderEvent(ConsumerRecord<String, Object> record) {
        UUID orderId = UUID.fromString(record.key());
        OrderEvent event = (OrderEvent) record.value();
        
        log.info("Processing event {} for order {} at offset {}", 
            event.getEventType(), orderId, record.offset());
        
        // Get or create state for this order
        OrderState state = orderStates.computeIfAbsent(orderId, 
            k -> new OrderState(orderId, record.offset()));
        
        // Check if we've already processed this offset
        if (state.getLastProcessedOffset() >= record.offset()) {
            log.info("Skipping already processed offset {} for order {}", 
                record.offset(), orderId);
            return;
        }
        
        // Process event in order
        try {
            processEventInOrder(event, state);
            state.setLastProcessedOffset(record.offset());
            
        } catch (OutOfOrderEventException e) {
            log.error("Out of order event detected for order {}: expected {} but got {}", 
                orderId, state.getExpectedSequence(), event.getSequence());
            // Handle out-of-order event (maybe store and wait)
            storeOutOfOrderEvent(orderId, event);
        }
    }
    
    @Data
    private static class OrderState {
        private final UUID orderId;
        private long lastProcessedOffset;
        private long expectedSequence;
        
        public OrderState(UUID orderId, long initialOffset) {
            this.orderId = orderId;
            this.lastProcessedOffset = initialOffset;
            this.expectedSequence = 1;
        }
    }
}

2. Schema & Contracts

Schema Registry
Definition: A centralized repository that stores and manages event schemas. Ensures compatibility between producers and consumers, prevents breaking changes, and provides schema evolution capabilities.

yaml
# docker-compose.yml for Schema Registry

      ZOOKEEPER_CLIENT_PORT: 2181
      ZOOKEEPER_TICK_TIME: 2000

      - 2181:2181

  kafka:
    image: confluentinc/cp-kafka:latest

      - zookeeper
    ports:
      - 9092:9092

      KAFKA_BROKER_ID: 1
      KAFKA_ZOOKEEPER_CONNECT: zookeeper:2181
      KAFKA_ADVERTISED_LISTENERS: PLAINTEXT://localhost:9092

@Configuration
@Slf4j
public class SchemaRegistryConfig {
    
    @Value("${spring.kafka.bootstrap-servers}")
    private String bootstrapServers;
    
    @Value("${spring.kafka.schema-registry-url}")
    private String schemaRegistryUrl;
    
    @Bean
    public ProducerFactory<String, Object> avroProducerFactory() {
        Map<String, Object> config = new HashMap<>();
        config.put(ProducerConfig.BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS_CONFIG, bootstrapServers);
        config.put(ProducerConfig.KEY_SERIALIZER_CLASS_CONFIG, StringSerializer.class);
        config.put(ProducerConfig.VALUE_SERIALIZER_CLASS_CONFIG, KafkaAvroSerializer.class);
        config.put(KafkaAvroSerializerConfig.SCHEMA_REGISTRY_URL_CONFIG, schemaRegistryUrl);
        config.put(KafkaAvroSerializerConfig.AUTO_REGISTER_SCHEMAS, true);
        
        // Compatibility settings
        config.put(KafkaAvroSerializerConfig.AUTO_REGISTER_SCHEMAS, true);
        config.put(KafkaAvroSerializerConfig.USE_LATEST_VERSION, false);
        
        return new DefaultKafkaProducerFactory<>(config);
    }
    
    @Bean
    public ConsumerFactory<String, Object> avroConsumerFactory() {
        Map<String, Object> config = new HashMap<>();
        config.put(ConsumerConfig.BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS_CONFIG, bootstrapServers);
        config.put(ConsumerConfig.GROUP_ID_CONFIG, "order-service-group");
        config.put(ConsumerConfig.KEY_DESERIALIZER_CLASS_CONFIG, StringDeserializer.class);
        config.put(ConsumerConfig.VALUE_DESERIALIZER_CLASS_CONFIG, KafkaAvroDeserializer.class);
        config.put(KafkaAvroDeserializerConfig.SCHEMA_REGISTRY_URL_CONFIG, schemaRegistryUrl);
        config.put(KafkaAvroDeserializerConfig.SPECIFIC_AVRO_READER_CONFIG, true);
        
        // Schema compatibility enforcement
        config.put(KafkaAvroDeserializerConfig.SCHEMA_COMPATIBILITY, "BACKWARD");
        
        return new DefaultKafkaConsumerFactory<>(config);
    }
}

// Producer using Avro schema
@Service
@Slf4j
public class AvroEventProducer {
    
    private final KafkaTemplate<String, Object> kafkaTemplate;
    
    public void publishOrderCreated(OrderCreatedEventAvro event) {
        log.info("Publishing OrderCreated event for order: {}", event.getOrderId());
        
        kafkaTemplate.send("order-events", event.getOrderId().toString(), event)
            .addCallback(
                result -> log.info("Event published to partition {} at offset {}",
                    result.getRecordMetadata().partition(),
                    result.getRecordMetadata().offset()),
                ex -> log.error("Failed to publish event", ex)
            );
    }
}

        log.info("Received event: Order {} created at {} with total ${}",
            event.getOrderId(),
            Instant.ofEpochMilli(event.getCreatedAt()),
            event.getTotalAmount());
        
        // Process event
        processOrderCreated(event);
    }

Schema Versioning Strategy

Definition: Strategy for managing multiple versions of schemas to support backward and forward compatibility, allowing producers and consumers to evolve independently.

java
@Component
@Slf4j
public class SchemaVersioningService {
    
    // Schema compatibility matrix
    private static final Map<String, Map<Integer, Class<?>>> SCHEMA_VERSIONS = Map.of(
        "OrderCreatedEvent", Map.of(
            1, OrderCreatedEventV1.class,
            2, OrderCreatedEventV2.class,
            3, OrderCreatedEventV3.class
        ),
        "PaymentProcessedEvent", Map.of(
            1, PaymentProcessedEventV1.class,
            2, PaymentProcessedEventV2.class
        )
    );
    
    @Value("${event.schema.default-version:latest}")
    private String defaultVersionStrategy;
    
    // Producer: Always use latest schema version
    public <T> T createEventWithVersion(String eventType, int version, Object data) {
        Class<T> eventClass = (Class<T>) SCHEMA_VERSIONS.get(eventType).get(version);
        if (eventClass == null) {
            throw new SchemaNotFoundException("Version " + version + " not found for " + eventType);
        }
        
        log.info("Creating event {} version {}", eventType, version);
        return mapToEvent(eventClass, data);
    }
    
    // Consumer: Handle multiple versions
    @KafkaListener(topics = "order-events")
    public void consumeEventWithVersioning(ConsumerRecord<String, byte[]> record) {
        String eventType = getEventType(record);
        int version = getSchemaVersion(record);
        
        switch (eventType) {
            case "OrderCreatedEvent":
                handleOrderCreatedEvent(record, version);
                break;
            case "PaymentProcessedEvent":
                handlePaymentProcessedEvent(record, version);
                break;
        }
    }
    
    private void handleOrderCreatedEvent(ConsumerRecord<String, byte[]> record, int version) {
        try {
            switch (version) {
                case 1:
                    OrderCreatedEventV1 v1 = deserialize(record.value(), OrderCreatedEventV1.class);
                    processV1(v1);
                    break;
                case 2:
                    OrderCreatedEventV2 v2 = deserialize(record.value(), OrderCreatedEventV2.class);
                    processV2(v2);
                    break;
                case 3:
                    OrderCreatedEventV3 v3 = deserialize(record.value(), OrderCreatedEventV3.class);
                    processV3(v3);
                    break;
                default:
                    log.warn("Unsupported version {} for OrderCreatedEvent", version);
                    // Use latest version for processing
                    processLatestVersion(record.value());
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("Failed to process event version {}", version, e);
        }
    }
    
    // Forward compatibility: Add new fields with defaults
    @Data
    public static class OrderCreatedEventV1 {
        private UUID orderId;
        private UUID customerId;
        private List<OrderItem> items;
        private BigDecimal totalAmount;
        private Instant createdAt;
        // Missing: shippingAddress, paymentMethod
    }
    
    @Data
    public static class OrderCreatedEventV2 extends OrderCreatedEventV1 {
        private Address shippingAddress;  // New field added
        // Backward compatible - old consumers ignore this field
    }
    
    @Data
    public static class OrderCreatedEventV3 extends OrderCreatedEventV2 {
        private PaymentMethod paymentMethod;  // Another new field
        private String promotionCode;  // Optional field
        // All old consumers can still read this if they ignore new fields
    }
}

// Schema compatibility checker
@Component
@Slf4j
public class SchemaCompatibilityChecker {
    
    public enum CompatibilityType {
        BACKWARD,   // New consumers can read old data
        FORWARD,    // Old consumers can read new data
        FULL,       // Both directions
        NONE        // No compatibility
    }
    
    public boolean checkCompatibility(Class<?> oldSchema, Class<?> newSchema, CompatibilityType type) {
        switch (type) {
            case BACKWARD:
                return isBackwardCompatible(oldSchema, newSchema);
            case FORWARD:
                return isForwardCompatible(oldSchema, newSchema);
            case FULL:
                return isBackwardCompatible(oldSchema, newSchema) 
                    && isForwardCompatible(oldSchema, newSchema);
            default:
                return true;
        }
    }
    
    private boolean isBackwardCompatible(Class<?> oldSchema, Class<?> newSchema) {
        // Check 1: No removed required fields
        Set<String> oldRequiredFields = getRequiredFields(oldSchema);
        Set<String> newRequiredFields = getRequiredFields(newSchema);
        
        for (String requiredField : newRequiredFields) {
            if (!oldRequiredFields.contains(requiredField)) {
                log.warn("Backward incompatible: New required field '{}' not in old schema", 
                    requiredField);
                return false;
            }
        }
        
        // Check 2: No type changes that break compatibility
        for (Field field : newSchema.getDeclaredFields()) {
            Field oldField = getField(oldSchema, field.getName());
            if (oldField != null && !isCompatibleType(oldField.getType(), field.getType())) {
                log.warn("Backward incompatible: Type changed for field '{}'", field.getName());
                return false;
            }
        }
        
        return true;
    }
    
    private boolean isForwardCompatible(Class<?> oldSchema, Class<?> newSchema) {
        // Check: Old consumers can ignore new fields
        // Forward compatibility is generally easier
        return true;
    }
}

Consumer-Driven Contract Tests

Definition: Tests where consumers define the expected contract (events they need), ensuring producers don't break consumers. Using tools like Pact to verify contracts.

java
// Pact Consumer Test (Order Service consumer of Payment Service events)
@ExtendWith(PactConsumerTestExt.class)
@PactTestFor(providerName = "PaymentService", port = "8080")
public class PaymentEventConsumerPactTest {
    
    @Pact(consumer = "OrderService")
    public MessagePact paymentProcessedEventPact(MessagePactBuilder builder) {
        Map<String, String> metadata = Map.of(
            "contentType", "application/json"
        );
        
        Map<String, Object> event = Map.of(
            "eventId", "123e4567-e89b-12d3-a456-426614174000",
            "orderId", "123e4567-e89b-12d3-a456-426614174001",
            "paymentId", "123e4567-e89b-12d3-a456-426614174002",
            "status", "SUCCESS",
            "amount", 99.99,
            "processedAt", "2026-03-28T10:00:00Z"
        );
        
        return builder
            .expectsToReceive("Payment processed event")
            .withMetadata(metadata)
            .withContent(JsonLikeMatcher.stringToJson(event))
            .toPact();
    }
    
    @Test
    @PactTestFor(pactMethod = "paymentProcessedEventPact")
    public void shouldHandlePaymentProcessedEvent(List<Message> messages) {
        // Verify that our consumer can handle the event as expected
        for (Message message : messages) {
            Map<String, Object> event = message.contentsAsMap();
            
            // Process the event
            PaymentProcessedEvent paymentEvent = PaymentProcessedEvent.builder()
                .eventId(UUID.fromString((String) event.get("eventId")))
                .orderId(UUID.fromString((String) event.get("orderId")))
                .paymentId(UUID.fromString((String) event.get("paymentId")))
                .status(PaymentStatus.valueOf((String) event.get("status")))
                .amount(BigDecimal.valueOf((Double) event.get("amount")))
                .processedAt(Instant.parse((String) event.get("processedAt")))
                .build();
            
            // Verify we can process it
            assertDoesNotThrow(() -> orderService.handlePaymentProcessed(paymentEvent));
        }
    }
}

// Pact Provider Test (Payment Service)
@Provider("PaymentService")
@PactBroker(scheme = "https", host = "pact-broker", port = "443")
public class PaymentEventProviderPactTest {
    
    @TestTemplate
    @ExtendWith(PactVerificationInvocationContextProvider.class)
    void pactVerificationTestTemplate(PactVerificationContext context) {
        context.verifyInteraction();
    }
    
    @BeforeEach
    void before(PactVerificationContext context) {
        context.setTarget(new HttpTestTarget("localhost", 8080, "/"));
    }
    
    @State("Payment processed event")
    public void paymentProcessedEventState() {
        // Setup the state required for the contract
        // This prepares the provider to generate the expected event
        whenPaymentIsProcessed();
    }
    
    @State("Payment failed event")
    public void paymentFailedEventState() {
        // Setup for failed payment scenario
        whenPaymentFails();
    }
}

3. Security

mTLS between Producers & Brokers
Definition: Mutual TLS (Transport Layer Security) where both client and server authenticate each other using certificates, ensuring secure communication between services and Kafka brokers.

yaml
# Docker Compose with Kafka SSL configuration
version: '3'
services:
  kafka:
    image: confluentinc/cp-kafka:latest
    environment:
      KAFKA_SSL_KEYSTORE_FILENAME: kafka.keystore.jks
      KAFKA_SSL_KEYSTORE_CREDENTIALS: keystore_creds
      KAFKA_SSL_KEY_CREDENTIALS: key_creds
      KAFKA_SSL_TRUSTSTORE_FILENAME: kafka.truststore.jks
      KAFKA_SSL_TRUSTSTORE_CREDENTIALS: truststore_creds
      KAFKA_SSL_CLIENT_AUTH: required
      KAFKA_SSL_ENABLED_PROTOCOLS: TLSv1.2,TLSv1.3
      KAFKA_SSL_PROTOCOL: TLS
      KAFKA_LISTENERS: SSL://0.0.0.0:9093
      KAFKA_ADVERTISED_LISTENERS: SSL://kafka:9093
java
@Configuration
@Slf4j
public class KafkaSecurityConfig {
    
    @Value("${kafka.ssl.keystore.location}")
    private String keystoreLocation;
    
    @Value("${kafka.ssl.keystore.password}")
    private String keystorePassword;
    
    @Value("${kafka.ssl.truststore.location}")
    private String truststoreLocation;
    
    @Value("${kafka.ssl.truststore.password}")
    private String truststorePassword;
    
    @Bean
    public ProducerFactory<String, Object> secureProducerFactory() {
        Map<String, Object> config = new HashMap<>();
        config.put(ProducerConfig.BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS_CONFIG, "kafka:9093");
        config.put(ProducerConfig.KEY_SERIALIZER_CLASS_CONFIG, StringSerializer.class);
        config.put(ProducerConfig.VALUE_SERIALIZER_CLASS_CONFIG, JsonSerializer.class);
        
        // SSL Configuration
        config.put("security.protocol", "SSL");
        config.put("ssl.keystore.location", keystoreLocation);
        config.put("ssl.keystore.password", keystorePassword);
        config.put("ssl.truststore.location", truststoreLocation);
        config.put("ssl.truststore.password", truststorePassword);
        config.put("ssl.key.password", keystorePassword);
        config.put("ssl.endpoint.identification.algorithm", "https");
        
        return new DefaultKafkaProducerFactory<>(config);
    }
    
    @Bean
    public ConsumerFactory<String, Object> secureConsumerFactory() {
        Map<String, Object> config = new HashMap<>();
        config.put(ConsumerConfig.BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS_CONFIG, "kafka:9093");
        config.put(ConsumerConfig.GROUP_ID_CONFIG, "secure-consumer-group");
        config.put(ConsumerConfig.KEY_DESERIALIZER_CLASS_CONFIG, StringDeserializer.class);
        config.put(ConsumerConfig.VALUE_DESERIALIZER_CLASS_CONFIG, JsonDeserializer.class);
        
        // SSL Configuration
        config.put("security.protocol", "SSL");
        config.put("ssl.keystore.location", keystoreLocation);
        config.put("ssl.keystore.password", keystorePassword);
        config.put("ssl.truststore.location", truststoreLocation);
        config.put("ssl.truststore.password", truststorePassword);
        
        return new DefaultKafkaConsumerFactory<>(config);
    }
}

// Certificate generation script
// generate-certificates.sh
#!/bin/bash
# Generate CA key
openssl req -new -x509 -keyout ca-key -out ca-cert -days 365 -subj "/CN=Kafka-CA"

# Generate keystore for Kafka broker
keytool -keystore kafka.keystore.jks -alias localhost -validity 365 -genkey -keyalg RSA \
  -dname "CN=kafka, OU=Dev, O=Company, L=City, S=State, C=US"

# Generate certificate signing request
keytool -keystore kafka.keystore.jks -alias localhost -certreq -file cert-file

# Sign the certificate with CA
openssl x509 -req -CA ca-cert -CAkey ca-key -in cert-file -out cert-signed -days 365 -CAcreateserial

# Import CA certificate to truststore
keytool -keystore kafka.truststore.jks -alias CARoot -import -file ca-cert

# Import signed certificate to keystore
keytool -keystore kafka.keystore.jks -alias localhost -import -file cert-signed
RBAC per Topic
Definition: Role-Based Access Control enforced on Kafka topics, ensuring only authorized services can produce to or consume from specific topics.

yaml
# Kafka ACL Configuration
apiVersion: v1
kind: ConfigMap
metadata:
  name: kafka-acls
data:
  acls.sh: |
    #!/bin/bash
    
    # Order Service permissions
    kafka-acls --authorizer-properties zookeeper.connect=zookeeper:2181 \
      --add --allow-principal User:order-service \
      --operation Write --topic order-events
    
    kafka-acls --authorizer-properties zookeeper.connect=zookeeper:2181 \
      --add --allow-principal User:order-service \
      --operation Read --topic payment-events \
      --group order-service-group
    
    # Payment Service permissions
    kafka-acls --authorizer-properties zookeeper.connect=zookeeper:2181 \
      --add --allow-principal User:payment-service \
      --operation Write --topic payment-events
    
    kafka-acls --authorizer-properties zookeeper.connect=zookeeper:2181 \
      --add --allow-principal User:payment-service \
      --operation Read --topic order-events \
      --group payment-service-group
    
    # Inventory Service permissions
    kafka-acls --authorizer-properties zookeeper.connect=zookeeper:2181 \
      --add --allow-principal User:inventory-service \
      --operation Write --topic inventory-events
    
    kafka-acls --authorizer-properties zookeeper.connect=zookeeper:2181 \
      --add --allow-principal User:inventory-service \
      --operation Read --topic order-events \
      --group inventory-service-group
java
@Component
@Slf4j
public class KafkaAuthorizationService {
    
    private final AdminClient adminClient;
    
    public KafkaAuthorizationService(@Value("${spring.kafka.bootstrap-servers}") 
                                      String bootstrapServers) {
        Properties props = new Properties();
        props.put(AdminClientConfig.BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS_CONFIG, bootstrapServers);
        this.adminClient = AdminClient.create(props);
    }
    
    public void createTopicWithAcls(String topicName, String producerService, 
                                     List<String> consumerServices) {
        // Create topic
        NewTopic newTopic = new NewTopic(topicName, 3, (short) 3);
        adminClient.createTopics(List.of(newTopic));
        
        // Add ACLs for producer
        addAcl(topicName, producerService, AclOperation.WRITE);
        
        // Add ACLs for consumers
        consumerServices.forEach(consumer -> 
            addAcl(topicName, consumer, AclOperation.READ));
        
        log.info("Created topic {} with ACLs", topicName);
    }
    
    private void addAcl(String topicName, String serviceName, AclOperation operation) {
        // Implementation using Kafka Admin API
        AclBinding aclBinding = new AclBinding(
            new ResourcePattern(ResourceType.TOPIC, topicName, PatternType.LITERAL),
            new AccessControlEntry(serviceName, "*", operation, AclPermissionType.ALLOW)
        );
        
        adminClient.createAcls(List.of(aclBinding));
        log.info("Added ACL: {} can {} to topic {}", serviceName, operation, topicName);
    }
    
    // Service-specific producer configuration
    @Bean
    @Qualifier("orderEventProducer")
    public KafkaTemplate<String, Object> orderEventProducer() {
        Map<String, Object> config = new HashMap<>();
        config.put(ProducerConfig.BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS_CONFIG, "kafka:9093");
        config.put("security.protocol", "SSL");
        config.put("ssl.keystore.location", "/certs/order-service.keystore.jks");
        config.put("ssl.keystore.password", "${ORDER_KEYSTORE_PASSWORD}");
        
        // Service principal
        config.put(ProducerConfig.CLIENT_ID_CONFIG, "order-service");
        
        return new KafkaTemplate<>(new DefaultKafkaProducerFactory<>(config));
    }
}

PII Masking in Event Payload

Definition: Automatically mask Personally Identifiable Information (PII) in event payloads to comply with data privacy regulations (GDPR, CCPA) and prevent sensitive data leakage.

java
@Component
@Slf4j
public class PIIMaskingService {
    
    private final ObjectMapper objectMapper;
    
    // PII fields that need masking
    private static final Set<String> PII_FIELDS = Set.of(
        "email", "emailAddress", "phone", "phoneNumber", "ssn", "socialSecurity",
        "creditCard", "cardNumber", "passport", "driverLicense"
    );
    
    // Sensitive fields that need full masking
    private static final Set<String> SENSITIVE_FIELDS = Set.of(
        "password", "secret", "token", "apiKey"
    );
    
    @PostConstruct
    public void configureObjectMapper() {
        objectMapper.addMixIn(Object.class, PIIClassMixin.class);
    }
    
    public <T> T maskPII(T event) {
        try {
            // Convert to JSON
            String json = objectMapper.writeValueAsString(event);
            
            // Mask PII fields
            String maskedJson = maskPIIFields(json);
            
            // Convert back to object
            return (T) objectMapper.readValue(maskedJson, event.getClass());
            
        } catch (JsonProcessingException e) {
            log.error("Failed to mask PII in event", e);
            return event;  // Return original if masking fails
        }
    }
    
    private String maskPIIFields(String json) {
        JsonNode root = objectMapper.readTree(json);
        maskRecursive(root);
        return objectMapper.writeValueAsString(root);
    }
    
    private void maskRecursive(JsonNode node) {
        if (node.isObject()) {
            node.fields().forEachRemaining(entry -> {
                String fieldName = entry.getKey();
                JsonNode value = entry.getValue();
                
                // Check if field contains PII
                if (isPIIName(fieldName)) {
                    maskValue(node, fieldName, value);
                } else if (value.isObject()) {
                    maskRecursive(value);
                } else if (value.isArray()) {
                    value.forEach(this::maskRecursive);
                }
            });
        }
    }
    
    private void maskValue(JsonNode parent, String fieldName, JsonNode value) {
        ObjectNode objectNode = (ObjectNode) parent;
        
        if (isSensitiveField(fieldName)) {
            // Fully mask sensitive fields
            objectNode.put(fieldName, "******");
        } else if (value.isTextual()) {
            // Partially mask PII (show first and last character)
            String text = value.asText();
            if (text.length() > 2) {
                String masked = text.charAt(0) + 
                    "*".repeat(text.length() - 2) + 
                    text.charAt(text.length() - 1);
                objectNode.put(fieldName, masked);
            } else {
                objectNode.put(fieldName, "**");
            }
        } else if (value.isNumber()) {
            // Mask numeric PII (credit cards, phones)
            objectNode.put(fieldName, "****");
        }
    }
    
    private boolean isPIIName(String fieldName) {
        String lowerField = fieldName.toLowerCase();
        return PII_FIELDS.stream().anyMatch(lowerField::contains);
    }
    
    private boolean isSensitiveField(String fieldName) {
        String lowerField = fieldName.toLowerCase();
        return SENSITIVE_FIELDS.stream().anyMatch(lowerField::contains);
    }
    
    // Annotate fields for automatic masking
    @Retention(RetentionPolicy.RUNTIME)
    @Target(ElementType.FIELD)
    public static @interface PII {
        MaskingType value() default MaskingType.PARTIAL;
    }
    
    public enum MaskingType {
        PARTIAL,    // Show only first and last character
        FULL,       // Completely mask
        EMAIL,      // Special masking for emails (user@domain.com -> u***@d****.com)
        CREDIT_CARD // Mask all but last 4 digits
    }
    
    // Custom serializer for PII fields
    public static class PIIMaskingSerializer extends StdSerializer<Object> {
        
        public PIIMaskingSerializer() {
            super(Object.class);
        }
        
        @Override
        public void serialize(Object value, JsonGenerator gen, SerializerProvider provider) 
                throws IOException {
            if (value == null) {
