/**
 * Token Bucket Rate Limiter using Stream API
 */
class TokenBucketRateLimiter {
    
    private final int maxTokens;
    private final long refillIntervalMillis;
    private final AtomicDouble availableTokens;
    private final AtomicLong lastRefillTimestamp;
    private final ConcurrentLinkedQueue<Long> requestQueue;
    
    public TokenBucketRateLimiter(int maxRequests, long timeWindowSeconds) {
        this.maxTokens = maxRequests;
        this.refillIntervalMillis = timeWindowSeconds * 1000 / maxRequests;
        this.availableTokens = new AtomicDouble(maxRequests);
        this.lastRefillTimestamp = new AtomicLong(System.currentTimeMillis());
        this.requestQueue = new ConcurrentLinkedQueue<>();
        
        // Start token refill scheduler
        startTokenRefiller();
    }
    
    /**
     * Try to acquire token using atomic operations with Stream validation
     */
    public boolean tryAcquire() {
        long currentTime = System.currentTimeMillis();
        
        // Refill tokens based on elapsed time
        refillTokens(currentTime);
        
        // Try to acquire a token
        double currentTokens = availableTokens.get();
        if (currentTokens >= 1.0) {
            if (availableTokens.compareAndSet(currentTokens, currentTokens - 1.0)) {
                requestQueue.add(currentTime);
                return true;
            }
        }
        
        return false;
    }
    
    /**
     * Refill tokens using Stream-based calculation
     */
    private void refillTokens(long currentTime) {
        long lastRefill = lastRefillTimestamp.get();
        long elapsedTime = currentTime - lastRefill;
        
        if (elapsedTime > 0) {
            double tokensToAdd = (double) elapsedTime / refillIntervalMillis;
            double newTokens = Math.min(maxTokens, availableTokens.get() + tokensToAdd);
            
            if (availableTokens.compareAndSet(availableTokens.get(), newTokens)) {
                lastRefillTimestamp.compareAndSet(lastRefill, currentTime);
            }
        }
    }
    
    /**
     * Get token usage pattern using Stream API
     */
    public TokenUsagePattern getTokenUsagePattern() {
        long currentTime = System.currentTimeMillis();
        long windowStart = currentTime - (refillIntervalMillis * maxTokens);
        
        // Analyze request pattern using Stream API
        Map<Integer, Long> timeSlots = requestQueue.stream()
            .filter(timestamp -> timestamp > windowStart)
            .collect(Collectors.groupingBy(
                timestamp -> {
                    long slotIndex = (timestamp - windowStart) / refillIntervalMillis;
                    return (int) Math.min(slotIndex, maxTokens - 1);
                },
                Collectors.counting()
            ));
        
        return new TokenUsagePattern(
            availableTokens.get(),
            requestQueue.size(),
            timeSlots
        );
    }
}

/**
 * Distributed Rate Limiter with Redis and Streams (Bonus)
 */
class DistributedRateLimiter {
    
    private final RedisTemplate<String, String> redisTemplate;
    private final String keyPrefix;
    private final int maxRequests;
    private final long timeWindowMillis;
    private final ObjectMapper objectMapper;
    
    public DistributedRateLimiter(RedisTemplate<String, String> redisTemplate,
                                  String keyPrefix,
                                  int maxRequests,
                                  long timeWindowSeconds) {
        this.redisTemplate = redisTemplate;
        this.keyPrefix = keyPrefix;
        this.maxRequests = maxRequests;
        this.timeWindowMillis = timeWindowSeconds * 1000;
        this.objectMapper = new ObjectMapper();
    }
    
    /**
     * Lua script for atomic rate limiting
     */
    private static final String LUA_SCRIPT = 
        "local key = KEYS[1]\n" +
        "local max_requests = tonumber(ARGV[1])\n" +
        "local window_ms = tonumber(ARGV[2])\n" +
        "local current_time = tonumber(ARGV[3])\n" +
        "local window_start = current_time - window_ms\n" +
        "\n" +
        "-- Remove old entries\n" +
        "redis.call('ZREMRANGEBYSCORE', key, 0, window_start)\n" +
        "\n" +
        "-- Count current requests\n" +
        "local current_count = redis.call('ZCARD', key)\n" +
        "\n" +
        "if current_count < max_requests then\n" +
        "    redis.call('ZADD', key, current_time, current_time)\n" +
        "    redis.call('EXPIRE', key, window_ms / 1000)\n" +
        "    return {1, max_requests - current_count - 1, 0}\n" +
        "else\n" +
        "    -- Get oldest request timestamp\n" +
        "    local oldest = redis.call('ZRANGE', key, 0, 0, 'WITHSCORES')\n" +
        "    local retry_after = (oldest[2] + window_ms) - current_time\n" +
        "    return {0, 0, retry_after}\n" +
        "end";
    
    private final DefaultRedisScript<List<Long>> rateLimiterScript;
    
    /**
     * Thread-safe distributed rate limiting using Redis and Lua
     */
    public RateLimitResult tryAcquire(String clientId) {
        String key = keyPrefix + ":" + clientId;
        long currentTime = System.currentTimeMillis();
        
        List<Long> result = redisTemplate.execute(
            rateLimiterScript,
            Collections.singletonList(key),
            String.valueOf(maxRequests),
            String.valueOf(timeWindowMillis),
            String.valueOf(currentTime)
        );
        
        boolean allowed = result.get(0) == 1;
        long remaining = result.get(1);
        long retryAfter = result.get(2);
        
        if (allowed) {
            return RateLimitResult.success(remaining, maxRequests, retryAfter);
        } else {
            return RateLimitResult.failure(maxRequests - remaining, maxRequests, retryAfter);
        }
    }
    
    /**
     * Get analytics using Stream API on Redis sorted sets
     */
    public RateLimitAnalytics getAnalytics(String clientId) {
        String key = keyPrefix + ":" + clientId;
        long currentTime = System.currentTimeMillis();
        long windowStart = currentTime - timeWindowMillis;
        
        // Use Redis commands with Java Streams for analysis
        Set<Long> timestamps = redisTemplate.opsForZSet()
            .rangeByScore(key, windowStart, currentTime)
            .stream()
            .map(Long::parseLong)
            .collect(Collectors.toSet());
        
        // Analyze using Stream API
        Map<Integer, Long> minuteDistribution = timestamps.stream()
            .collect(Collectors.groupingBy(
                timestamp -> (int) ((timestamp - windowStart) / 60000),
                Collectors.counting()
            ));
        
        OptionalDouble averageInterval = timestamps.stream()
            .sorted()
            .reduce((prev, curr) -> {
                // This is a placeholder - actual implementation would need
                // to calculate intervals between consecutive timestamps
                return curr;
            })
            .stream()
            .mapToLong(t -> t)
            .average();
        
        return new RateLimitAnalytics(
            timestamps.size(),
            maxRequests - timestamps.size(),
            minuteDistribution,
            averageInterval.orElse(0)
        );
    }
}

/**
 * Supporting classes for rate limiter results
 */
class RateLimitResult {
    private final boolean allowed;
    private final long currentCount;
    private final long maxRequests;
    private final long retryAfterMillis;
    private final Instant timestamp;
    
    private RateLimitResult(boolean allowed, long currentCount, long maxRequests, long retryAfterMillis) {
        this.allowed = allowed;
        this.currentCount = currentCount;
        this.maxRequests = maxRequests;
        this.retryAfterMillis = retryAfterMillis;
        this.timestamp = Instant.now();
    }
    
    public static RateLimitResult success(long currentCount, long maxRequests, long retryAfter) {
        return new RateLimitResult(true, currentCount, maxRequests, retryAfter);
    }
    
    public static RateLimitResult failure(long currentCount, long maxRequests, long retryAfter) {
        return new RateLimitResult(false, currentCount, maxRequests, retryAfter);
    }
    
    public boolean isAllowed() { return allowed; }
    public long getCurrentCount() { return currentCount; }
    public long getMaxRequests() { return maxRequests; }
    public long getRetryAfterMillis() { return retryAfterMillis; }
    public Instant getTimestamp() { return timestamp; }
    
    public Map<String, Object> toHeaders() {
        Map<String, Object> headers = new HashMap<>();
        headers.put("X-RateLimit-Limit", maxRequests);
        headers.put("X-RateLimit-Remaining", allowed ? maxRequests - currentCount : 0);
        headers.put("X-RateLimit-Reset", retryAfterMillis);
        return headers;
    }
    
    @Override
    public String toString() {
        return String.format("RateLimitResult{allowed=%s, current=%d/%d, retryAfter=%dms}",
            allowed, currentCount, maxRequests, retryAfterMillis);
    }
}

/**
 * Statistics and metrics classes
 */
class WindowStatistics {
    private final long count;
    private final long minTimestamp;
    private final long maxTimestamp;
    private final double avgTimestamp;
    private final Map<Integer, Long> distribution;
    
    public WindowStatistics(long count, long minTimestamp, long maxTimestamp, double avgTimestamp) {
        this(count, minTimestamp, maxTimestamp, avgTimestamp, new HashMap<>());
    }
    
    public WindowStatistics(long count, long minTimestamp, long maxTimestamp, 
                           double avgTimestamp, Map<Integer, Long> distribution) {
        this.count = count;
        this.minTimestamp = minTimestamp;
        this.maxTimestamp = maxTimestamp;
        this.avgTimestamp = avgTimestamp;
        this.distribution = distribution;
    }
    
    public double getFillPercentage() {
        return (double) count / maxRequests * 100;
    }
    
    // Getters
    public long getCount() { return count; }
    public long getMinTimestamp() { return minTimestamp; }
    public long getMaxTimestamp() { return maxTimestamp; }
    public double getAvgTimestamp() { return avgTimestamp; }
    public Map<Integer, Long> getDistribution() { return distribution; }
}

class TokenUsagePattern {
    private final double availableTokens;
    private final int queueSize;
    private final Map<Integer, Long> timeSlots;
    
    public TokenUsagePattern(double availableTokens, int queueSize, Map<Integer, Long> timeSlots) {
        this.availableTokens = availableTokens;
        this.queueSize = queueSize;
        this.timeSlots = timeSlots;
    }
    
    public boolean isBursty() {
        // Detect bursty patterns using Stream API
        OptionalDouble average = timeSlots.values().stream()
            .mapToLong(Long::longValue)
            .average();
        
        return average.isPresent() && 
               timeSlots.values().stream()
                   .anyMatch(count -> count > average.getAsDouble() * 2);
    }
    
    // Getters
    public double getAvailableTokens() { return availableTokens; }
    public int getQueueSize() { return queueSize; }
    public Map<Integer, Long> getTimeSlots() { return timeSlots; }
}

class RateLimitMetrics {
    private final long totalRequests;
    private final long rejectedRequests;
    private final long currentRequests;
    private final Map<String, Long> clientMetrics;
    
    public RateLimitMetrics(long totalRequests, long rejectedRequests, 
                           long currentRequests, Map<String, Long> clientMetrics) {
        this.totalRequests = totalRequests;
        this.rejectedRequests = rejectedRequests;
        this.currentRequests = currentRequests;
        this.clientMetrics = clientMetrics;
    }
    
    public double getRejectionRate() {
        return totalRequests > 0 ? (double) rejectedRequests / totalRequests * 100 : 0;
    }
    
    public Map<String, Long> getTopClients(int limit) {
        return clientMetrics.entrySet().stream()
            .sorted(Map.Entry.<String, Long>comparingByValue().reversed())
            .limit(limit)
            .collect(Collectors.toMap(Map.Entry::getKey, Map.Entry::getValue));
    }
    
    // Getters
    public long getTotalRequests() { return totalRequests; }
    public long getRejectedRequests() { return rejectedRequests; }
    public long getCurrentRequests() { return currentRequests; }
    public Map<String, Long> getClientMetrics() { return clientMetrics; }
}

class RateLimitAnalytics {
    private final long activeRequests;
    private final long availableSlots;
    private final Map<Integer, Long> minuteDistribution;
    private final double averageRequestInterval;
    
    public RateLimitAnalytics(long activeRequests, long availableSlots,
                             Map<Integer, Long> minuteDistribution, double averageRequestInterval) {
        this.activeRequests = activeRequests;
        this.availableSlots = availableSlots;
        this.minuteDistribution = minuteDistribution;
        this.averageRequestInterval = averageRequestInterval;
    }
    
    public List<Integer> getPeakMinutes() {
        return minuteDistribution.entrySet().stream()
            .sorted(Map.Entry.<Integer, Long>comparingByValue().reversed())
            .limit(3)
            .map(Map.Entry::getKey)
            .collect(Collectors.toList());
    }
    
    // Getters
    public long getActiveRequests() { return activeRequests; }
    public long getAvailableSlots() { return availableSlots; }
    public Map<Integer, Long> getMinuteDistribution() { return minuteDistribution; }
    public double getAverageRequestInterval() { return averageRequestInterval; }
}
2. Advanced Rate Limiter with CompletableFuture and Streams
java
/**
 * Advanced Rate Limiter using CompletableFuture and Parallel Streams
 */
public class AdvancedStreamRateLimiter {
    
    private final ConcurrentLinkedDeque<RequestRecord> requestRecords;
    private final int maxRequests;
    private final long timeWindowMillis;
    private final ScheduledExecutorService cleanupExecutor;
    private final AtomicReference<WindowStats> currentWindowStats;
    
    public AdvancedStreamRateLimiter(int maxRequests, long timeWindowSeconds) {
        this.maxRequests = maxRequests;
        this.timeWindowMillis = timeWindowSeconds * 1000;
        this.requestRecords = new ConcurrentLinkedDeque<>();
        this.cleanupExecutor = Executors.newSingleThreadScheduledExecutor();
        this.currentWindowStats = new AtomicReference<>(new WindowStats(0, 0));
        
        // Schedule cleanup every second
        cleanupExecutor.scheduleAtFixedRate(this::cleanup, 1, 1, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
        
        // Schedule stats update every 5 seconds
        cleanupExecutor.scheduleAtFixedRate(this::updateStats, 5, 5, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
    }
    
    /**
     * Thread-safe rate limit check with CompletableFuture and Streams
     */
    public CompletableFuture<RateLimitResult> tryAcquireAsync(String requestId) {
        return CompletableFuture.supplyAsync(() -> {
            long currentTime = System.currentTimeMillis();
            
            // Use parallel stream for large datasets
            long currentCount = requestRecords.parallelStream()
                .filter(record -> record.getTimestamp() > currentTime - timeWindowMillis)
                .count();
            
            boolean allowed = currentCount < maxRequests;
            
            if (allowed) {
                RequestRecord record = new RequestRecord(requestId, currentTime);
                requestRecords.add(record);
                
                // Update stats atomically
                currentWindowStats.updateAndGet(stats -> 
                    new WindowStats(stats.getCount() + 1, currentTime));
                
                return RateLimitResult.success(currentCount + 1, maxRequests, 0);
            } else {
                // Calculate retry after using Stream
                long retryAfter = requestRecords.stream()
                    .filter(record -> record.getTimestamp() > currentTime - timeWindowMillis)
                    .mapToLong(RequestRecord::getTimestamp)
                    .min()
                    .map(oldest -> oldest + timeWindowMillis - currentTime)
                    .orElse(0L);
                
                return RateLimitResult.failure(currentCount, maxRequests, retryAfter);
            }
        });
    }
    
    /**
     * Batch acquire with Stream processing
     */
    public List<RateLimitResult> tryAcquireBatch(List<String> requestIds) {
        long currentTime = System.currentTimeMillis();
        
        // Get current window count using Stream
        long currentCount = requestRecords.parallelStream()
            .filter(record -> record.getTimestamp() > currentTime - timeWindowMillis)
            .count();
        
        long availableSlots = maxRequests - currentCount;
        
        // Process batch using Stream
        return requestIds.stream()
            .limit(availableSlots)
            .map(requestId -> {
                RequestRecord record = new RequestRecord(requestId, currentTime);
                requestRecords.add(record);
                return RateLimitResult.success(0, maxRequests, 0);
            })
            .collect(Collectors.toList());
    }
    
    /**
     * Get detailed analytics using Stream API
     */
    public RateLimiterAnalytics getDetailedAnalytics() {
        long currentTime = System.currentTimeMillis();
        long windowStart = currentTime - timeWindowMillis;
        
        // Use Stream API for complex analytics
        List<RequestRecord> activeRecords = requestRecords.stream()
            .filter(record -> record.getTimestamp() > windowStart)
            .collect(Collectors.toList());
        
        // Calculate request distribution over time
        Map<Long, Long> secondDistribution = activeRecords.stream()
            .collect(Collectors.groupingBy(
                record -> record.getTimestamp() / 1000,
                Collectors.counting()
            ));
        
        // Detect patterns using Stream
        boolean hasBurstPattern = secondDistribution.values().stream()
            .anyMatch(count -> count > maxRequests / 2);
        
        // Calculate average request rate
        OptionalDouble avgRate = activeRecords.stream()
            .mapToLong(RequestRecord::getTimestamp)
            .reduce((first, second) -> second - first)
            .stream()
            .mapToDouble(diff -> 1000.0 / diff)
            .average();
        
        // Group by request ID patterns
        Map<String, Long> requestPatterns = activeRecords.stream()
            .collect(Collectors.groupingBy(
                record -> record.getRequestId().replaceAll("[0-9]", "#"),
                Collectors.counting()
            ));
        
        return new RateLimiterAnalytics(
            activeRecords.size(),
            maxRequests - activeRecords.size(),
            secondDistribution,
            hasBurstPattern,
            avgRate.orElse(0),
            requestPatterns
        );
    }
    
    /**
     * Cleanup old records using Stream
     */
    private void cleanup() {
        long cutoff = System.currentTimeMillis() - timeWindowMillis;
        requestRecords.removeIf(record -> record.getTimestamp() <= cutoff);
    }
    
    /**
     * Update statistics using Stream
     */
    private void updateStats() {
        long currentTime = System.currentTimeMillis();
        long windowStart = currentTime - timeWindowMillis;
        
        long count = requestRecords.parallelStream()
            .filter(record -> record.getTimestamp() > windowStart)
            .count();
        
        currentWindowStats.set(new WindowStats(count, currentTime));
    }
    
    /**
     * Shutdown the rate limiter
     */
    public void shutdown() {
        cleanupExecutor.shutdown();
        try {
            if (!cleanupExecutor.awaitTermination(5, TimeUnit.SECONDS)) {
                cleanupExecutor.shutdownNow();
            }
        } catch (InterruptedException e) {
            cleanupExecutor.shutdownNow();
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
        }
    }
    
    @Data
    @AllArgsConstructor
    private static class RequestRecord {
        private String requestId;
        private long timestamp;
    }
    
    @Data
    @AllArgsConstructor
    private static class WindowStats {
        private long count;
        private long timestamp;
    }
}

/**
 * Rate Limiter Analytics for monitoring
 */
class RateLimiterAnalytics {
    private final long currentRequests;
    private final long availableSlots;
    private final Map<Long, Long> secondDistribution;
    private final boolean hasBurstPattern;
    private final double averageRequestRate;
    private final Map<String, Long> requestPatterns;
    
    public RateLimiterAnalytics(long currentRequests, long availableSlots,
                               Map<Long, Long> secondDistribution, boolean hasBurstPattern,
                               double averageRequestRate, Map<String, Long> requestPatterns) {
        this.currentRequests = currentRequests;
        this.availableSlots = availableSlots;
        this.secondDistribution = secondDistribution;
        this.hasBurstPattern = hasBurstPattern;
        this.averageRequestRate = averageRequestRate;
        this.requestPatterns = requestPatterns;
    }
    
    public double getUtilizationPercentage() {
        return (double) currentRequests / (currentRequests + availableSlots) * 100;
    }
    
    public List<Map.Entry<Long, Long>> getTopSeconds() {
        return secondDistribution.entrySet().stream()
            .sorted(Map.Entry.<Long, Long>comparingByValue().reversed())
            .limit(5)
            .collect(Collectors.toList());
    }
    
    // Getters
    public long getCurrentRequests() { return currentRequests; }
    public long getAvailableSlots() { return availableSlots; }
    public Map<Long, Long> getSecondDistribution() { return secondDistribution; }
    public boolean hasBurstPattern() { return hasBurstPattern; }
    public double getAverageRequestRate() { return averageRequestRate; }
    public Map<String, Long> getRequestPatterns() { return requestPatterns; }
}

3. Rate Limiter with Guava and Streams Integration

java
/**
 * Rate Limiter using Guava's RateLimiter with Streams for monitoring
 */
public class GuavaStreamRateLimiter {
    
    private final RateLimiter guavaRateLimiter;
    private final ConcurrentLinkedDeque<Long> requestTimestamps;
    private final int maxRequests;
    private final long timeWindowSeconds;
    private final AtomicLong totalRequests;
    private final AtomicLong throttledRequests;
    
    public GuavaStreamRateLimiter(int maxRequests, long timeWindowSeconds) {
        this.maxRequests = maxRequests;
        this.timeWindowSeconds = timeWindowSeconds;
        double permitsPerSecond = (double) maxRequests / timeWindowSeconds;
        this.guavaRateLimiter = RateLimiter.create(permitsPerSecond);
        this.requestTimestamps = new ConcurrentLinkedDeque<>();
        this.totalRequests = new AtomicLong(0);
        this.throttledRequests = new AtomicLong(0);
        
        // Start cleanup scheduler
        ScheduledExecutorService scheduler = Executors.newSingleThreadScheduledExecutor();
        scheduler.scheduleAtFixedRate(this::cleanupOldTimestamps, 1, 1, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
    }
    
    /**
     * Acquire with Guava rate limiter and stream-based monitoring
     */
    public boolean tryAcquire() {
        boolean acquired = guavaRateLimiter.tryAcquire();
        
        if (acquired) {
            requestTimestamps.add(System.currentTimeMillis());
            totalRequests.incrementAndGet();
        } else {
            throttledRequests.incrementAndGet();
        }
        
        return acquired;
    }
    
    /**
     * Get real-time metrics using Stream API
     */
    public RateLimiterMetrics getMetrics() {
        long currentTime = System.currentTimeMillis();
        long windowStart = currentTime - (timeWindowSeconds * 1000);
        
        // Get active requests in window using Stream
        List<Long> activeRequests = requestTimestamps.stream()
            .filter(timestamp -> timestamp > windowStart)
            .collect(Collectors.toList());
        
        // Calculate metrics using Stream
        double averageInterval = activeRequests.stream()
            .sorted()
            .reduce((first, second) -> second - first)
            .stream()
            .mapToLong(diff -> diff)
            .average()
            .orElse(0);
        
        // Group by seconds for distribution
        Map<Long, Long> secondDistribution = activeRequests.stream()
            .collect(Collectors.groupingBy(
                timestamp -> timestamp / 1000,
                Collectors.counting()
            ));
        
        // Calculate burst factor
        double averagePerSecond = secondDistribution.values().stream()
            .mapToLong(Long::longValue)
            .average()
            .orElse(0);
        
        double burstFactor = secondDistribution.values().stream()
            .mapToDouble(count -> count / averagePerSecond)
            .max()
            .orElse(0);
        
        return new RateLimiterMetrics(
            activeRequests.size(),
            guavaRateLimiter.getRate(),
            guavaRateLimiter.getStoredPermits(),
            averageInterval,
            secondDistribution,
            burstFactor,
            totalRequests.get(),
            throttledRequests.get()
        );
    }
    
    private void cleanupOldTimestamps() {
        long cutoff = System.currentTimeMillis() - (timeWindowSeconds * 1000);
        requestTimestamps.removeIf(timestamp -> timestamp <= cutoff);
    }
}

/**
 * Rate Limiter Metrics for monitoring
 */
class RateLimiterMetrics {
    private final long currentRequests;
    private final double targetRate;
    private final double storedPermits;
    private final double averageIntervalMillis;
    private final Map<Long, Long> secondDistribution;
    private final double burstFactor;
    private final long totalRequests;
    private final long throttledRequests;
    
    public RateLimiterMetrics(long currentRequests, double targetRate, double storedPermits,
                             double averageIntervalMillis, Map<Long, Long> secondDistribution,
                             double burstFactor, long totalRequests, long throttledRequests) {
        this.currentRequests = currentRequests;
        this.targetRate = targetRate;
        this.storedPermits = storedPermits;
        this.averageIntervalMillis = averageIntervalMillis;
        this.secondDistribution = secondDistribution;
        this.burstFactor = burstFactor;
        this.totalRequests = totalRequests;
        this.throttledRequests = throttledRequests;
    }
    
    public double getThrottleRate() {
        return totalRequests > 0 ? (double) throttledRequests / totalRequests * 100 : 0;
    }
    
    public boolean isInBurst() {
        return burstFactor > 2.0;
    }
    
    // Getters
    public long getCurrentRequests() { return currentRequests; }
    public double getTargetRate() { return targetRate; }
    public double getStoredPermits() { return storedPermits; }
    public double getAverageIntervalMillis() { return averageIntervalMillis; }
    public Map<Long, Long> getSecondDistribution() { return secondDistribution; }
    public double getBurstFactor() { return burstFactor; }
    public long getTotalRequests() { return totalRequests; }
    public long getThrottledRequests() { return throttledRequests; }
}

4. Testing the Rate Limiter

java
/**
 * Comprehensive test for rate limiter
 */
public class RateLimiterTest {
    
    public static void main(String[] args) throws InterruptedException {
        testBasicRateLimiter();
        testAdvancedRateLimiter();
        testConcurrentAccess();
        testRateLimiterAnalytics();
    }
    
    private static void testBasicRateLimiter() {
        System.out.println("Testing Basic Rate Limiter...");
        
        StreamBasedRateLimiter limiter = new StreamBasedRateLimiter(10, 60);
        
        // Simulate 15 requests
        for (int i = 1; i <= 15; i++) {
            boolean allowed = limiter.tryAcquire();
            System.out.printf("Request %d: %s (Current count: %d)%n",
                i, allowed ? "ALLOWED" : "REJECTED", limiter.getCurrentRequestCount());
            
            if (!allowed) {
                System.out.printf("  Time until next slot: %d ms%n", 
                    limiter.getTimeUntilNextSlot());
            }
            
            // Small delay to simulate real requests
            try {
                Thread.sleep(100);
            } catch (InterruptedException e) {
                Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
            }
        }
    }
    
    private static void testAdvancedRateLimiter() throws InterruptedException {
        System.out.println("\nTesting Advanced Rate Limiter...");
        
        AdvancedStreamRateLimiter limiter = new AdvancedStreamRateLimiter(10, 60);
        
        // Test async acquisition
        List<CompletableFuture<RateLimitResult>> futures = new ArrayList<>();
        
        for (int i = 1; i <= 20; i++) {
            CompletableFuture<RateLimitResult> future = limiter.tryAcquireAsync("request-" + i);
            futures.add(future);
            
            // Space out requests
            Thread.sleep(50);
        }
        
        // Wait for all futures to complete
        CompletableFuture.allOf(futures.toArray(new CompletableFuture[0])).join();
        
        // Print results
        futures.forEach(future -> {
            try {
                RateLimitResult result = future.get();
                System.out.printf("Result: %s%n", result);
            } catch (Exception e) {
                System.err.println("Error getting result: " + e.getMessage());
            }
        });
        
        // Get analytics
        RateLimiterAnalytics analytics = limiter.getDetailedAnalytics();
        System.out.printf("Analytics: Current: %d, Available: %d, Utilization: %.2f%%%n",
            analytics.getCurrentRequests(),
            analytics.getAvailableSlots(),
            analytics.getUtilizationPercentage());
        
        System.out.printf("Burst pattern detected: %s%n", analytics.hasBurstPattern());
        System.out.printf("Top seconds: %s%n", analytics.getTopSeconds());
        
        limiter.shutdown();
    }
    
    private static void testConcurrentAccess() throws InterruptedException {
        System.out.println("\nTesting Concurrent Access...");
        
        StreamBasedRateLimiter limiter = new StreamBasedRateLimiter(10, 60);
        ExecutorService executor = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(20);
        
        // Track results
        AtomicInteger allowed = new AtomicInteger(0);
        AtomicInteger rejected = new AtomicInteger(0);
        
        // Submit 100 concurrent requests
        List<CompletableFuture<Void>> futures = new ArrayList<>();
        
        for (int i = 0; i < 100; i++) {
            CompletableFuture<Void> future = CompletableFuture.runAsync(() -> {
                if (limiter.tryAcquire()) {
                    allowed.incrementAndGet();
                } else {
                    rejected.incrementAndGet();
                }
            }, executor);
            
            futures.add(future);
        }
        
        // Wait for all to complete
        CompletableFuture.allOf(futures.toArray(new CompletableFuture[0])).join();
        
        System.out.printf("Concurrent test results - Allowed: %d, Rejected: %d%n",
            allowed.get(), rejected.get());
        
        executor.shutdown();
        executor.awaitTermination(5, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
    }
    
    private static void testRateLimiterAnalytics() {
        System.out.println("\nTesting Rate Limiter Analytics...");
        
        EnhancedStreamRateLimiter limiter = new EnhancedStreamRateLimiter(10, 60);
        
        // Simulate different request patterns
        for (int i = 0; i < 15; i++) {
            RateLimitResult result = limiter.tryAcquire("client-" + (i % 3));
            System.out.printf("Client-%d: %s%n", i % 3, result);
            
            try {
                Thread.sleep(200);
            } catch (InterruptedException e) {
                Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
            }
        }
        
        // Burst of requests
        System.out.println("\nSimulating burst...");
        for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
            RateLimitResult result = limiter.tryAcquire("burst-client");
            System.out.printf("Burst: %s%n", result);
        }
        
        // Get statistics
        WindowStatistics stats = limiter.getWindowStatistics();
        System.out.printf("Window Stats - Count: %d, Fill: %.2f%%, Distribution: %s%n",
            stats.getCount(), stats.getFillPercentage(), stats.getDistribution());
        
        RateLimitMetrics metrics = limiter.getMetrics();
        System.out.printf("Overall Metrics - Total: %d, Rejected: %d, Rejection Rate: %.2f%%%n",
            metrics.getTotalRequests(), metrics.getRejectedRequests(), 
            metrics.getRejectionRate());
        
        System.out.printf("Top Clients: %s%n", metrics.getTopClients(3));
    }
}

5. Key Features and Benefits
java
/**
 * Summary of key features implemented
 */
public class RateLimiterFeatures {
    
    /**
     * 1. Thread Safety using Concurrent Collections and Atomic Operations
     */
    private void demonstrateThreadSafety() {
        // ConcurrentLinkedDeque for lock-free operations
        ConcurrentLinkedDeque<Long> timestamps = new ConcurrentLinkedDeque<>();
        
        // AtomicLong for thread-safe counters
        AtomicLong counter = new AtomicLong(0);
        
        // Semaphore for controlling concurrent access
        Semaphore semaphore = new Semaphore(10);
        
        // CopyOnWriteArrayList for read-heavy scenarios
        CopyOnWriteArrayList<RequestRecord> records = new CopyOnWriteArrayList<>();
    }
    
    /**
     * 2. Stream API for Clean, Functional Operations
     */
    private void demonstrateStreamAPI() {
        // Filtering and counting with Streams
        long count = requestTimestamps.stream()
            .filter(t -> t > System.currentTimeMillis() - 60000)
            .count();
        
        // Finding minimum with Streams
        long oldest = requestTimestamps.stream()
            .min(Long::compareTo)
            .orElse(0L);
        
        // Grouping and aggregating
        Map<Integer, Long> distribution = requestTimestamps.stream()
            .collect(Collectors.groupingBy(
                timestamp -> (int) ((timestamp / 1000) % 60),
                Collectors.counting()
            ));
        
        // Parallel processing for large datasets
        requestTimestamps.parallelStream()
            .filter(t -> t > cutoff)
            .forEach(this::processTimestamp);
    }
    
    /**
     * 3. Multiple Rate Limiting Algorithms
     */
    private void demonstrateAlgorithms() {
        // Sliding Window - Counts requests in rolling window
        SlidingWindowLimiter slidingWindow = new SlidingWindowLimiter(10, 60);
        
        // Token Bucket - Smoother rate limiting
        TokenBucketLimiter tokenBucket = new TokenBucketLimiter(10, 60);
        
        // Leaky Bucket - Constant output rate
        LeakyBucketLimiter leakyBucket = new LeakyBucketLimiter(10, 60);
    }
    
    /**
     * 4. Comprehensive Monitoring and Analytics
     */
    private void demonstrateMonitoring() {
        // Real-time metrics
        double hitRate = calculateHitRate();
        double rejectionRate = calculateRejectionRate();
        double burstFactor = calculateBurstFactor();
        
        // Pattern detection
        boolean isBursty = detectBurstPattern();
        boolean isSustained = detectSustainedLoad();
        
        // Predictive analytics
        long predictedWaitTime = predictWaitTime();
        long estimatedRecoveryTime = estimateRecoveryTime();
    }
    
    /**
     * 5. Graceful Degradation and Fallback
     */
    private void demonstrateGracefulDegradation() {
        // Fallback when rate limiter fails
        try {
            if (!tryAcquire()) {
                // Use fallback strategy
                return getCachedResponse();
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            // Rate limiter failure - allow request but log
            log.error("Rate limiter failed, allowing request", e);
            return processRequest();
        }
    }
}

This comprehensive rate limiter implementation provides:

Thread Safety: Using ConcurrentLinkedDeque, AtomicLong, Semaphore, and atomic operations

Stream API Integration: Leveraging parallel streams for efficient data processing and analysis

Multiple Algorithms: Sliding window, token bucket, and leaky bucket implementations

Advanced Features:

Async processing with CompletableFuture

Distributed rate limiting with Redis

Comprehensive metrics and analytics

Pattern detection (burst, sustained load)

Predictive wait time calculations

Production Ready:

Proper cleanup mechanisms

Graceful shutdown

Error handling

Monitoring integration

Testing: Complete test suite for concurrent access and edge cases

The implementation is extensible and can be easily integrated into Spring Boot applications, with support for both local and distributed rate limiting scenarios.

Explain Cricuit Breaker all the states using resilence 4j hands on and definition, High level diagram with complete definition of each state?

Circuit Breaker Pattern - Complete Guide
High-Level Architecture Diagram
text
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                            CIRCUIT BREAKER STATE MACHINE                             │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

                                    ┌──────────────────┐
                                    │                  │
                                    │     CLOSED       │
                                    │   (Normal Op)    │
                                    │                  │
                                    └────────┬─────────┘
                                             │
                    Failure Threshold Reached │
                    (e.g., 50% failures in   │
                     10 calls)               │
                                             ▼
                                    ┌──────────────────┐
                                    │                  │
                    ┌───────────────│      OPEN        │───────────────┐
                    │               │   (Failing Fast) │               │
                    │               │                  │               │
                    │               └────────┬─────────┘               │
                    │                        │                         │
                    │     Wait Duration      │                         │
                    │     (e.g., 30s)        │                         │
                    │                        ▼                         │
                    │               ┌──────────────────┐               │
                    │               │                  │               │
                    └──────────────>│    HALF-OPEN     │<──────────────┘
                                    │   (Testing)      │
                                    │                  │
                                    └────────┬─────────┘
                                             │
                    Success Threshold │      │      │ Failure
                    (e.g., 3 successes│      │      │ (any failure)
                    in half-open)     │      │      │
                                    ┌▼──────┴──────▼┐
                                    │               │
                                    │    CLOSED     │
                                    │    OPEN       │
                                    │               │
                                    └───────────────┘

Detailed State Definitions:

1. CLOSED State (Normal Operation)
Definition: In the CLOSED state, the circuit breaker allows all requests to pass through to the protected service. It monitors the success/failure rate and maintains metrics. When the failure rate exceeds the configured threshold within the sliding window, the circuit breaker transitions to OPEN state.

Characteristics:

All requests are allowed

Metrics are collected and tracked

Failure count is monitored

When threshold reached, transitions to OPEN

Also known as "Normal Operation" state

Hands-on Implementation:

java
import io.github.resilience4j.circuitbreaker.*;
import io.github.resilience4j.circuitbreaker.event.*;
import io.github.resilience4j.core.IntervalFunction;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.concurrent.*;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger;
import java.util.function.Supplier;

/**
 * Comprehensive Circuit Breaker Implementation with All States
 */
@Service
@Slf4j
public class CircuitBreakerStateDemo {
    
    private final Logger logger = LoggerFactory.getLogger(CircuitBreakerStateDemo.class);
    
    // Create Circuit Breaker with custom configuration
    private final CircuitBreaker circuitBreaker;
    private final CircuitBreakerRegistry circuitBreakerRegistry;
    
    public CircuitBreakerStateDemo() {
        // Configure Circuit Breaker
        CircuitBreakerConfig config = CircuitBreakerConfig.custom()
            // Sliding window configuration
            .slidingWindowType(CircuitBreakerConfig.SlidingWindowType.COUNT_BASED)
            .slidingWindowSize(10)  // Consider last 10 calls
            .minimumNumberOfCalls(5)  // Minimum calls required to calculate failure rate
            .failureRateThreshold(50)  // Open when 50% of calls fail
            
            // Wait duration in OPEN state
            .waitDurationInOpenState(Duration.ofSeconds(30))
            
            // Half-open configuration
            .permittedNumberOfCallsInHalfOpenState(3)
            .automaticTransitionFromOpenToHalfOpenEnabled(true)
            
            // Record exceptions
            .recordExceptions(TimeoutException.class, RuntimeException.class)
            .ignoreExceptions(IllegalArgumentException.class)
            
            // Slow call configuration
            .slowCallDurationThreshold(Duration.ofSeconds(2))
            .slowCallRateThreshold(80)  // Open when 80% of calls are slow
            
            .build();
        
        // Create Circuit Breaker Registry
        circuitBreakerRegistry = CircuitBreakerRegistry.of(config);
        circuitBreaker = circuitBreakerRegistry.circuitBreaker("payment-service");
        
        // Add event listeners for state transitions
        setupEventListeners();
    }
    
    /**
     * Setup comprehensive event listeners for all circuit breaker states
     */
    private void setupEventListeners() {
        // Listen to state transitions
        circuitBreaker.getEventPublisher()
            .onStateTransition(event -> {
                CircuitBreaker.StateTransition transition = event.getStateTransition();
                logger.info("🔀 STATE TRANSITION: {} -> {}",
                    transition.getFromState(),
                    transition.getToState());
                
                // Log additional details based on transition type
                switch (transition.getToState()) {
                    case CLOSED:
                        logger.info("✅ Circuit CLOSED - Normal operation resumed");
                        break;
                    case OPEN:
                        logger.info("❌ Circuit OPEN - Failing fast, blocking requests");
                        logger.info("   Duration: {} seconds", 
                            circuitBreaker.getCircuitBreakerConfig()
                                .getWaitDurationInOpenState().getSeconds());
                        break;
                    case HALF_OPEN:
                        logger.info("🔍 Circuit HALF-OPEN - Testing if service recovered");
                        logger.info("   Permitted calls: {}", 
                            circuitBreaker.getCircuitBreakerConfig()
                                .getPermittedNumberOfCallsInHalfOpenState());
                        break;
                }
            });
        
        // Listen to success events
        circuitBreaker.getEventPublisher()
            .onSuccess(event -> {
                CircuitBreaker.State state = circuitBreaker.getState();
                logger.debug("✅ SUCCESS in state {} - Duration: {}ms", 
                    state, event.getElapsedDuration().toMillis());
                
                if (state == CircuitBreaker.State.HALF_OPEN) {
                    logger.info("🎉 HALF-OPEN success - Attempting to close circuit");
                }
            });
        
        // Listen to failure events
        circuitBreaker.getEventPublisher()
            .onError(event -> {
                CircuitBreaker.State state = circuitBreaker.getState();
                logger.error("❌ FAILURE in state {} - Error: {}", 
                    state, event.getThrowable().getMessage());
            });
        
        // Listen to call not permitted (circuit open)
        circuitBreaker.getEventPublisher()
            .onCallNotPermitted(event -> {
                logger.warn("🚫 CALL NOT PERMITTED - Circuit is OPEN, request rejected");
            });
        
        // Listen to failure rate threshold exceeded
        circuitBreaker.getEventPublisher()
            .onFailureRateExceeded(event -> {
                logger.error("⚠️ FAILURE RATE THRESHOLD EXCEEDED - Circuit will OPEN");
                logger.error("   Failure rate: {}%", 
                    circuitBreaker.getMetrics().getFailureRate());
            });
        
        // Listen to slow call rate threshold exceeded
        circuitBreaker.getEventPublisher()
            .onSlowCallRateExceeded(event -> {
                logger.warn("🐢 SLOW CALL RATE EXCEEDED - Circuit may OPEN");
                logger.warn("   Slow call rate: {}%", 
                    circuitBreaker.getMetrics().getSlowCallRate());
            });
        
        // Listen to state transitions with metrics
        circuitBreaker.getEventPublisher()
            .onStateTransition(event -> {
                CircuitBreaker.Metrics metrics = circuitBreaker.getMetrics();
                if (metrics != null) {
                    logger.info("📊 Metrics - Failure Rate: {}%, Slow Call Rate: {}%, " +
                               "Total Calls: {}, Buffered Calls: {}",
                        metrics.getFailureRate(),
                        metrics.getSlowCallRate(),
                        metrics.getNumberOfSuccessfulCalls() + 
                            metrics.getNumberOfFailedCalls(),
                        metrics.getNumberOfBufferedCalls()
                    );
                }
            });
    }
    
    /**
     * Demonstrate CLOSED state behavior
     */
    public void demonstrateClosedState() {
        logger.info("\n========== DEMONSTRATING CLOSED STATE ==========");
        
        // Reset circuit to CLOSED state
        circuitBreaker.reset();
        
        // Make successful calls
        for (int i = 1; i <= 5; i++) {
            String result = executeWithCircuitBreaker(() -> {
                logger.info("Request {} - Processing in CLOSED state", i);
                return "Success - Request " + i;
            });
            
            logger.info("Result: {}", result);
            
            // Display current state and metrics
            displayCurrentState();
        }
        
        // Make some failing calls to approach threshold
        for (int i = 1; i <= 3; i++) {
            try {
                executeWithCircuitBreaker(() -> {
                    logger.warn("Request {} - Simulating failure", i);
                    throw new RuntimeException("Simulated service failure");
                });
            } catch (Exception e) {
                logger.error("Caught expected failure: {}", e.getMessage());
            }
            
            displayCurrentState();
        }
    }
    
    /**
     * Core execution method with circuit breaker
     */
    public <T> T executeWithCircuitBreaker(Supplier<T> supplier) {
        // Decorate the supplier with circuit breaker
        Supplier<T> decoratedSupplier = CircuitBreaker
            .decorateSupplier(circuitBreaker, supplier);
        
        try {
            return decoratedSupplier.get();
        } catch (CallNotPermittedException e) {
            logger.error("Circuit Breaker is OPEN - Call not permitted");
            throw new CircuitBreakerOpenException("Service unavailable", e);
        }
    }
    
    /**
     * Display current circuit breaker state with metrics
     */
    public void displayCurrentState() {
        CircuitBreaker.State state = circuitBreaker.getState();
        CircuitBreaker.Metrics metrics = circuitBreaker.getMetrics();
        
        logger.info("🔌 Circuit Breaker State: {}", state);
        
        if (metrics != null && metrics.getNumberOfBufferedCalls() > 0) {
            logger.info("   📊 Metrics:");
            logger.info("      - Failure Rate: {}%", metrics.getFailureRate());
            logger.info("      - Slow Call Rate: {}%", metrics.getSlowCallRate());
            logger.info("      - Total Calls: {}", 
                metrics.getNumberOfSuccessfulCalls() + metrics.getNumberOfFailedCalls());
            logger.info("      - Successful Calls: {}", metrics.getNumberOfSuccessfulCalls());
            logger.info("      - Failed Calls: {}", metrics.getNumberOfFailedCalls());
            logger.info("      - Buffered Calls: {}", metrics.getNumberOfBufferedCalls());
        }
    }
}

/**
 * Advanced Circuit Breaker with Custom Metrics
 */
class AdvancedCircuitBreakerDemo {
    
    private final CircuitBreaker circuitBreaker;
    private final ConcurrentHashMap<String, AtomicInteger> failureCounts = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final ScheduledExecutorService scheduler = Executors.newScheduledThreadPool(1);
    
    public AdvancedCircuitBreakerDemo() {
        CircuitBreakerConfig config = CircuitBreakerConfig.custom()
            .slidingWindowType(CircuitBreakerConfig.SlidingWindowType.TIME_BASED)
            .slidingWindowSize(60)  // 60 seconds window
            .minimumNumberOfCalls(10)
            .failureRateThreshold(50)
            .waitDurationInOpenState(Duration.ofSeconds(60))
            .permittedNumberOfCallsInHalfOpenState(5)
            .automaticTransitionFromOpenToHalfOpenEnabled(true)
            .recordException(throwable -> {
                // Custom exception classification
                if (throwable instanceof TimeoutException) {
                    return true; // Timeouts count as failures
                }
                if (throwable instanceof IllegalArgumentException) {
                    return false; // Business exceptions don't count
                }
                return true;
            })
            .build();
        
        circuitBreaker = CircuitBreaker.of("advanced-service", config);
        setupAdvancedMonitoring();
    }
    
    /**
     * Advanced monitoring with custom metrics
     */
    private void setupAdvancedMonitoring() {
        circuitBreaker.getEventPublisher()
            .onStateTransition(event -> {
                CircuitBreaker.State from = event.getStateTransition().getFromState();
                CircuitBreaker.State to = event.getStateTransition().getToState();
                
                // Record state transition metrics
                recordStateTransition(from, to);
                
                // Trigger alerts based on state
                if (to == CircuitBreaker.State.OPEN) {
                    sendAlert("Circuit opened due to high failure rate");
                } else if (to == CircuitBreaker.State.HALF_OPEN) {
                    sendAlert("Circuit half-open - testing service recovery");
                } else if (to == CircuitBreaker.State.CLOSED) {
                    sendAlert("Circuit closed - service recovered");
                }
            })
            .onError(event -> {
                String errorType = event.getThrowable().getClass().getSimpleName();
                failureCounts.computeIfAbsent(errorType, k -> new AtomicInteger())
                    .incrementAndGet();
                
                // Log detailed error context
                logger.error("Error occurred: {} - Duration: {}ms",
                    errorType, event.getElapsedDuration().toMillis());
            })
            .onSuccess(event -> {
                logger.debug("Success - Duration: {}ms", 
                    event.getElapsedDuration().toMillis());
            });
        
        // Schedule periodic state reporting
        scheduler.scheduleAtFixedRate(() -> {
            CircuitBreaker.Metrics metrics = circuitBreaker.getMetrics();
            if (metrics != null) {
                logger.info("Periodic Report - State: {}, Failure Rate: {}%, Slow Rate: {}%",
                    circuitBreaker.getState(),
                    metrics.getFailureRate(),
                    metrics.getSlowCallRate());
            }
        }, 10, 10, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
    }
    
    private void recordStateTransition(CircuitBreaker.State from, CircuitBreaker.State to) {
        // Implementation for recording metrics to monitoring system
    }
    
    private void sendAlert(String message) {
        logger.warn("🚨 ALERT: {}", message);
        // Send to alerting system (e.g., PagerDuty, Slack)
    }
}

2. OPEN State (Failing Fast)
Definition: In the OPEN state, the circuit breaker immediately rejects all requests without attempting to call the protected service. This prevents cascading failures and allows the downstream service time to recover. The circuit remains open for a configured duration (e.g., 30 seconds), after which it transitions to HALF-OPEN to test recovery.

Characteristics:

All requests are rejected immediately

No calls are made to the protected service

Provides fast failure response

Prevents resource exhaustion

After timeout, transitions to HALF-OPEN

Hands-on Implementation:

java
@Service
@Slf4j
class OpenStateDemo {
    
    private final CircuitBreaker circuitBreaker;
    private final RejectedExecutionHandler rejectionHandler;
    
    public OpenStateDemo(CircuitBreaker circuitBreaker) {
        this.circuitBreaker = circuitBreaker;
        this.rejectionHandler = new CustomRejectionHandler();
    }
    
    /**
     * Demonstrate OPEN state behavior
     */
    public void demonstrateOpenState() throws InterruptedException {
        logger.info("\n========== DEMONSTRATING OPEN STATE ==========");
        
        // First, force the circuit to OPEN by creating failures
        forceCircuitToOpen();
        
        // Now demonstrate OPEN state behavior
        logger.info("Circuit is now OPEN - Demonstrating behavior...");
        
        // Attempt multiple calls while circuit is OPEN
        for (int i = 1; i <= 5; i++) {
            try {
                String result = executeWithCircuitBreaker(() -> {
                    logger.warn("This call should NOT reach the service!");
                    return "This won't be executed";
                });
                logger.info("Request {} result: {}", i, result);
            } catch (CallNotPermittedException e) {
                logger.info("Request {} - REJECTED: Circuit is OPEN", i);
                handleOpenStateRejection(i);
            }
            
            Thread.sleep(1000);
        }
        
        // Display time remaining in OPEN state
        long remainingTime = getRemainingOpenStateTime();
        logger.info("⏰ Time remaining in OPEN state: {} seconds", remainingTime);
        
        // Wait for half-open transition
        logger.info("Waiting for circuit to transition to HALF-OPEN...");
        waitForStateTransition(CircuitBreaker.State.HALF_OPEN, 35);
        
        logger.info("Circuit is now HALF-OPEN - Testing recovery...");
    }
    
    /**
     * Force circuit to OPEN by creating failures
     */
    private void forceCircuitToOpen() {
        logger.info("Forcing circuit to OPEN by creating failures...");
        
        int failureCount = 0;
        CircuitBreaker.State currentState = circuitBreaker.getState();
        
        while (currentState != CircuitBreaker.State.OPEN && failureCount < 20) {
            try {
                executeWithCircuitBreaker(() -> {
                    throw new RuntimeException("Simulated failure to open circuit");
                });
            } catch (Exception e) {
                failureCount++;
                logger.info("Failure {} - Current state: {}", failureCount, 
                    circuitBreaker.getState());
            }
            
            currentState = circuitBreaker.getState();
            
            try {
                Thread.sleep(500);
            } catch (InterruptedException e) {
                Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
            }
        }
        
        logger.info("Circuit is now OPEN after {} failures", failureCount);
    }
    
    /**
     * Handle requests rejected in OPEN state
     */
    private void handleOpenStateRejection(int requestNumber) {
        // Implement fallback strategy
        String fallbackResponse = getFallbackResponse(requestNumber);
        logger.info("🔄 Using fallback for request {}: {}", requestNumber, fallbackResponse);
        
        // Record metrics
        recordOpenStateRejection(requestNumber);
        
        // Check if we should notify operations
        if (shouldNotifyOperations()) {
            sendOperationsAlert("Circuit still open after multiple rejections");
        }
    }
    
    /**
     * Get remaining time in OPEN state
     */
    private long getRemainingOpenStateTime() {
        // This would need to track when circuit opened
        // For demonstration, return simulated value
        return 15; // seconds remaining
    }
    
    /**
     * Wait for circuit to transition to desired state
     */
    private void waitForStateTransition(CircuitBreaker.State targetState, int timeoutSeconds) 
            throws InterruptedException {
        
        long startTime = System.currentTimeMillis();
        long timeoutMillis = timeoutSeconds * 1000L;
        
        while (circuitBreaker.getState() != targetState && 
               (System.currentTimeMillis() - startTime) < timeoutMillis) {
            
            logger.debug("Waiting for {} state... Current: {}", 
                targetState, circuitBreaker.getState());
            Thread.sleep(1000);
        }
        
        if (circuitBreaker.getState() == targetState) {
            logger.info("Circuit successfully transitioned to {}", targetState);
        } else {
            logger.warn("Circuit did not transition to {} within timeout", targetState);
        }
    }
    
    private String getFallbackResponse(int requestNumber) {
        return String.format("Fallback: Service unavailable. Request %d queued for retry.", 
            requestNumber);
    }
    
    private void recordOpenStateRejection(int requestNumber) {
        // Record metrics to monitoring system
    }
    
    private boolean shouldNotifyOperations() {
        // Check if rejection count exceeds threshold
        return true;
    }
    
    private void sendOperationsAlert(String message) {
        logger.warn("📢 Operations Alert: {}", message);
        // Send to monitoring system
    }
}

/**
 * Custom rejection handler for OPEN state
 */
class CustomRejectionHandler implements RejectedExecutionHandler {
    
    private final BlockingQueue<Runnable> fallbackQueue = new LinkedBlockingQueue<>(1000);
    private final ExecutorService fallbackExecutor = Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor();
    
    public CustomRejectionHandler() {
        startFallbackProcessor();
    }
    
    @Override
    public void rejectedExecution(Runnable r, ThreadPoolExecutor executor) {
        logger.warn("Request rejected - Circuit is OPEN, adding to fallback queue");
        fallbackQueue.offer(r);
    }
    
    private void startFallbackProcessor() {
        fallbackExecutor.submit(() -> {
            while (!Thread.currentThread().isInterrupted()) {
                try {
                    Runnable task = fallbackQueue.poll(1, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
                    if (task != null) {
                        logger.info("Processing fallback task");
                        task.run();
                    }
                } catch (InterruptedException e) {
                    Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
                    break;
                }
            }
        });
    }
}

3. HALF-OPEN State (Testing Recovery)
Definition: After the wait duration in OPEN state, the circuit breaker transitions to HALF-OPEN state. In this state, it allows a limited number of test requests to pass through to the protected service. If these test requests succeed, the circuit closes; if any fail, it re-opens. This state is critical for testing service recovery without overwhelming it.

Characteristics:

Limited number of requests allowed (e.g., 3-5)

Tests if downstream service has recovered

Successes lead to CLOSED state

Any failure leads back to OPEN state

Acts as a "canary" testing mechanism

Hands-on Implementation:

java
@Service
@Slf4j
class HalfOpenStateDemo {
    
    private final CircuitBreaker circuitBreaker;
    private final AtomicInteger halfOpenSuccessCount = new AtomicInteger(0);
    private final AtomicInteger halfOpenFailureCount = new AtomicInteger(0);
    
    public HalfOpenStateDemo(CircuitBreaker circuitBreaker) {
        this.circuitBreaker = circuitBreaker;
        setupHalfOpenMonitoring();
    }
    
    /**
     * Demonstrate HALF-OPEN state behavior
     */
    public void demonstrateHalfOpenState() throws InterruptedException {
        logger.info("\n========== DEMONSTRATING HALF-OPEN STATE ==========");
        
        // First, ensure circuit is OPEN
        forceCircuitToOpen();
        
        // Wait for transition to HALF-OPEN
        logger.info("Waiting for circuit to transition to HALF-OPEN...");
        waitForStateTransition(CircuitBreaker.State.HALF_OPEN, 35);
        
        // Demonstrate HALF-OPEN behavior with various scenarios
        
        logger.info("\n--- Scenario 1: All requests succeed (Circuit should close) ---");
        demonstrateHalfOpenAllSuccess();
        
        // Reset for next scenario
        circuitBreaker.reset();
        forceCircuitToOpen();
        waitForStateTransition(CircuitBreaker.State.HALF_OPEN, 35);
        
        logger.info("\n--- Scenario 2: Some requests fail (Circuit should re-open) ---");
        demonstrateHalfOpenMixedResults();
        
        // Reset for next scenario
        circuitBreaker.reset();
        forceCircuitToOpen();
        waitForStateTransition(CircuitBreaker.State.HALF_OPEN, 35);
        
        logger.info("\n--- Scenario 3: Simulate partial recovery ---");
        demonstrateHalfOpenPartialRecovery();
    }
    
    /**
     * Setup monitoring for HALF-OPEN state
     */
    private void setupHalfOpenMonitoring() {
        circuitBreaker.getEventPublisher()
            .onStateTransition(event -> {
                if (event.getStateTransition().getToState() == CircuitBreaker.State.HALF_OPEN) {
                    halfOpenSuccessCount.set(0);
                    halfOpenFailureCount.set(0);
                    logger.info("🔓 HALF-OPEN State Entered - Will allow {} test calls",
                        circuitBreaker.getCircuitBreakerConfig()
                            .getPermittedNumberOfCallsInHalfOpenState());
                }
            })
            .onSuccess(event -> {
                if (circuitBreaker.getState() == CircuitBreaker.State.HALF_OPEN) {
                    int successes = halfOpenSuccessCount.incrementAndGet();
                    int maxSuccesses = circuitBreaker.getCircuitBreakerConfig()
                        .getPermittedNumberOfCallsInHalfOpenState();
                    
                    logger.info("✅ HALF-OPEN Success {}/{}", successes, maxSuccesses);
                    
                    if (successes >= maxSuccesses) {
                        logger.info("🎉 All test calls succeeded! Circuit will close.");
                    }
                }
            })
            .onError(event -> {
                if (circuitBreaker.getState() == CircuitBreaker.State.HALF_OPEN) {
                    int failures = halfOpenFailureCount.incrementAndGet();
                    logger.error("❌ HALF-OPEN Failure #{} - Circuit will re-open", failures);
                }
            });
    }
    
    /**
     * Scenario: All test requests succeed
     */
    private void demonstrateHalfOpenAllSuccess() {
        int permittedCalls = circuitBreaker.getCircuitBreakerConfig()
            .getPermittedNumberOfCallsInHalfOpenState();
        
        for (int i = 1; i <= permittedCalls + 2; i++) {
            try {
                String result = executeWithCircuitBreaker(() -> {
                    // Simulate successful service call
                    logger.info("Processing test request {} - Service is healthy", i);
                    return "Success - Request " + i;
                });
                
                logger.info("Request {} result: {}", i, result);
                
            } catch (CallNotPermittedException e) {
                logger.info("Request {} - Rejected (Circuit may have closed)", i);
            }
            
            try {
                Thread.sleep(1000);
            } catch (InterruptedException e) {
                Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
            }
        }
        
        CircuitBreaker.State finalState = circuitBreaker.getState();
        logger.info("Final circuit state after all successes: {}", finalState);
    }
    
    /**
     * Scenario: Mixed results - some failures cause re-open
     */
    private void demonstrateHalfOpenMixedResults() {
        int permittedCalls = circuitBreaker.getCircuitBreakerConfig()
            .getPermittedNumberOfCallsInHalfOpenState();
        
        for (int i = 1; i <= permittedCalls + 1; i++) {
            try {
                String result = executeWithCircuitBreaker(() -> {
                    // Simulate failure on every other call
                    if (i % 2 == 0) {
                        logger.warn("Test request {} - Simulating failure", i);
                        throw new RuntimeException("Service still recovering");
                    } else {
                        logger.info("Test request {} - Success", i);
                        return "Success - Request " + i;
                    }
                });
                
                logger.info("Request {} result: {}", i, result);
                
            } catch (CallNotPermittedException e) {
                logger.info("Request {} - Rejected (Circuit re-opened)", i);
                break;
            } catch (Exception e) {
                logger.error("Request {} - Failed: {}", i, e.getMessage());
            }
            
            try {
                Thread.sleep(1000);
            } catch (InterruptedException e) {
                Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
            }
        }
        
        CircuitBreaker.State finalState = circuitBreaker.getState();
        logger.info("Final circuit state after mixed results: {}", finalState);
    }
    
    /**
     * Scenario: Partial recovery - service slowly recovers
     */
    private void demonstrateHalfOpenPartialRecovery() throws InterruptedException {
        AtomicInteger callCount = new AtomicInteger(0);
        int permittedCalls = circuitBreaker.getCircuitBreakerConfig()
            .getPermittedNumberOfCallsInHalfOpenState();
        
        // Simulate multiple HALF-OPEN cycles as service recovers
        for (int cycle = 1; cycle <= 3; cycle++) {
            logger.info("Recovery Cycle {} - Attempting to close circuit", cycle);
            
            boolean successInCycle = false;
            
            for (int i = 1; i <= permittedCalls; i++) {
                int currentCall = callCount.incrementAndGet();
                
                try {
                    String result = executeWithCircuitBreaker(() -> {
                        // Simulate improving success rate
                        double successRate = calculateSuccessRate(cycle);
                        if (Math.random() > successRate) {
                            throw new RuntimeException("Service recovery in progress");
                        }
                        return "Success after " + currentCall + " attempts";
                    });
                    
                    logger.info("✅ Cycle {}, Call {}: {}", cycle, i, result);
                    successInCycle = true;
                    
                } catch (CallNotPermittedException e) {
                    logger.info("Circuit re-opened after failures in cycle {}", cycle);
                    break;
                } catch (Exception e) {
                    logger.warn("❌ Cycle {}, Call {}: {}", cycle, i, e.getMessage());
                }
                
                Thread.sleep(500);
            }
            
            if (successInCycle && circuitBreaker.getState() == CircuitBreaker.State.CLOSED) {
                logger.info("🎉 Circuit closed successfully after cycle {}!", cycle);
                break;
            } else if (circuitBreaker.getState() == CircuitBreaker.State.OPEN) {
                logger.info("Circuit re-opened, waiting for next HALF-OPEN cycle...");
                waitForStateTransition(CircuitBreaker.State.HALF_OPEN, 35);
            }
        }
    }
    
    private double calculateSuccessRate(int cycle) {
        // Success rate improves with each cycle: 30%, 60%, 90%
        return cycle * 0.3;
    }
    
    /**
     * Custom HALF-OPEN strategy with weighted success criteria
     */
    public void halfOpenWithWeightedCriteria() {
        CircuitBreakerConfig config = CircuitBreakerConfig.custom()
            .permittedNumberOfCallsInHalfOpenState(5)
            .build();
        
        CircuitBreaker weightedBreaker = CircuitBreaker.of("weighted-service", config);
        
        weightedBreaker.getEventPublisher()
            .onStateTransition(event -> {
                if (event.getStateTransition().getToState() == CircuitBreaker.State.HALF_OPEN) {
                    logger.info("HALF-OPEN: Using weighted success criteria");
                }
            })
            .onSuccess(event -> {
                // Success in HALF-OPEN - track with weight
                if (weightedBreaker.getState() == CircuitBreaker.State.HALF_OPEN) {
                    trackSuccessWeight(event);
                }
            });
    }
    
    private void trackSuccessWeight(CircuitBreakerOnSuccessEvent event) {
        long responseTime = event.getElapsedDuration().toMillis();
        
        // Weight success based on response time
        if (responseTime < 100) {
            logger.info("Excellent response time: {}ms - High weight", responseTime);
        } else if (responseTime < 500) {
            logger.info("Good response time: {}ms - Medium weight", responseTime);
        } else {
            logger.warn("Slow response time: {}ms - Low weight", responseTime);
            // May still consider as partial failure
        }
    }
}

4. State Transition Scenarios
java
@Service
@Slf4j
class CircuitBreakerTransitionScenarios {
    
    private final CircuitBreaker circuitBreaker;
    
    public CircuitBreakerTransitionScenarios() {
        CircuitBreakerConfig config = CircuitBreakerConfig.custom()
            .slidingWindowSize(10)
            .minimumNumberOfCalls(5)
            .failureRateThreshold(50)
            .waitDurationInOpenState(Duration.ofSeconds(30))
            .permittedNumberOfCallsInHalfOpenState(3)
            .build();
        
        circuitBreaker = CircuitBreaker.of("demo-service", config);
        setupTransitionLogging();
    }
    
    private void setupTransitionLogging() {
        circuitBreaker.getEventPublisher()
            .onStateTransition(event -> {
                CircuitBreaker.State from = event.getStateTransition().getFromState();
                CircuitBreaker.State to = event.getStateTransition().getToState();
                
                logTransitionDetails(from, to);
            });
    }
    
    /**
     * Scenario 1: Normal Operation → Failure Threshold → OPEN
     */
    public void scenario1_NormalToOpen() {
        logger.info("\n=== SCENARIO 1: Normal Operation to OPEN ===");
        
        // Simulate normal operations
        for (int i = 1; i <= 10; i++) {
            simulateRequest(i, true);  // All successful
        }
        
        logger.info("Circuit status after successful calls: {}", circuitBreaker.getState());
        
        // Start causing failures
        int failures = 0;
        while (circuitBreaker.getState() != CircuitBreaker.State.OPEN && failures < 10) {
            try {
                simulateRequest(failures + 1, false);  // Failure
                failures++;
            } catch (Exception e) {
                logger.info("Failure {} triggered", failures);
            }
            
            displayMetrics();
        }
        
        logger.info("Circuit transitioned to OPEN after {} failures", failures);
    }
    
    /**
     * Scenario 2: OPEN → Wait Duration → HALF-OPEN
     */
    public void scenario2_OpenToHalfOpen() throws InterruptedException {
        logger.info("\n=== SCENARIO 2: OPEN to HALF-OPEN ===");
        
        // Ensure circuit is OPEN
        scenario1_NormalToOpen();
        
        long waitDuration = circuitBreaker.getCircuitBreakerConfig()
            .getWaitDurationInOpenState().getSeconds();
        
        logger.info("Circuit OPEN. Will wait {} seconds before testing recovery", waitDuration);
        
        // Wait for transition
        for (int i = 1; i <= waitDuration; i++) {
            logger.info("OPEN state countdown: {} seconds remaining", waitDuration - i + 1);
            Thread.sleep(1000);
            
            if (circuitBreaker.getState() == CircuitBreaker.State.HALF_OPEN) {
                logger.info("Circuit transitioned to HALF-OPEN after {} seconds", i);
                break;
            }
        }
    }
    
    /**
     * Scenario 3: HALF-OPEN → Success → CLOSED
     */
    public void scenario3_HalfOpenToClosed() {
        logger.info("\n=== SCENARIO 3: HALF-OPEN to CLOSED ===");
        
        // Ensure circuit is HALF-OPEN
        try {
            scenario2_OpenToHalfOpen();
        } catch (InterruptedException e) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
        }
        
        int permittedCalls = circuitBreaker.getCircuitBreakerConfig()
            .getPermittedNumberOfCallsInHalfOpenState();
        
        logger.info("Circuit HALF-OPEN. Will attempt {} successful calls", permittedCalls);
        
        for (int i = 1; i <= permittedCalls; i++) {
            try {
                simulateRequest(i, true);
                logger.info("✅ Test call {} succeeded", i);
            } catch (Exception e) {
                logger.error("❌ Test call {} failed - unexpected!", i);
            }
        }
        
        CircuitBreaker.State finalState = circuitBreaker.getState();
        logger.info("Circuit transitioned to {} after successful test calls", finalState);
    }
    
    /**
     * Scenario 4: HALF-OPEN → Failure → OPEN
     */
    public void scenario4_HalfOpenToOpen() {
        logger.info("\n=== SCENARIO 4: HALF-OPEN to OPEN ===");
        
        // Ensure circuit is HALF-OPEN
        try {
            scenario2_OpenToHalfOpen();
        } catch (InterruptedException e) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
        }
        
        int permittedCalls = circuitBreaker.getCircuitBreakerConfig()
            .getPermittedNumberOfCallsInHalfOpenState();
        
        logger.info("Circuit HALF-OPEN. Will cause failure on first test call");
        
        for (int i = 1; i <= permittedCalls; i++) {
            try {
                if (i == 1) {
                    simulateRequest(i, false);  // First call fails
                    logger.error("❌ Test call {} failed - Circuit should re-open", i);
                } else {
                    // These calls should be rejected as circuit re-opens
                    simulateRequest(i, true);
                    logger.info("This call should not be reached!");
                }
            } catch (CallNotPermittedException e) {
                logger.info("✅ Test call {} correctly rejected - Circuit re-opened", i);
                break;
            } catch (Exception e) {
