                logger.info("Test call {} failed as expected", i);
            }
        }
        
        logger.info("Circuit re-opened after failure in HALF-OPEN state");
    }
    
    /**
     * Scenario 5: Rapid State Fluctuations (Thrashing Prevention)
     */
    public void scenario5_ThrashingPrevention() throws InterruptedException {
        logger.info("\n=== SCENARIO 5: Preventing Circuit Thrashing ===");
        
        // Configure with longer wait duration to prevent rapid transitions
        CircuitBreakerConfig stableConfig = CircuitBreakerConfig.custom()
            .slidingWindowSize(20)
            .minimumNumberOfCalls(10)
            .failureRateThreshold(50)
            .waitDurationInOpenState(Duration.ofSeconds(60))  // Longer wait
            .permittedNumberOfCallsInHalfOpenState(5)
            .build();
        
        CircuitBreaker stableBreaker = CircuitBreaker.of("stable-service", stableConfig);
        
        stableBreaker.getEventPublisher()
            .onStateTransition(event -> {
                CircuitBreaker.State from = event.getStateTransition().getFromState();
                CircuitBreaker.State to = event.getStateTransition().getToState();
                
                logger.info("State transition: {} -> {}", from, to);
                
                // Track transition frequency
                trackTransitionFrequency(event);
            });
        
        // Simulate unstable service that fluctuates
        logger.info("Simulating unstable service with fluctuating success rate");
        
        for (int cycle = 1; cycle <= 5; cycle++) {
            logger.info("Cycle {}", cycle);
            
            // Alternate between success and failure patterns
            boolean useSuccessPattern = cycle % 2 == 0;
            
            for (int i = 1; i <= 15; i++) {
                try {
                    boolean success = useSuccessPattern ? (i % 3 != 0) : (i % 5 == 0);
                    simulateRequestWithBreaker(stableBreaker, i, success);
                } catch (Exception e) {
                    // Expected
                }
                
                Thread.sleep(500);
            }
            
            displayBreakerMetrics(stableBreaker);
            Thread.sleep(2000);
        }
    }
    
    private void simulateRequest(int requestId, boolean success) {
        Supplier<String> task = () -> {
            if (success) {
                logger.debug("Request {} - SUCCESS", requestId);
                return "Success";
            } else {
                throw new RuntimeException("Simulated failure");
            }
        };
        
        Supplier<String> decorated = CircuitBreaker.decorateSupplier(circuitBreaker, task);
        
        try {
            decorated.get();
        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw e;
        }
    }
    
    private void simulateRequestWithBreaker(CircuitBreaker breaker, int requestId, boolean success) {
        Supplier<String> task = () -> {
            if (success) {
                logger.debug("Request {} - SUCCESS", requestId);
                return "Success";
            } else {
                throw new RuntimeException("Simulated failure");
            }
        };
        
        Supplier<String> decorated = CircuitBreaker.decorateSupplier(breaker, task);
        
        try {
            decorated.get();
        } catch (Exception e) {
            // Expected
        }
    }
    
    private void displayMetrics() {
        CircuitBreaker.Metrics metrics = circuitBreaker.getMetrics();
        if (metrics != null && metrics.getNumberOfBufferedCalls() > 0) {
            logger.info("📊 Failure Rate: {}%, Slow Call Rate: {}%, Buffered: {}",
                metrics.getFailureRate(),
                metrics.getSlowCallRate(),
                metrics.getNumberOfBufferedCalls());
        }
    }
    
    private void displayBreakerMetrics(CircuitBreaker breaker) {
        CircuitBreaker.Metrics metrics = breaker.getMetrics();
        if (metrics != null) {
            logger.info("Breaker State: {}, Failure Rate: {}%, Success: {}, Failure: {}",
                breaker.getState(),
                metrics.getFailureRate(),
                metrics.getNumberOfSuccessfulCalls(),
                metrics.getNumberOfFailedCalls());
        }
    }
    
    private void logTransitionDetails(CircuitBreaker.State from, CircuitBreaker.State to) {
        logger.info("┌─────────────────────────────────────────┐");
        logger.info("│ STATE TRANSITION DETECTED              │");
        logger.info("├─────────────────────────────────────────┤");
        logger.info("│ From: {:20} │", from);
        logger.info("│ To:   {:20} │", to);
        logger.info("└─────────────────────────────────────────┘");
        
        switch (to) {
            case CLOSED:
                logger.info("✅ Circuit CLOSED - Normal operation restored");
                break;
            case OPEN:
                logger.info("❌ Circuit OPEN - Failing fast, service likely unhealthy");
                logger.info("   Will attempt recovery in {} seconds",
                    circuitBreaker.getCircuitBreakerConfig()
                        .getWaitDurationInOpenState().getSeconds());
                break;
            case HALF_OPEN:
                logger.info("🔍 Circuit HALF-OPEN - Testing service health");
                logger.info("   Allowed test calls: {}",
                    circuitBreaker.getCircuitBreakerConfig()
                        .getPermittedNumberOfCallsInHalfOpenState());
                break;
        }
    }
    
    private void trackTransitionFrequency(CircuitBreakerOnStateTransitionEvent event) {
        // Implementation to track and alert on frequent transitions
        // This helps prevent circuit thrashing
    }
}

5. Advanced Circuit Breaker with Custom Metrics and Alerting

java
@Service
@Slf4j
class ProductionCircuitBreakerManager {
    
    private final Map<String, CircuitBreaker> circuitBreakers = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<String, CircuitBreakerMetrics> metricsMap = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final ScheduledExecutorService alertScheduler = Executors.newScheduledThreadPool(1);
    
    @PostConstruct
    public void init() {
        alertScheduler.scheduleAtFixedRate(this::checkAndAlert, 10, 10, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
    }
    
    /**
     * Create circuit breaker for a service with custom configuration
     */
    public CircuitBreaker createCircuitBreaker(String serviceName, CircuitBreakerConfig config) {
        CircuitBreaker circuitBreaker = CircuitBreaker.of(serviceName, config);
        circuitBreakers.put(serviceName, circuitBreaker);
        metricsMap.put(serviceName, new CircuitBreakerMetrics(serviceName));
        
        setupMetricsCollection(serviceName, circuitBreaker);
        setupAlerting(serviceName, circuitBreaker);
        
        return circuitBreaker;
    }
    
    /**
     * Setup comprehensive metrics collection
     */
    private void setupMetricsCollection(String serviceName, CircuitBreaker breaker) {
        CircuitBreakerMetrics metrics = metricsMap.get(serviceName);
        
        breaker.getEventPublisher()
            .onSuccess(event -> {
                metrics.recordSuccess(event.getElapsedDuration().toMillis());
                updateGauges(serviceName, breaker);
            })
            .onError(event -> {
                metrics.recordFailure(event.getElapsedDuration().toMillis(), 
                    event.getThrowable());
                updateGauges(serviceName, breaker);
            })
            .onCallNotPermitted(event -> {
                metrics.recordRejection();
                updateGauges(serviceName, breaker);
            })
            .onStateTransition(event -> {
                metrics.recordStateTransition(event.getStateTransition());
                updateGauges(serviceName, breaker);
            });
    }
    
    /**
     * Setup alerting based on circuit breaker state
     */
    private void setupAlerting(String serviceName, CircuitBreaker breaker) {
        breaker.getEventPublisher()
            .onStateTransition(event -> {
                CircuitBreaker.State newState = event.getStateTransition().getToState();
                
                AlertSeverity severity;
                String message;
                
                switch (newState) {
                    case OPEN:
                        severity = AlertSeverity.CRITICAL;
                        message = String.format("Circuit opened for %s - Service unhealthy", 
                            serviceName);
                        sendAlert(severity, message, getOpenStateDetails(breaker));
                        break;
                    case HALF_OPEN:
                        severity = AlertSeverity.WARNING;
                        message = String.format("Circuit half-open for %s - Testing recovery", 
                            serviceName);
                        sendAlert(severity, message, getHalfOpenDetails(breaker));
                        break;
                    case CLOSED:
                        severity = AlertSeverity.INFO;
                        message = String.format("Circuit closed for %s - Service recovered", 
                            serviceName);
                        sendAlert(severity, message, getClosedStateDetails(breaker));
                        break;
                }
            })
            .onFailureRateExceeded(event -> {
                AlertSeverity severity = AlertSeverity.WARNING;
                String message = String.format(
                    "Failure rate threshold exceeded for %s: %.2f%%", 
                    serviceName, breaker.getMetrics().getFailureRate());
                sendAlert(severity, message, null);
            });
    }
    
    /**
     * Health check endpoint for circuit breaker status
     */
    public CircuitBreakerHealth getHealthStatus(String serviceName) {
        CircuitBreaker breaker = circuitBreakers.get(serviceName);
        if (breaker == null) {
            return CircuitBreakerHealth.unavailable(serviceName);
        }
        
        CircuitBreaker.Metrics metrics = breaker.getMetrics();
        CircuitBreakerMetrics customMetrics = metricsMap.get(serviceName);
        
        return CircuitBreakerHealth.builder()
            .serviceName(serviceName)
            .state(breaker.getState())
            .failureRate(metrics != null ? metrics.getFailureRate() : 0)
            .slowCallRate(metrics != null ? metrics.getSlowCallRate() : 0)
            .totalCalls(customMetrics != null ? customMetrics.getTotalCalls() : 0)
            .successfulCalls(customMetrics != null ? customMetrics.getSuccessfulCalls() : 0)
            .failedCalls(customMetrics != null ? customMetrics.getFailedCalls() : 0)
            .rejectedCalls(customMetrics != null ? customMetrics.getRejectedCalls() : 0)
            .averageResponseTime(customMetrics != null ? customMetrics.getAvgResponseTime() : 0)
            .timeInOpenState(customMetrics != null ? customMetrics.getTimeInOpenState() : 0)
            .build();
    }
    
    /**
     * Get all circuit breaker statuses
     */
    public Map<String, CircuitBreakerHealth> getAllHealthStatuses() {
        return circuitBreakers.keySet().stream()
            .collect(Collectors.toMap(
                service -> service,
                this::getHealthStatus
            ));
    }
    
    private void updateGauges(String serviceName, CircuitBreaker breaker) {
        // Update Prometheus metrics or other monitoring system
        CircuitBreaker.Metrics metrics = breaker.getMetrics();
        if (metrics != null) {
            // Record metrics to monitoring system
            recordMetric(serviceName, "circuit_breaker_state", 
                breaker.getState().ordinal());
            recordMetric(serviceName, "circuit_breaker_failure_rate", 
                metrics.getFailureRate());
            recordMetric(serviceName, "circuit_breaker_slow_call_rate", 
                metrics.getSlowCallRate());
        }
    }
    
    private void checkAndAlert() {
        for (Map.Entry<String, CircuitBreaker> entry : circuitBreakers.entrySet()) {
            CircuitBreaker breaker = entry.getValue();
            CircuitBreaker.Metrics metrics = breaker.getMetrics();
            
            if (metrics != null && metrics.getFailureRate() > 30) {
                // Send proactive alert before circuit opens
                Alert alert = Alert.builder()
                    .severity(AlertSeverity.WARNING)
                    .service(entry.getKey())
                    .message(String.format("High failure rate: %.2f%%", 
                        metrics.getFailureRate()))
                    .timestamp(Instant.now())
                    .build();
                
                sendProactiveAlert(alert);
            }
        }
    }
    
    private void recordMetric(String service, String metric, double value) {
        // Implementation for Prometheus/Grafana
    }
    
    private void sendAlert(AlertSeverity severity, String message, Object details) {
        logger.warn("ALERT [{}]: {} - Details: {}", severity, message, details);
        // Integration with PagerDuty, Slack, etc.
    }
    
    private void sendProactiveAlert(Alert alert) {
        logger.warn("Proactive Alert: {}", alert);
    }
    
    private Object getOpenStateDetails(CircuitBreaker breaker) {
        return Map.of(
            "waitDuration", breaker.getCircuitBreakerConfig()
                .getWaitDurationInOpenState().getSeconds(),
            "failureRate", breaker.getMetrics().getFailureRate(),
            "slowCallRate", breaker.getMetrics().getSlowCallRate()
        );
    }
    
    private Object getHalfOpenDetails(CircuitBreaker breaker) {
        return Map.of(
            "permittedCalls", breaker.getCircuitBreakerConfig()
                .getPermittedNumberOfCallsInHalfOpenState(),
            "currentState", breaker.getState()
        );
    }
    
    private Object getClosedStateDetails(CircuitBreaker breaker) {
        return Map.of(
            "failureRate", breaker.getMetrics().getFailureRate(),
            "successfulCalls", breaker.getMetrics().getNumberOfSuccessfulCalls()
        );
    }
}

/**
 * Custom metrics collection for circuit breaker
 */
@Data
class CircuitBreakerMetrics {
    private final String serviceName;
    private final AtomicLong totalCalls = new AtomicLong(0);
    private final AtomicLong successfulCalls = new AtomicLong(0);
    private final AtomicLong failedCalls = new AtomicLong(0);
    private final AtomicLong rejectedCalls = new AtomicLong(0);
    private final AtomicLong totalResponseTime = new AtomicLong(0);
    private final AtomicLong timeInOpenState = new AtomicLong(0);
    private final AtomicReference<CircuitBreaker.State> currentState = 
        new AtomicReference<>(CircuitBreaker.State.CLOSED);
    private final AtomicLong lastStateChange = new AtomicLong(System.currentTimeMillis());
    
    public void recordSuccess(long responseTimeMs) {
        totalCalls.incrementAndGet();
        successfulCalls.incrementAndGet();
        totalResponseTime.addAndGet(responseTimeMs);
    }
    
    public void recordFailure(long responseTimeMs, Throwable throwable) {
        totalCalls.incrementAndGet();
        failedCalls.incrementAndGet();
        totalResponseTime.addAndGet(responseTimeMs);
    }
    
    public void recordRejection() {
        rejectedCalls.incrementAndGet();
    }
    
    public void recordStateTransition(CircuitBreaker.StateTransition transition) {
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        CircuitBreaker.State oldState = transition.getFromState();
        CircuitBreaker.State newState = transition.getToState();
        
        if (oldState == CircuitBreaker.State.OPEN) {
            timeInOpenState.addAndGet(now - lastStateChange.get());
        }
        
        currentState.set(newState);
        lastStateChange.set(now);
    }
    
    public double getAvgResponseTime() {
        long total = successfulCalls.get() + failedCalls.get();
        return total > 0 ? totalResponseTime.get() / (double) total : 0;
    }
    
    public double getSuccessRate() {
        long total = successfulCalls.get() + failedCalls.get();
        return total > 0 ? (double) successfulCalls.get() / total * 100 : 100;
    }
}

/**
 * Circuit breaker health status DTO
 */
@Data
@Builder
class CircuitBreakerHealth {
    private String serviceName;
    private CircuitBreaker.State state;
    private double failureRate;
    private double slowCallRate;
    private long totalCalls;
    private long successfulCalls;
    private long failedCalls;
    private long rejectedCalls;
    private double averageResponseTime;
    private long timeInOpenState;
    private boolean isHealthy;
    
    public static CircuitBreakerHealth unavailable(String serviceName) {
        return CircuitBreakerHealth.builder()
            .serviceName(serviceName)
            .state(null)
            .isHealthy(false)
            .build();
    }
    
    public boolean isHealthy() {
        return state == CircuitBreaker.State.CLOSED && failureRate < 30;
    }
}

/**
 * Alert severity levels
 */
enum AlertSeverity {
    INFO,
    WARNING,
    CRITICAL,
    EMERGENCY
}

/**
 * Alert DTO
 */
@Data
@Builder
class Alert {
    private AlertSeverity severity;
    private String service;
    private String message;
    private Instant timestamp;
    private Map<String, Object> details;
}

6. Complete Testing Suite

java
@SpringBootTest
class CircuitBreakerCompleteTest {
    
    @Test
    void testAllStateTransitions() throws Exception {
        CircuitBreakerConfig config = CircuitBreakerConfig.custom()
            .slidingWindowSize(5)
            .minimumNumberOfCalls(3)
            .failureRateThreshold(50)
            .waitDurationInOpenState(Duration.ofSeconds(2))
            .permittedNumberOfCallsInHalfOpenState(2)
            .build();
        
        CircuitBreaker breaker = CircuitBreaker.of("test", config);
        AtomicInteger callCount = new AtomicInteger(0);
        
        // Test 1: CLOSED state - normal operation
        System.out.println("\n=== TEST 1: CLOSED State ===");
        for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
            try {
                executeTest(breaker, () -> {
                    callCount.incrementAndGet();
                    return "Success";
                });
                System.out.println("Request " + (i+1) + ": SUCCESS");
            } catch (Exception e) {
                System.out.println("Request " + (i+1) + ": FAILED");
            }
        }
        assert breaker.getState() == CircuitBreaker.State.CLOSED;
        assert callCount.get() == 5;
        
        // Test 2: Force OPEN by causing failures
        System.out.println("\n=== TEST 2: Transition to OPEN ===");
        callCount.set(0);
        for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
            try {
                executeTest(breaker, () -> {
                    callCount.incrementAndGet();
                    throw new RuntimeException("Service failure");
                });
            } catch (Exception e) {
                System.out.println("Request " + (i+1) + ": FAILED (expected)");
            }
        }
        
        // Wait for state to settle
        Thread.sleep(100);
        CircuitBreaker.State stateAfterFailures = breaker.getState();
        System.out.println("State after failures: " + stateAfterFailures);
        
        // Test 3: OPEN state - calls should be rejected
        System.out.println("\n=== TEST 3: OPEN State ===");
        callCount.set(0);
        for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
            try {
                executeTest(breaker, () -> {
                    callCount.incrementAndGet();
                    return "Should not reach";
                });
                System.out.println("Request " + (i+1) + ": Should not succeed");
            } catch (CallNotPermittedException e) {
                System.out.println("Request " + (i+1) + ": CORRECTLY REJECTED");
            }
        }
        assert callCount.get() == 0;
        
        // Test 4: Wait for transition to HALF-OPEN
        System.out.println("\n=== TEST 4: Waiting for HALF-OPEN ===");
        Thread.sleep(2500); // Wait for open state to expire
        
        CircuitBreaker.State stateAfterWait = breaker.getState();
        System.out.println("State after wait: " + stateAfterWait);
        
        // Test 5: HALF-OPEN state - test with successful calls
        System.out.println("\n=== TEST 5: HALF-OPEN State - Successful Recovery ===");
        callCount.set(0);
        int successfulCalls = 0;
        
        for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
            try {
                executeTest(breaker, () -> {
                    callCount.incrementAndGet();
                    return "Success";
                });
                successfulCalls++;
                System.out.println("Request " + (i+1) + ": SUCCESS");
            } catch (CallNotPermittedException e) {
                System.out.println("Request " + (i+1) + ": REJECTED");
            }
        }
        
        CircuitBreaker.State finalState = breaker.getState();
        System.out.println("\nFinal state: " + finalState);
        
        assert finalState == CircuitBreaker.State.CLOSED : "Circuit should be closed after successful half-open calls";
        assert successfulCalls >= 2 : "At least 2 successful calls should be allowed in half-open";
    }
    
    private <T> T executeTest(CircuitBreaker breaker, Supplier<T> supplier) {
        Supplier<T> decorated = CircuitBreaker.decorateSupplier(breaker, supplier);
        return decorated.get();
    }
    
    @Test
    void testConcurrentAccess() throws InterruptedException {
        CircuitBreakerConfig config = CircuitBreakerConfig.custom()
            .slidingWindowSize(10)
            .minimumNumberOfCalls(5)
            .failureRateThreshold(50)
            .build();
        
        CircuitBreaker breaker = CircuitBreaker.of("concurrent", config);
        ExecutorService executor = Executors.newFixedThreadPool(10);
        AtomicInteger successCount = new AtomicInteger(0);
        AtomicInteger failureCount = new AtomicInteger(0);
        
        List<CompletableFuture<Void>> futures = new ArrayList<>();
        
        for (int i = 0; i < 100; i++) {
            CompletableFuture<Void> future = CompletableFuture.runAsync(() -> {
                try {
                    Supplier<String> task = () -> {
                        if (Math.random() < 0.3) {
                            throw new RuntimeException("Random failure");
                        }
                        return "Success";
                    };
                    
                    Supplier<String> decorated = CircuitBreaker.decorateSupplier(breaker, task);
                    decorated.get();
                    successCount.incrementAndGet();
                } catch (CallNotPermittedException e) {
                    failureCount.incrementAndGet();
                } catch (Exception e) {
                    failureCount.incrementAndGet();
                }
            }, executor);
            
            futures.add(future);
        }
        
        CompletableFuture.allOf(futures.toArray(new CompletableFuture[0])).join();
        
        System.out.println("Concurrent Test Results:");
        System.out.println("Successes: " + successCount.get());
        System.out.println("Failures: " + failureCount.get());
        System.out.println("Final State: " + breaker.getState());
        
        CircuitBreaker.Metrics metrics = breaker.getMetrics();
        System.out.println("Failure Rate: " + (metrics != null ? metrics.getFailureRate() : 0));
        
        executor.shutdown();
        executor.awaitTermination(5, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
    }
}

Summary: Circuit Breaker States:

State	Behavior	When Entered	When Exited	Key Characteristics
CLOSED	Allows all requests, monitors failures	Initial state, after successful recovery	Failure threshold exceeded	• Normal operation
• Collects metrics
• Counts failures
• Tracks success rate
OPEN	Rejects all requests immediately	Failure rate exceeds threshold	Wait duration elapsed	• Fast failure
• Prevents cascading failures
• Gives service time to recover
• No calls to backend
HALF-OPEN	Allows limited test requests	After wait duration in OPEN	Success threshold reached (→CLOSED) or any failure (→OPEN)	• Limited test calls
• Probes for recovery
• Decides next state
• Prevents overloading recovering service
Key Configuration Parameters
yaml
resilience4j:
  circuitbreaker:
    instances:
      payment-service:
        # Sliding window configuration
        sliding-window-type: COUNT_BASED  # or TIME_BASED
        sliding-window-size: 10           # Number of calls to track
        minimum-number-of-calls: 5        # Minimum calls to evaluate
        
        # Failure thresholds
        failure-rate-threshold: 50        # Percentage of failures to trip
        slow-call-rate-threshold: 80      # Percentage of slow calls to trip
        slow-call-duration-threshold: 2s  # Duration considered "slow"
        
        # State transitions
        wait-duration-in-open-state: 30s  # Time to stay in OPEN state
        permitted-number-of-calls-in-half-open-state: 3
        automatic-transition-from-open-to-half-open-enabled: true
        
        # Exception handling
        record-exceptions:
          - org.springframework.web.client.HttpServerErrorException
          - java.util.concurrent.TimeoutException
        ignore-exceptions:
          - com.example.BusinessException
		  
This comprehensive guide covers all circuit breaker states with detailed definitions, high-level diagrams, and production-ready implementations using Resilience4j in Java. The examples demonstrate real-world scenarios and best practices for implementing circuit breakers in microservices architectures.
